It was in fact her love for Madame and Monsieur, and for Mademoiselle Rose, and Bibi’s strange dislike of them, which had brought the only clouds in the sunshine of their affection.

===Chinese===
的确，她对他没有半点疑心；可她对他的信任，也没有到把匣子、把那背柴老人的神秘声音讲给他听的地步；那些是她自己的秘密，更是牵涉弗朗特纳克一家的秘密——对比比，她可从来没见他对这一家有过多少好感，他甚至还劝过弗勒蕾特，不要同他们太过来往。实际上，正是她对夫人、先生和罗丝小姐的爱，同比比对他们的莫名嫌恶，才给这父女深情的阳光里添上仅有的几片阴云。

===Original===
But of this Fleurette was not thinking at the moment, her one thought was of her secret and how best to guard it. All the same she would not have denied Bibi chéri the kiss he asked for. She must take the risk, that was all, and once again trust to her wits. She allowed him to put his arms round her neck and held up her fresh young face for his kiss: she held the casket so carefully that he did not feel its sharp angles. All was well, for now she was free from his embrace, but still he had hold of her left hand, and drew her close to him.

===Chinese===
可弗勒蕾特此刻没有想到这些，她一心只想着她的秘密，想着怎样把它护得最稳妥。尽管如此，亲爱的比比讨这一吻，她是不肯不给的。冒一冒险就是了，再一次凭她的机智。她由着他把双臂搂上自己的脖颈，仰起鲜妍年轻的脸儿承受他的吻：她把匣子护得那么小心，他并没有触到它尖利的棱角。一切都好，此刻她已脱出他的怀抱，可他还握着她的左手，把她拉近身前。

===Original===
“Fleurette, my little one,” he said earnestly.

===Chinese===
「弗勒蕾特，我的小宝贝，」他恳切地说。

===Original===
“Yes, Bibi.”

===Chinese===
「嗯，比比。」

===Original===
“Do you know where the two Frontenac women have gone to?”

===Chinese===
「你可知道弗朗特纳克家那两个女人上哪儿去了？」

===Original===
“No, Bibi, I do not,” Fleurette was able to reply in all truthfulness, and looked her father straight in the eyes? “They were gone before I came.”

===Chinese===
「不知道，比比，我确实不知道，」弗勒蕾特全然问心无愧地答道，一面直直地望着父亲的眼睛。「我来之前她们就已经走了。」

===Original===
“It is for their good that I ask you.”

===Chinese===
「我这样问，是为了她们好。」

===Original===
“I am sure it is, Bibi chéri, but really, really I do not know.”

===Chinese===
「这我相信，亲爱的比比，可是真的、真的我不知道。」

===Original===
Bibi gave a quick, impatient sigh.

===Chinese===
比比急躁地、短促地叹了口气。

===Original===
“Ah, well! goodbye my Fleurette.”

===Chinese===
「唉，好吧！再见了，我的弗勒蕾特。」

===Original===
“Good night, Bibi.”

===Chinese===
「晚安，比比。」

===Original===
At last she was free. With her left hand she blew a last kiss to Bibi, and then quickly sped across the yard. Her heart felt heavy, and there was an uncomfortable lump in her throat. For the first time she had been brought face to face with the realities of life. Hitherto she had lived in a kind of fairyland in which she was the carefully tended and guarded queen, and Bibi the acknowledged king as well as slave. Everything in the world was perfect, and lovely and wonderful; the men and women in it﻿—not only Bibi, but Louise, and M. Duflos the butcher and M. Colombe the grocer, and﻿—and M. Amédé﻿—they were all kind and generous and gentle. But now cruelty and spite had come within her ken. An ugly ghoul called “hatred” had passed by hand in hand with his ugly brother “mistrust” and the latter had whispered something in her ear just now, which had caused her to shrink within herself when Bibi had kissed her, and to turn from him and to run away with a strange sense of relief.

===Chinese===
她终于自由了。她用左手朝比比飞了最后一个吻，随即快步穿过院子，跑远了。她心头沉重，喉咙里哽着一团发堵的东西。这是她生平头一回直面生活的真实。在此以前，她一直活在一座仙乡里，自己是被悉心照料、小心守护的王后，比比是公认的国王，兼公认的奴仆。世上的一切都完美、可爱、奇妙；世上的人们——不但是比比，还有路易丝，还有屠户迪弗洛先生、杂货铺老板科隆布先生，还有——还有阿梅代先生——个个都善良、慷慨、温柔。可如今，残忍和刻毒闯进了她的眼界。一个唤作「仇恨」的丑恶食尸鬼，手挽着他丑恶的兄弟「猜疑」打这儿走过；方才，后者在她耳边低声说了几句什么，害得比比吻她时她暗自一缩，转过身去，带着一种说不清的如释重负逃开了。

===Original===
She did not look back as she sped across the yard, and when she came to the small postern gate she was thankful to find it on the latch, so that she could slip out unseen.

===Chinese===
她一路飞跑穿过院子，没有回头；到了那道小边门跟前，她满心庆幸地发现门只搭着闩，正好悄悄溜出去，不叫人瞧见。

## IX / 第九章

===Original===
Fleurette was too young, too ignorant for self-analysis. She could not have told you what had made her act in the way she did, nor what had caused her so to mistrust Bibi as not to share her precious secret with him. All she knew was that she had had a wild desire to get away from him.

===Chinese===
弗勒蕾特年纪太轻、心思太单纯，还做不来省察自身。她说不出是什么促使她那样行事，也说不清是什么让她对比比起了疑心，竟不肯把自己那桩宝贵的秘密说给他听。她只知道，当时她心里有一股按捺不住的狂念，只想离他远远的。

===Original===
A cart track led from the postern gate across a couple of fields where it joined the main road; one or two isolated farm buildings belonging to M. de Frontenac, and the open fields on both sides, made the track fairly safe from footpads. The main road too which led through the village would be safer after dark, than the shortcut over the mountains. Fleurette hastened along, hugging her treasures, hoping that she would not fall in with the soldiers on their return from the château.

===Chinese===
一条车马道打后角门通出来，穿过两片田地，便接上了大路；德·弗朗特纳克先生名下一两座孤零零的农舍，加上两侧开阔的田野，使这条路遇上拦路的匪徒倒还算安全。再说，那条穿村而过的大路，天黑以后走，总比翻山的近路稳妥些。弗勒蕾特把她的宝贝紧紧搂在怀里，一路急赶，只盼别撞上从庄园折返的士兵。

===Original===
The weather had not fulfilled the promise made by the beauty of the sunset: heavy clouds hung over the sky; only one or two streaks of pale lemon-coloured light, like great gashes through the leaden clouds, still lingered in the West. Through the gloom farm-sheds and isolated trees loomed out like great immobile giants, and, on the right, the dense mass of the avenue of acacias and elder and the great gates of the château.

===Chinese===
天公并没有兑现落日的绚丽所许下的诺言：浓云低垂，压满了天空；只剩一两道淡柠檬色的光，像铅灰色云层上豁开的几道大口子，还滞留在西天。昏暝之中，田头的棚舍和孤零零的树，像一个个纹丝不动的巨人隐现；右边，是刺槐与接骨木夹道浓黑的一团，还有庄园的大门。

===Original===
Fleurette was already well on her way along the high road and in sight of the first house of the village, the cottage where Adèle lived with her aunt, the widow Tronchet, when she heard the all too familiar sound behind her of the heavy tramping of feet and of horses’ hoofs raising the dust of the road. The night was so still that the sounds reached her ears distinctly. She heard the lieutenant’s harsh voice giving a brief word of command: the creaking of the château gates, as they swung upon their hinges. Just then Roy, Monsieur’s dog, set up a dismal howl, and from one of the tall poplar-trees that bordered the road an owl gave a hoot and fluttered out into the night.

===Chinese===
弗勒蕾特已经沿大路走出去好一程，望得见村里的头一座房子了——就是阿黛尔同她姨妈特龙谢寡妇住的那间小屋；恰在此时，她听见身后传来那再熟悉不过的声响：沉重的脚步和马蹄，踏得路上的尘土扬起来。夜静极了，种种声响传进她的耳朵，声声清晰。她听见中尉粗厉的嗓音简短地喝出一声口令；听见庄园的大门在铰链上吱呀转动。正在这时，先生养的狗罗伊（Roy）发出一声凄惨的长嗥，路旁一株高高的白杨树上，一只猫头鹰叫了一声，扑棱棱地飞进夜色里去了。

===Original===
Fleurette broke out into a run. She knew that she could ask for shelter in the widow Tronchet’s cottage and wait there until the soldiers had gone by. Perhaps Adèle would walk home with her after that. Fortunately she could already perceive the light glimmering in one of the tiny windows, and just at the moment Adèle came out of the front door, probably to see for herself what the unusual sounds were about.

===Chinese===
弗勒蕾特撒腿跑了起来。她知道，可以到特龙谢寡妇的小屋里讨个躲避，等士兵们走过去再说。过后说不定阿黛尔还会陪她一同回家。幸好她已经望得见一扇小窗里闪着一点微光，而且恰在那个当口，阿黛尔从前门走了出来——多半是想亲自瞧瞧，这不寻常的动静究竟是怎么回事。

===Original===
She was mightily surprised to see Fleurette come running along.

===Chinese===
她看见弗勒蕾特一路奔来，好不吃惊。

===Original===
“They are the same soldiers, Adèle,” Fleurette explained breathlessly, as she followed her foster-sister into the cottage, “who were at Lou Mas this afternoon. Close the door, do, and I’ll tell you all about them.”

===Chinese===
「就是下午到庐庄去过的那伙士兵，阿黛尔，」弗勒蕾特跟着她的奶姐姐往屋里走，气喘吁吁地解释道，「快把门关上吧，我把他们的事原原本本讲给你听。」

===Original===
The widow Tronchet came out of her kitchen, and looked disapprovingly at Fleurette. She did not like the girl, and discouraged all intercourse between her and Adèle. She was a thrifty, hard-featured, hard-hearted peasant﻿—older than her sister Louise by a couple of years﻿—who had exacted every ounce of work and obedience from Adèle in payment for the shelter of her roof and for her daily bread. She had never forgiven her sister for leaving Adèle on her hands, though the girl had always worked her fingers to the bone, grudgingly no doubt, but diligently, in order to bring additional comfort into the cottage. But it was a poor, ill-furnished cottage, wherein food was none too plentiful, and beds hard, whereas Louise at Lou Mas lived in the lap of luxury; and envy had fostered dislike until it had almost become hatred.

===Chinese===
特龙谢寡妇从厨房里出来，不以为然地打量了弗勒蕾特一眼。她不喜欢这姑娘，一向不让阿黛尔同她来往。她是个又俭省、相貌又冷硬、心肠又硬的农妇——比她妹妹路易丝大着两岁——她要阿黛尔拿出十足十的活计和服从来，抵偿她给的一席容身之地和每日的口粮。妹妹把阿黛尔撂在她手里，这事她从来没有原谅过；虽说这丫头为了给小屋多添几许贴补，一向累断了手指地干活——不用说不情愿是有的，却勤勤恳恳、从不懈怠。可这终究是个穷苦、家什寒碜的小屋，吃的不宽裕，床铺也硬；而路易丝在庐庄却享尽奢华安逸；于是妒忌滋长了嫌恶，几乎养成了仇恨。

===Original===
She listened, with a frown on her hard wrinkled face, to Fleurette’s breathless tale of what had happened at the château. It would be the gossip of the village by tomorrow, that the soldiers of the Republic had arrested Monsieur, and that Madame and Mademoiselle had fled no one knew whither.

===Chinese===
她听弗勒蕾特气喘吁吁地讲庄园里的变故，那张冷硬多皱的脸上蹙起了眉头。到明天，共和国的士兵抓走了先生、夫人和小姐不知逃往了何处，这消息就要传遍全村，成为闲话了。

===Original===
“Oh, Ma’ame Tronchet,” Fleurette concluded, her fresh voice hoarse with sobs, “dear Ma’ame Tronchet, you don’t think they’re really going to harm Monsieur, do you?”

===Chinese===
「哦，特龙谢大娘，」弗勒蕾特末了说道，清脆的嗓音已经哭得沙哑，「好特龙谢大娘，您总不至于当真以为，他们会加害先生吧？」

===Original===
The widow Tronchet shrugged her shoulders and gave a short, harsh laugh.

===Chinese===
特龙谢寡妇耸了耸肩，发出一声短促刺耳的笑。

===Original===
“I’m not thinking about it at all one way or the other,” she said dryly. “What difference does it make to us poor people,” she went on, grumbling, while she busied herself about the room, “what happens to all those aristos? They never cared what happened to us.”

===Chinese===
「这档子事好也罢歹也罢，我压根儿想都不去想它，」她干巴巴地说。「那些贵族佬有个什么下场，」她一边在屋里忙这忙那，一边咕哝着接了下去，「同我们穷人有什么相干？我们的下场，他们可从来没放在心上。」

===Original===
For the moment Fleurette could do no more than stare at the widow Tronchet, in horror. Never had she heard anyone say anything so wicked. She was quite ready to defend Monsieur and Madame against any accusation of hard-heartedness, and would have done so at risk of offending the disagreeable, ill-natured old woman, but for the moment her attention, as well as that of Adèle’s, was riveted on the sounds outside. The soldiers had just come round the bend of the road; they were quite close to the cottage already, with the two horsemen walking their mounts in the van.

===Chinese===
一时间，弗勒蕾特只能满心惊骇地瞪着特龙谢寡妇。她生平从没听人说过这等狠毒的话。谁若指责先生和夫人心肠狠，她随时愿意挺身分辩，哪怕开罪这个讨厌刻毒的老太婆也在所不惜；可这会儿，她的注意力——连同阿黛尔的注意力——都被外头的动静牢牢摄住了。士兵们刚刚转过道路的拐弯处，离小屋已经十分近了；两名骑马人压着坐骑缓缓而行，走在队伍前头。

===Original===
“They are going on to Serres,” Fleurette whispered. In her heart she was wondering what Bibi was going to do. He was evidently not going to Orange, as he had said he would. Would he spend the night at Lou Mas after all? If he did, was there any danger of Fleurette’s secret leaking out? Of Bibi chéri finding out something about the casket and the precious wallet? Fleurette was still hugging the casket, she could see the widow Tronchet’s hard, steely eyes, gazing curiously at the bulge underneath her shawl, and then at the fullness in her kirtle where the wallet and the moneybag lay hidden in the pockets: Fleurette felt the blood rush up to her cheeks, and then had the mortification of seeing Adèle’s pinched-up little face break into a smile. Of what were those two women thinking? Surely not that she, Fleurette, had been stealing. Their faces were so inscrutable: the older woman’s hard and set, and Adèle’s ratlike and furtive, as if determined to conceal her thoughts.

===Chinese===
「他们是往塞尔去的。」弗勒蕾特悄声说。她心里正掂量着比比打算怎么办。他分明不像他自己说的那样要去奥朗日了。他到底还是要在庐庄过夜吗？若真过夜，弗勒蕾特的秘密有没有败露的危险？亲爱的比比（Bibi chéri）会不会探出那只匣子和那只贵重钱夹的些许端倪？弗勒蕾特还紧紧抱着那只匣子；她瞧得见特龙谢寡妇那双冷硬如钢的眼睛，好奇地盯住她披肩底下鼓起的一团，又移向她裙腰间鼓鼓囊囊的地方——钱夹和钱袋就掖在那儿的口袋里：弗勒蕾特只觉热血直往脸上涌，接着又羞又恼地看见，阿黛尔那张瘪缩的小脸上绽开了一丝笑。这两个女人在想些什么？总不至于以为她弗勒蕾特偷了东西吧。她们的脸色都那般高深莫测：老的那个又冷又板，阿黛尔却像老鼠一样鬼祟躲闪，仿佛拿定了主意，不肯让人看穿她的心思。

===Original===
The next moment they all heard the horsemen go by. Adèle ran to the door and peered out into the night. Over her shoulder she said to Fleurette:

===Chinese===
转眼间，她们都听见骑马人走了过去。阿黛尔跑到门口，朝夜色里张望。她回过头来，对弗勒蕾特说：

===Original===
“There’s your father riding with the soldiers. Shall I shout to him and tell him you are here?”

===Chinese===
「你父亲正跟着士兵们骑马走呢。要不要我喊他一声，告诉他你在这儿？」

===Original===
Instinctively Fleurette shook her head, and with that same inscrutable smile still on her face, Adèle deliberately closed the door again.

===Chinese===
弗勒蕾特本能地摇了摇头；阿黛尔脸上依旧挂着那副高深莫测的笑，不慌不忙地又把门关上了。

===Original===
“They’ve got Monsieur walking between them,” she commented dryly.

===Chinese===
「他们押着先生，叫他走在中间呢。」她干巴巴地评了一句。

===Original===
“It would have been better,” the widow said acidly to Fleurette, “for Citizen Armand to know that you are here. It won’t be safe for women to be alone on the high road this night, I am thinking.”

===Chinese===
「本来嘛，」寡妇尖酸地对弗勒蕾特说，「该让公民阿尔芒知道你在这儿才好。依我看，今儿夜里，女人家独自走大路，怕是不太平。」

===Original===
Then, as Fleurette remained silent, debating within herself what she had best do, the old woman went on curtly: “The sooner you get home now, my girl, the better. Adèle has got to put in an hour’s work at Citizen Colombe’s up at the village; it is miserable pay enough,” she continued muttering to herself, “and a shame that one girl should have to work so hard, whilst another lives a pampered life of luxury. But anyway,” she concluded abruptly, “I can’t be wasting any lamp-oil on you.”

===Chinese===
见弗勒蕾特默不作声，兀自在心里盘算何去何从，老太婆便又简慢地说了下去：「如今还是趁早回家的好，姑娘。阿黛尔还得去村那头的公民科隆布家做上一个钟头的活计；那点工钱已少得可怜，」她接着自顾自地咕哝，「一个丫头家偏偏要做死做活，另一个却娇养着过奢侈日子，真是没天理。不过横竖，」她没头没脑地收了尾，「我可不能白白为你费灯油。」

===Original===
“No﻿—no﻿—of course not, Ma’ame Tronchet,” Fleurette stammered. But the widow, still muttering under her breath, was paying no more attention to her. She had climbed on to a chair, and reaching up to the lamp that hung from the ceiling, she turned out the light. The room was now in darkness except for the light that came in through the open kitchen door. The widow with a curt: “Don’t be late, Adèle,” went off into the kitchen, and a moment or two later could be heard busy with her pots and pans.

===Chinese===
「不——不——当然不用，特龙谢大娘，」弗勒蕾特结结巴巴地说。可寡妇兀自压着嗓子咕哝，再也不理会她了。她爬上一把椅子，伸手够到吊在天花板上的那盏灯，把灯熄了。屋里顿时黑了下来，只剩敞开的厨房门里透进的一点亮光。寡妇撂下一句简短的「别回来晚了，阿黛尔」，便进了厨房；过不了一两分钟，就听得见她在那儿锅碗瓢盆地忙开了。

===Original===
Adèle had picked up her shawl, and equally unceremoniously gone as far as the door, when Fleurette called her shyly back.

===Chinese===
阿黛尔已拿起她的披肩，也同样不打一声招呼，径自走到门口；弗勒蕾特怯生生地把她唤了回来。

===Original===
“Adèle!”

===Chinese===
「阿黛尔！」

===Original===
The girl turned without speaking, her hand on the door which she was holding open.

===Chinese===
那姑娘一声不响地回过身来，一只手扶着门，把门敞着。

===Original===
“If you are going to M’sieur Colombe, could you﻿—”

===Chinese===
「你要是去科隆布先生家，能不能——」

===Original===
Fleurette stammered, “I mean, would you tell Monsieur Amédé, that﻿—that I am here, and perhaps﻿—”

===Chinese===
弗勒蕾特结结巴巴地说，「我是说，你能不能告诉阿梅代先生，说——说我在这儿，兴许——」

===Original===
“Why don’t you come along with me?” Adèle retorted dryly, “and tell him what you want.”

===Chinese===
「你干吗不跟我一道儿去？」阿黛尔干巴巴地顶了一句，「有什么话当面跟他说去。」

===Original===
Of course Fleurette could not tell her that she did not want Monsieur and Madame Colombe to know that she had something important to say to M’sieu’ Amédé. So all she said was: “Oh, Adèle, please!”

===Chinese===
弗勒蕾特自然没法对她说，她不愿让科隆布先生和科隆布大娘知道，她有要紧话要同阿梅代先生讲。于是她只说了一句：「哦，阿黛尔，求求你啦！」

===Original===
Adèle retorted with a shrug of the shoulders and an ugly little sneer:

===Chinese===
阿黛尔耸了耸肩，撇出一丝难看的讥笑，回嘴道：

===Original===
“You don’t want his papa and mama to know, I suppose.”

===Chinese===
「我猜，你是不想让他爸爸妈妈知道吧。」

===Original===
Fleurette whispered: “No!”

===Chinese===
弗勒蕾特悄声说：「不想！」

===Original===
“Very well!” was all that Adèle said in reply. “I’ll tell him.”

===Chinese===
「那好吧！」阿黛尔就答了这么一句。「我会告诉他的。」

===Original===
And in her usual, furtive, noiseless way she went out of the house, closing the door behind her.

===Chinese===
于是她照着惯常那副鬼祟、悄没声儿的做派，出了屋子，随手把门带上了。

## X / 第十章

===Original===
Fleurette remained in darkness, silent, motionless as a little mouse, listening for the well-known footstep which in a few minutes, she knew, would be at the door. It had perhaps been a rash thing thus to give herself away to Adèle, but the girl was uncommunicative and had never been known to gossip. Between two risks Fleurette had chosen the lesser one. If Bibi﻿—as she feared﻿—was going back to Lou Mas, there would be no chance whatever of keeping the secret of Madame’s casket and valuables from him, and what Bibi’s attitude would be towards them, Fleurette could not guess. It was the great Unknown. For Madame’s sake and Mademoiselle’s she would not risk it.

===Chinese===
弗勒蕾特留在黑暗里，不作一声，一动不动，像一只小老鼠，侧耳听着那熟悉的脚步声——她知道，不出几分钟，它就会到门口。就这样把自己泄露给阿黛尔，也许是一着莽撞棋；可这丫头嘴紧，从没听说她传过闲话。两害相权，弗勒蕾特取了其轻。倘若比比——正如她担心的那样——要回庐庄去，那么夫人的匣子和贵重之物，便再无半点瞒住他的可能；而比比对这两样东西会是什么态度，弗勒蕾特猜不出来。那全然是个未知数。为了夫人，也为了小姐，她不能冒这个险。

===Original===
Like an inspiration the thought of M’sieur Amédé had occurred to her; of Amédé who, when she was a little girl, and he a growing lad, would always take the blame on himself and know how to shield her when they had got into mischief together. She felt now, especially since this afternoon, that she could trust Amédé in a way that she had never trusted anyone else. Not even Bibi. Unfortunately Adèle had to be made a part confidant of the purpose: but after all what did Adèle know? She couldn’t know anything about the casket and Madame’s valuables: and if she did sneer, or even talk to her aunt about this message sent to M’sieu’ Amédé through her, well! Fleurette was prepared to face the gossip﻿—so long as her secret was safe.

===Chinese===
阿梅代先生的念头，像一道灵光闪现在她心里；那位阿梅代，在她还是个小丫头、他还是个半大小子的时候，两人一道闯了祸，总是他把过错揽到自己身上，总有法子护着她。如今她觉得——尤其打今天下午起——她信得过阿梅代，用一种她从未信过任何人的信法。连比比也没这样信过。只是这桩图谋，不得不拉阿黛尔做了半个知心人：可说到底，阿黛尔知道什么呢？匣子和夫人的贵重之物，她一概无从知晓；就算她撇嘴讥笑，甚至同她姨妈念叨这个托她捎给阿梅代先生的口信，那又怎样！闲话，弗勒蕾特豁得出去受——只要她的秘密安然无恙。

===Original===
She was counting the minutes﻿—the seconds﻿—Five minutes for Adèle to go to the Rue Haute: three and a half for Amédé to run along here﻿—she did not doubt but that he would run. Then there would be the intervening time whilst Adèle sought for an opportunity to speak to him alone. But oh! how Time dragged on leaden-footed! Nearly fifteen minutes must have gone by since Adèle went away. The widow Tronchet was still busy in the kitchen, rattling her pots and pans: but any moment she might finish and perhaps come in here and find Fleurette still waiting. Then there would be more acrimonious remarks, questions, arguments﻿—Had Fleurette known anything about nerves, she would have said that hers were irritated to snapping-point; but there was little talk of nerves in that year, 1794, and none in this remote corner of Dauphiné.

===Chinese===
她一分一秒地数着——阿黛尔去上街，要五分钟；阿梅代一路奔来，要三分半——她毫不怀疑，他准是一路奔来的。当中还得有一段空当，是阿黛尔寻机会同他单独说话的工夫。可是啊！时间拖着铅铸般的脚步，何等磨蹭！阿黛尔走后，怕是快有十五分钟了。特龙谢寡妇还在厨房里忙，锅碗瓢盆摔得叮当响：可她随时都可能忙完，说不定就会进这屋来，撞见弗勒蕾特还在这儿干等。到那时，又是刻薄话、盘问、争执，没个完——倘若弗勒蕾特懂得什么叫神经，她准会说，自己的神经已经烦躁到了就要绷断的地步；可在 1794 年那个年头，「神经」还很少有人说起，在多菲内这个偏僻的角落里，更是无人提及。

===Original===
Fleurette found it very difficult even to sit still. Would Amédé never come? All sorts of possibilities occurred to her, bringing her to the point of screaming with impatience. Perhaps he was from home, or working in the shop under his father’s eye. Perhaps the soldiers had called at the *épicerie* and taken him away, and Fleurette would never see him again﻿—Oh! if only time would stand still until Amédé came!

===Chinese===
弗勒蕾特觉得，连安安稳稳坐着都难了。阿梅代到底还来不来？种种可能在她脑子里一个接一个冒出来，急得她几乎要尖叫。也许他不在家，也许他正在铺子里、在他父亲眼皮底下做活。也许士兵们到杂货铺（*épicerie*）去了一趟，把他带走了，弗勒蕾特从此再也见不着他——啊！但愿时间停住不动，一直停到阿梅代来吧！

===Original===
Then at last, when she was on the point of bursting into tears with disappointment, she heard the quick, familiar step. Amédé!!! As noiselessly as possible she opened the door and slipped out. There sure enough was Amédé coming along. Though it was very dark now, Fleurette knew it was he, because of the sound of his footsteps. Hearing hers, he came to a halt, and she ran up to him, breathless with excitement. All at once the enormity of what she had done struck terror in her heart. She, Fleurette, whose reputation had stood hitherto above all gossip, who, for three years in succession had been crowned Queen of the month of May, an honour only accorded to girls of spotless character, she had actually given an assignation to a young man﻿—at night﻿—far from her home and his!

===Chinese===
终于，就在她失望得几乎要涌出泪来的当口，她听见了那急促而熟悉的脚步声。阿梅代！！！她放轻再放轻地开了门，溜了出去。可不是么，那边走过来的正是阿梅代。夜虽已黑透，弗勒蕾特却知道是他，凭的是那脚步声。他听见了她的脚步，便站住了；她奔到他跟前，激动得气都喘不上来。刹那间，她才醒悟到自己此举是何等惊世骇俗，心里猛地生出恐惧。她，弗勒蕾特，名声向来高出于一切闲话之上，连续三年加冕为五月王后（Queen of the month of May）——那项荣耀，只赐给品行无瑕的姑娘——竟真的同一个年轻男子私订了幽会——在夜里——远离她家、也远离他家！

===Original===
And with the horror of what she had done, came an intense shyness. What would M’sieur Amédé himself think of her? Indeed she had to evoke all her fondness for Madame and all her fears for Mademoiselle before she could summon enough courage to approach him, and to place a timid little hand upon his arm. She felt it trembling at her touch, and through the silence of the night came an answering timid sigh and a whisper:

===Chinese===
随着对所做之事的惊惧，又涌上来一阵强烈的羞怯。阿梅代先生本人会怎么想她呢？她实在得把对夫人的满心爱戴、对小姐的满腹忧惧统统唤起来，才鼓得起足够的勇气走近他，把一只怯生生的小手搭上他的手臂。她觉出那手臂在她的触碰下微微发颤；夜的静默里，传来一声回应的、同样怯生生的叹息，和一句悄语：

===Original===
“Mam’zelle Fleurette! What can I do in your service?”

===Chinese===
「弗勒蕾特小姐！有什么可以为您效劳的？」

===Original===
His timidity gave her courage. Gently she led him to the edge of the road where the tall poplar trees cast long, impenetrable shadows.

===Chinese===
他的怯懦给了她勇气。她轻轻把他引到路边，那里高高的白杨树投下长长的、深不见底的影子。

===Original===
“M’sieur Amédé,” Fleurette began, whispering low so that chance eavesdroppers might not hear: “I don’t know what you’ll think of me. I know I have done something which everyone in the village would call reprehensible. I sent for you in secret because﻿—because, M’sieur Amédé, there is no one in the world I could trust, as I do trust you.”

===Chinese===
「阿梅代先生，」弗勒蕾特开了口，声音压得低低的，惟恐叫偶然的偷听人听去：「我不知道你会怎么看我。我知道我做下的事，全村人人都要斥为不端。我悄悄打发人叫你来，是因为——因为，阿梅代先生，这世上再没有一个人，是我能像信你这样信得过的。」

===Original===
This time there came no sigh on the part of the young peasant, only a quick intaking of the breath, as if he had suddenly been dazzled by a wonderful light. His hard, rough hand crept up shyly and fastened over the soft, quivering one that lay upon his sleeve just like a frightened bird. But he was a man of few words, and therefore said nothing: and Fleurette, encouraged by the pressure of that rough hand, went on more glibly.

===Chinese===
这一回，年轻的庄稼人没有叹息，只急促地吸了一口气，仿佛蓦然间叫一道奇妙的光晃了眼。他那只粗硬的大手怯生生地探上来，扣住了搭在他衣袖上的那只柔软颤抖的小手——那只小手活像一只受惊的鸟。但他是个话少的人，于是什么也没说：弗勒蕾特得了那只粗手一握的鼓励，底下的话便说得流利了些。

===Original===
“It is about Monsieur, Madame and Mademoiselle,” she said, “up at the château. Soldiers have visited the place and they have broken the furniture and torn the beautiful carpets and the curtains: why, I know not. They have also called Monsieur, Madame and Mademoiselle traitors and aristos, and they have seized Monsieur and dragged him away from his home. By a miracle, M’sieur Amédé, a miracle wrought by the *bon Dieu* himself, Madame and Mademoiselle were able to escape out of the château before those awful soldiers came. I know that they are safe, but﻿—”

===Chinese===
「是为了先生、夫人和小姐的事，」她说，「就是庄园上那三位。士兵们到那宅子上搜过，砸了家具，撕破了漂亮的地毯和帘幔：为的什么，我不知道。他们还骂先生、夫人和小姐是叛徒、是贵族佬，把先生抓起来，从他家里拖走了。全亏一个奇迹，阿梅代先生，一个好天主（*bon Dieu*）亲自行出的奇迹，夫人和小姐赶在那伙可怕的士兵来到之前，逃出了庄园。我知道她们平安无事，只是——」

===Original===
“How do you know that, Mam’zelle Fleurette?” Amédé asked also in a whisper.

===Chinese===
「您怎么知道她们平安呢，弗勒蕾特小姐？」阿梅代也压着嗓子问。

===Original===
“Because, M’sieur Amédé,” she replied, “there is a mysterious personage working for the safety of Madame and Mademoiselle, under the direct guidance of the good God. I feel quite sure that Monsieur will also presently be saved through him.”

===Chinese===
「因为，阿梅代先生，」她答道，「有一位神秘人物，在好天主的亲自指引下，为夫人和小姐的平安出力奔走。我深信不疑，先生不久也会经由他获救。」

===Original===
“A mysterious personage, Mam’zelle Fleurette?”

===Chinese===
「一位神秘人物，弗勒蕾特小姐？」

===Original===
“Yes, a direct messenger from heaven. He has come down to earth in the guise of an old faggot-carrier. He looks old and decrepit and toil-worn, but when he speaks his voice is like that of an archangel, and if he looks at you his eyes give you the strength of giants and celestial joy.”

===Chinese===
「是的，一位直接从天上下凡的信使。他化作一个背柴老人的形貌，降临到尘世。他看上去老态龙钟、饱经劳苦，可他一开口，那声音就像天使长的声音；他若望你一眼，那双眼睛便给你巨人的气力和天上的喜乐。」

===Original===
“But, Mam’zelle Fleurette﻿—”

===Chinese===
「可是，弗勒蕾特小姐——」

===Original===
“His voice spoke to me this afternoon, M’sieu’ Amédé. All it said to me was that papers and valuables were behind the panel in Madame’s room. At that time I knew nothing about the soldiers. I had seen them but did not know that they were going to the château to arrest Monsieur and Madame and Mademoiselle Rose. Nevertheless when that voice spoke to me, I felt I must go over to the château as quickly as may be.”

===Chinese===
「今天下午，他的声音对我说话了，阿梅代先生。他对我说的话只有一句：文书和贵重之物，在夫人房里的护板后头。那时候，士兵们的事我还一无所知。我见过他们，却不知道他们是上庄园去抓先生、夫人和罗丝小姐的。然而那声音一对我开口，我就觉得，我必须尽快赶到庄园去。」

===Original===
“Why did you not send for me then, Mam’zelle Fleurette?”

===Chinese===
「那您当时怎么不打发人叫我去呢，弗勒蕾特小姐？」

===Original===
“I seemed to be in a hurry, impelled to run along as fast as I could. So I went by the mountain track. When I arrived at the château, the soldiers had been there some time. They had turned the place topsy-turvy, scared the servants and smashed and torn up everything, leaving nothing but the walls intact. It seemed as if a great tempest had swept by and wrecked everything. Monsieur was under arrest and Madame and Mademoiselle had gone. No one knew whither. Then suddenly I remembered that mysterious voice: I found my way to Madame’s room, and I found the panel, behind which Madame used to hide her household books and her money. I had often watched her doing this when I was a child. I tried to remember how to make the panel work and the good God helped me. And behind the panel I found Madame’s papers and her money, and a small box which, I am sure, has precious things in it, or it would not have been there.”

===Chinese===
「我当时只觉心里急迫，有一股力量催着我撒开腿快跑。于是我便走了山道。我到庄园的时候，士兵们已经在那里有些时候了。他们把那宅子上上下下翻了个底朝天，吓坏了仆人，见什么砸什么、撕什么，只剩下四面墙壁完好。就像刮过了一场大风暴，把一切都毁尽了。先生被看押起来，夫人和小姐已经走了。没人知道去了哪里。忽然间，我想起了那个神秘的声音：我一路摸到夫人的房里，找到了那块护板——夫人从前惯把家用账簿和银钱藏在那后头。我小时候常看她这么做。我拼命回想那护板是怎么办动的，好天主帮了我。护板后头，我找到了夫人的文书和她的银钱，还有一只小匣子——我敢肯定，里头装着贵重的东西，不然它不会放在那儿。」

===Original===
“Then what did you do, Mam’zelle Fleurette?” Amédé gasped under his breath, his none too sharp wits slowly taking in the details of the amazing adventure.

===Chinese===
「那后来您做了什么呢，弗勒蕾特小姐？」阿梅代屏息问道，他那不算灵光的脑瓜，正慢腾腾地领会着这桩惊人奇遇的种种细节。

===Original===
“I just took the wallet, M’sieu’ Amédé,” she replied simply, “and the moneybag, and the box. And here they are.”

===Chinese===
「我就拿了那只钱夹，阿梅代先生，」她简单地答道，「还有那只钱袋，还有匣子。都在这儿呢。」

===Original===
She tapped the pockets of her kirtle and made him feel the bulge underneath her shawl.

===Chinese===
她拍了拍裙子上的口袋，又让他摸了摸披肩底下鼓起的那一团。

===Original===
“Oh, *mon Dieu*!” he exclaimed fervently.

===Chinese===
「哦，*mon Dieu*（我的天主）！」他热切地失声道。

===Original===
And then she told him about Bibi, and how frightened she was lest when she returned to Lou Mas she should find him there. Bibi’s sympathies seemed to be all with the soldiers, she explained, and he would for certain make her give up Madame’s papers and valuables to the lieutenant.

===Chinese===
接着她把比比的事也对他说了，说她多么害怕，惟恐回到庐庄时会撞见他还在那儿。她解释说，比比的心思似乎全在士兵们那一边，他准会逼她把夫人的文书和贵重之物交出去，交给中尉。

===Original===
“That is why,” she concluded with a return to her first timidity. “I wished to speak with you, dear M’sieu’ Amédé.”

===Chinese===
「为的就是这个，」她收尾道，又回到了起初那份怯生生，「我才想同你说句话，亲爱的阿梅代先生。」

===Original===
“The Eternal Eve!” It was the first time Fleurette had used an endearing word when speaking to Amédé. Born and bred in this remote corner of Dauphiné, unsophisticated, untutored in the ways of coquetry and cajolery, she knew nevertheless, true daughter of the first mother that she was, that after this he would be mere wax in her hands.

===Chinese===
「永恒的夏娃啊！」这是弗勒蕾特头一回对阿梅代用出亲昵的字眼。她生在多菲内这个偏僻的角落，长在这个偏僻的角落，不谙世故，没学过卖弄风情、撒娇哄人的那一套手段；然而她毕竟是第一位母亲的亲女儿，天生就懂得：这一声过后，他就要成了她手里的一团软蜡。

===Original===
He was!

===Chinese===
他果真成了！

===Original===
All that he wanted to know was what he could do for her. Had she asked him to throw himself into the Buëche, he would have done it: but all that she wanted was for him to put her treasures in a safe place, until such time as Madame required them.

===Chinese===
他一心只想知道，自己能为她做点什么。哪怕她叫他一头栽进比埃什河（Buëche）里去，他也会照栽不误：可她要的，不过是请他把她的宝贝放到一个稳妥的地方，等夫人需要的时候再取。

===Original===
“If Bibi knew what I was doing, M’sieu’ Amédé,” she pleaded, “he would order me to give up Madame’s property. But I know that the *bon Dieu* meant me to take charge of it, or why,” she argued naively, “should He have sent His messenger to me?”

===Chinese===
「要是比比知道我在做的事，阿梅代先生，」她央告道，「他会命我把夫人的财物交出去的。可我知道，好天主的意思就是要我来掌管它；不然的话，」她天真地辩道，「他干吗把他的信使派到我这里来呢？」

===Original===
Of course Amédé was only too ready to share the burden of this wonderful secret with Fleurette.

===Chinese===
阿梅代自然求之不得，巴不得同弗勒蕾特分担这桩奇妙秘密的重负。

===Original===
It was wonderful to share anything with this loveliest being in all the world; and the thought that she trusted him more even than her father, was sending him well-nigh crazy with joy.

===Chinese===
能同这全世界最可爱的人儿共有任何一件事，本身就奇妙极了；而一想到她信任他竟胜过信任她的父亲，他简直欢喜得要发狂了。

===Original===
“I’ll tell you what I’ll do, Mam’zelle Fleurette,” he said: “There’s an old tool-shed at the back of our house where all sorts of rubbish are kept. It is an absolute litter now, and the back of it has not been cleared or interfered with for years. But I know of a convenient hole in the flooring, hidden well away in a corner. I’ll put these things there. They’ll be quite safe﻿—Mam’zelle Fleurette, you’ll know where to find them after I’ve gone away, if you want them.”

===Chinese===
「我告诉您我要怎么办，弗勒蕾特小姐，」他说：「我们家屋后有一间旧工具棚，堆着各式各样的破烂家什。如今那里面简直是狼藉一片，靠里那一半，多少年没人清理、没人动过了。可我知道地板上有个合用的窟窿，藏在一个犄角旮旯里，遮掩得严严实实。我把这些东西搁到那儿去。保管太平无事——弗勒蕾特小姐，等我走了以后，您要是用得着它们，就知道上哪儿去找。」

===Original===
“After you’ve gone away?”

===Chinese===
「等你走了以后？」

===Original===
For the moment she had forgotten. Of course he was going! How could she forget? He was going to join the army﻿—to fight the English﻿—! Perhaps he was never coming back﻿—oh! How could she﻿—how could she forget?

===Chinese===
一时间她把这忘了。他当然是要走的！她怎么会忘呢？他要去从军——去打英国人——！也许他永远回不来了——啊！她怎么会——她怎么会忘呢？

===Original===
Amédé after the long speech which he had delivered in a whisper﻿—his longest speech on record﻿—had remained silent. The tone of anguish in Fleurette’s voice, just now when he recalled the fact that he was going away, had given him an immense thrill of joy. Altogether poor Amédé felt so happy that he was almost ashamed. The night was so beautifully still; the wind had gone down, and slowly the great clouds that had obscured the sky since sunset were rolling away over the valley. Already overhead a patch of translucent indigo appeared, ever-widening, and revealing one by one the scintillating worlds that are beyond man’s ken. Amédé did not want to speak; he wanted it less than he had ever done before. He just wanted to stand there beside this exquisite creature, wrapped in the silence of the night, feeling her nearness, hearing the gentle murmur of her breath come and go through her perfect mouth. She had extracted the casket from under her shawl and given it to him to hold, and she also gave him the wallet and the moneybag; and as she did this, her little hand, so soft and so warm, came in contact with his now and then﻿—quite often﻿—and poor Amédé was on the point of swooning with delight.

===Chinese===
阿梅代悄声说完了那一大篇话——那是他有生以来最长的一篇话——之后就一直没有作声。方才他提起自己要走这件事，弗勒蕾特话音里那一缕哀痛，给了他一阵巨大的喜悦震颤。总而言之，可怜的阿梅代只觉得太幸福了，幸福得几乎羞愧起来。夜是那样美好的静谧；风已经息了，日落以来遮蔽天空的大团阴云，正缓缓滚过山谷那边散开去。头顶上已经现出一角透明的靛蓝，越展越宽，把人力所不及的、晶晶闪耀的一个个世界逐一显露出来。阿梅代不想说话；他比以往任何时候都更不想说话。他只想就那样站在这位妙人儿身边，裹在夜的静默里，觉着她在近旁，听她那轻柔的呼吸，在她那完美的唇间来来去去。她已从披肩底下取出那只匣子，交给他拿着；又把那只钱夹和那只钱袋也给了他；她递东西的时候，那只小手那么软、那么暖，时不时碰着他的手——碰得还挺勤——可怜的阿梅代眼看就要欢喜得晕过去了。

===Original===
“I do trust you, M’sieu’ Amédé,” she whispered in the end: “and you’ll do this for Madame’s sake, will you not? and also Mademoiselle’s. And also,” she added softly, “for mine.”

===Chinese===
「我真的信得过你，阿梅代先生，」她末了悄声说：「你会为了夫人做这件事的，是不是？也为了小姐。还有，」她轻轻地添上一句，「也为了我。」

===Original===
“Oh! Mam’zelle Fleurette,” Amédé sighed. What he had wished to say was: “I would die for you, beloved of my heart: at a word from you I would lay down my life, or barter my soul.” But Amédé had no command of words, and was now cursing himself for being a clumsy fool. He stowed away the wallet and the bag into the pockets of his breeches, and tucked the casket underneath his blouse.

===Chinese===
「哦！弗勒蕾特小姐，」阿梅代叹道。他本想说的是：「我甘愿为您去死，我心头的至爱：只要您一句话，我便舍出这条性命，或是拿灵魂去换。」可阿梅代驾驭不了言辞，这会儿正骂自己是个笨嘴拙舌的傻瓜。他把钱夹和钱袋塞进马裤的口袋，把匣子掖在了罩衫底下。

===Original===
“And now I must go home, dear Monsieur Amédé,” Fleurette said. “As it is, I am afraid Bibi will be anxious.”

===Chinese===
「现在我得回家了，亲爱的阿梅代先生，」弗勒蕾特说。「照这么耽搁下去，只怕比比要着急了。」

===Original===
Her hand was on his arm: and with a sudden impulse he stooped and pressed his lips against that exquisite little hand. Fortunately they were still standing in the shadows cast by the poplar-trees, or Amédé must have seen the blush that rose to Fleurette’s cheeks when she felt the delicious thrill of that timid kiss. A soft breeze stirred the branches above their heads, and through the quivering leaves there came a sigh that was like an echo of their own. And above the crests of Pelvoux the waning moon suddenly rent the last clouds that veiled her mystery, and flooded the snowy immensities with a shower of gold. Slowly the shades of night yielded to the magic and the high road glistened like a silvery ribbon winding, snakelike, toward Laragne.

===Chinese===
她的手正搭在他的手臂上：他心念一动，蓦地俯下身去，把嘴唇印上了那只精巧的小手。幸而两人还站在白杨树投下的影子里，不然阿梅代准会看见弗勒蕾特涌上双颊的红晕——那怯生生的一吻印上来，她感到一阵美妙的震颤。一阵软风撩动了他们头顶的枝柯，颤动的叶隙间传来一声叹息，像是他们自己那声叹息的回响。而在佩尔武（Pelvoux）的雪峰之上，残月忽然撕开了掩住她神秘的最后几缕云，把一阵金雨泼洒在茫茫雪野之上。夜色缓缓向这魔法让了位，大路熠熠闪亮，像一条银色的缎带，蜿蜒如蛇，通向拉拉涅。

===Original===
Fleurette gave a sudden start of alarm.

===Chinese===
弗勒蕾特惊惶地猛然一颤。

===Original===
“What is it, Mam’zelle Fleurette,” Amédé asked.

===Chinese===
「怎么了，弗勒蕾特小姐？」阿梅代问。

===Original===
“Someone,” she said. “I saw someone move there﻿—furtively﻿—among the shadows.”

===Chinese===
「有人，」她说。「我看见有个人影在那儿动——鬼鬼祟祟的——就在那些暗影里。」

===Original===
He turned to look. A small figure wrapped in a shawl had just gone past on the other side of the road.

===Chinese===
他转身去看。一个小小的身影裹着披肩，刚刚从大路对面走了过去。

===Original===
“It is only Adèle,” he said carelessly. “She is going home.”

===Chinese===
「那不过是阿黛尔，」他漫不经心地说。「她回家去呢。」

===Original===
Not altogether reassured, Fleurette peered into the shadows. She did not think that it was Adèle whom she had seen, or if it was Adèle, there was someone else lurking in the shadows, she felt sure; and though she was not altogether frightened, she felt herself trembling, and her knees giving way under her. No doubt it was in order to save herself from falling that she had leaned more heavily against Amédé’s arm. Certain it is that he put that arm round her, only in order to support her; but the contact of that warm, quivering young body against his breast sent the last shred of his self-control flying away on the evening breeze.

===Chinese===
弗勒蕾特并未全然放下心来，又朝暗影里细细张望。她觉得，她看见的并不是阿黛尔；就算真是阿黛尔，暗影里必定还另有一个人潜伏着——这一点她拿得准；她虽说并不怎么害怕，却觉出自己在发抖，膝弯里发软，眼看要撑不住。毫无疑问，她之所以把身子更重地倚在阿梅代的手臂上，只是为了免得跌倒。千真万确，他伸出那条手臂搂住她，也只是为了搀扶她；可那温热发颤的年轻身子一贴上他的胸膛，便把他最后一丝自制，随着晚风送得无影无踪了。

===Original===
The high road was bathed in honey-coloured light, but these two were standing in the deep shadows cast by the poplar-trees; and the darkness wrapped them round as in a velvety downy blanket. His arm tightened round her shoulders, pressed her closer and closer to his breast, held her there so closely that she could scarcely breathe.

===Chinese===
大路浸在蜜色的光辉里，可这两个人站在白杨树投下的浓影之中；黑暗把他们周身裹住，像裹在一条天鹅绒的绒毯里。他的手臂收紧，搂住她的双肩，把她越搂越紧地贴向自己的胸膛，搂得那样紧，紧得她几乎透不过气来。

===Original===
It was only in order to get her breath that she raised her face to his; far be it from me to suggest that it was for any other motive; but this proved the final undoing of poor M’sieu’ Amédé; for the next moment his lips were fastened hungrily on hers, and her sweet young soul went out to him, in a first, a most delicious kiss.

===Chinese===
她抬起脸来望他，只是为了好换一口气；我决无意暗示，她抬脸另有别的缘故；可这一抬脸，便断送了可怜的阿梅代先生的最后一点定力；因为下一瞬，他的嘴唇已经如饥似渴地贴上了她的唇，她那甜美年轻的灵魂便向他迎去，在一个初次的、最甘美的吻里。

## XI / 第十一章

===Original===
It all seemed like a lovely dream after that: this walking together arm in arm down the high road with the waning moon throwing great patches of silvery light to guide them on their way.

===Chinese===
打那以后的一切，恍如一场甜美的梦：就这样臂挽着臂，一同走在大路上，一弯残月洒下一大片一大片银白的光，为他们照着前路。

===Original===
They went through the village, not caring whom they met. They belonged to each other now; that wonderful kiss was a bond between them that only death could sever. That was how they felt; supremely, marvellously happy, thrilled with this new delight, this undreamed joy: and with it all a cloud of measureless sorrow at the impending farewell. The magic words had been spoken: “You love me, Fleurette?” The eternal question to which the only answer is a sigh. No, they did not care whom they met. They could laugh at gossip now: from this night they were tokened to one another, and only M. le Curé’s blessing could make their happiness more complete.

===Chinese===
他们穿过村子，撞上谁也满不在乎。如今他们已彼此相属；那美妙的一吻成了连结两人的纽带，唯有死亡才能将它割断。他们的心境正是如此：幸福到了极处，美妙到了极处，为这崭新的欢悦、这梦想不到的喜事而心头发颤；而在这一切之中，又笼着一团无边的哀伤，为着那眼看便要来到的离别。那句有魔力的话已经说出了口：「你爱我吗，弗勒蕾特？」这是亘古的一问，唯一的回答只有一声叹息。是啊，撞上谁他们都满不在乎。如今他们尽可以笑对旁人的闲话了：打这一夜起，他们已互订终身，只消再得本堂神父的一句祝福，他们的幸福便更臻圆满。

===Original===
As a matter of fact they met no one, for they avoided the main street of the village and made their way to Lou Mas along narrow bypaths that meandered through orchards of almond-trees heavy with blossom. For the most part they were silent. Fleurette’s little hand rested on Amédé’s arm. Now and then he gave that hand a quick, excited squeeze and this relieved his feelings for the time being. Under his other arm he hugged the casket, the precious treasure that had been the mute but main spring of his happiness. It represented Fleurette’s trust in him; that priceless guerdon he would not have bartered for a kingdom.

===Chinese===
其实他们一个人也没撞上，因为他们避开了村子的大街，拣着窄窄的小径穿行，那些小径蜿蜒在繁花压满枝头的杏树园间，一路通到庐庄。一路上大半工夫他们都默然无语。弗勒蕾特的一只小手搭在阿梅代的臂弯里。他不时把那只手飞快地、激动地一握，权且舒泄一下心头的情感。另一只胳膊底下，他紧紧夹着那只匣子——正是这桩无声的宝贝，做了他幸福的主发条。它代表着弗勒蕾特对他的信托；这份无价的酬赏，拿一个王国来换他也不干。

===Original===
“You will not part with Madame’s valuables, will you, Amédé?” she had enjoined him most solemnly. “Not to anyone?”

===Chinese===
「夫人的这些贵重物件，你决不会交到旁人手里吧，阿梅代？」她曾万分郑重地叮嘱他。「给谁都不行？」

===Original===
“Never, Fleurette,” he had replied solemnly. “On my soul!”

===Chinese===
「决不，弗勒蕾特，」他曾郑重其事地答道。「我以灵魂担保！」

===Original===
When they were within sight of Lou Mas, they decided that it would be best for him to turn back. She, Fleurette, was quite safe now, and of course old Louise would be waiting for her﻿—and perhaps Bibi. She was not going to make a secret of her walk home with Amédé. Indeed she wished it proclaimed from the housetops that they were tokened to one another, and that they would be married as soon as this horrible war was over. There was to be no secret about it, and Fleurette knew well enough that neither Bibi nor M’sieu’ Colombe would object; but because of Madame’s valuables, she did not want Amédé to come to Lou Mas until tomorrow. And so that first wonderful kiss found its successor in another﻿—one that was perhaps even more delicious, because it was more poignant﻿—the precursor of the last farewell.

===Chinese===
望得见庐庄的时候，两人商定，他还是就此折回去的好。她弗勒蕾特如今已经十分安全，老路易丝必定在家候着她——兴许比比也在。她不打算把同阿梅代一路走回家这件事瞒着人。恰恰相反，她巴不得把它嚷嚷到屋顶上去：他们已经互订终身，只等这场可怖的战争一了结便成婚。这事用不着瞒谁，弗勒蕾特心里透亮得很，比比也好、科隆布先生也好，都不会有二话；只是因为夫人的那些贵重物件，她不愿阿梅代在明天之前到庐庄来。于是，那第一记美妙的吻有了它的后继者——另一记吻，兴许还要更加甘美，因为它更加揪心——那正是最后诀别的前奏。

---

===Original===
Fleurette found Louise anxiously waiting for her. Bibi had not returned and the old woman knew nothing, of course, of the tragic events that had occurred at the château. Fleurette told her what had happened, and while she was speaking Bibi came in. He looked tired and anxious, but Fleurette thought it prudent not to appear to notice anything unusual about him. He made no reference to the events at Frontenac, and when nine o’clock came he kissed Fleurette as tenderly, as unconcernedly as usual. Nine o’clock! What a lifetime, as far as Fleurette was concerned had been crowded into this past hour!

===Chinese===
弗勒蕾特到家时，见路易丝正焦急地等着她。比比还没有回来，庄园里发生的那些惨变，老太太自然一无所知。弗勒蕾特把事情原原本本讲给她听，正讲着，比比进来了。他看去又疲惫又焦灼，可弗勒蕾特觉得，还是装作不曾看出他有什么异样才稳妥。他对弗朗特纳克家的事只字不提；到了九点钟，他照常温存地、若无其事地吻了吻弗勒蕾特。九点钟！在弗勒蕾特想来，方才过去的那一个钟头里，塞进了多么漫长的一生啊！

===Original===
She went to bed as in a dream, partly made up of sorrow and partly of great joy: even the excitement of her adventure at the château was lost in the immensity of that joy. Fleurette fell asleep with her cheek against the hand on which Amédé had planted that first timid kiss.

===Chinese===
她上床去睡，恍恍惚惚如在梦里，这梦一半是悲，一半是大喜：连庄园里那场历险的激动，也淹没在这欢喜的浩瀚里了。弗勒蕾特入睡时，脸颊贴着那只手——阿梅代把第一记羞怯的吻印在上面的那只手。

===Original===
When she came down in the early morning Bibi had already gone.

===Chinese===
第二天清早她下楼来时，比比已经走了。

## XII / 第十二章

===Original===
The soldiers of the Republic together with their officer had spent half the night at Laragne in the tavern kept by the Père Gramme, drinking and jesting with the drabs of the village. Each man had a tale to tell of his own prowess at the château, and how but for him, the ci-devant Frontenac would have slipped through the fingers of justice as readily as the two women had done.

===Chinese===
共和国的士兵们连同他们的军官，头天夜里在拉拉涅格拉姆老爹（Père Gramme）开的酒馆里消磨了半个夜晚，同村里的荡妇们喝酒调笑。人人都有一段自己的英勇事迹可吹，讲他在庄园里如何大显身手，又说若不是有他出力，旧贵族弗朗特纳克岂不早像那两个女人一样，从法网的指缝里轻易溜走了。

===Original===
They were very proud of their prisoner, who sat lonely and silent in a corner of the low-raftered room, foul with the odour of sour wine and perspiring humanity. Monsieur de Frontenac﻿—the ci-devant as he was curtly termed﻿—was apparently taking his misfortune calmly; neither threats nor vain promises caused him to depart from his attitude of quiet philosophy. The soldiers had, of course, made up their minds that he knew well enough where his wife and daughter were in hiding, but they had also realized by now that it was not in their power to force him to divulge what he knew.

===Chinese===
他们很为自己的俘虏得意。那人孤零零、闷声不响地坐在低梁屋子的一角，满屋酸酒气和人汗臭熏人。德·弗朗特纳克先生——人们径直简称他「旧贵族」——遭此横逆，看样子倒处之泰然；威吓也好，空头许愿也好，都不能叫他改变那副恬静的哲人态度。士兵们自然拿定了主意，认定他分明知道妻女藏身何处；可他们如今也明白了，凭他们的能耐，休想逼他吐露半分。

===Original===
The lieutenant﻿—a man who had begun life as a notary’s clerk, and therefore had some education﻿—was content to shrug his shoulders and to declare that the citizens of the nearest Committee of Public Safety, had plenty of means at their disposal for making an obdurate prisoner speak. He recalled that at the trial of the Widow Capet she had been forced into admissions which, before that, she would sooner have died than make. Mocking glances, jeers and insults were thereupon cast on the prisoner who remained as unconcerned, as serene as before.

===Chinese===
中尉——此人早年出身公证人手下的书记，好歹受过些教育——只满足于耸耸肩膀，声言离此地最近的公安委员会的公民们，有的是手段，叫嘴硬的犯人开口。他还提起，当年审判卡佩寡妇，就逼得她招出了先前宁死也不肯招的话。于是讥诮的眼光、嘲骂和侮辱一齐泼向俘虏，他却依旧毫不在意，安详如故。

===Original===
The lieutenant had commandeered billets for his men in the better houses of the village, and just before midnight the party broke up. The prisoner was then conducted to the small, local *poste de gendarmerie* and there incarcerated in the cell usually occupied by vagabonds and cattle-thieves. Two or three of the soldiers remained at the *poste* to reinforce the local gendarmes, in case some hotheads in the village meditated a coup to wrest the traitor Frontenac from the clutches of justice. The lieutenant himself had selected the house of Citizen Colombe the grocer of the Rue Haute for his night-quarters? To say that the worthy *épicier* did not accord this representative of his country’s army a warm welcome, would be to put it mildly. He was furious, and showed it as plainly as he dared; but there is in every French peasant a sound vein of common sense, and he knows﻿—none better﻿—when submission to the ruling powers is not only the best policy, but at the same time the most conducive to the preservation of his own dignity.

===Chinese===
中尉给部下在村里较好的人家强派了宿营，将近午夜，这一伙人就散了。俘虏随即被押到本村小小的宪兵队（*poste de gendarmerie*），关进那间平日关押流浪汉和偷牛贼的牢房。两三个士兵留守宪兵队，给本地宪兵添几分兵力，以防村里有什么性子火爆的家伙图谋动手，把叛国犯弗朗特纳克从法网的爪子里抢走。中尉本人则挑中了上街杂货商公民科隆布的家过夜。说这位体面的杂货商（*épicier*）没有热络地款待祖国军队的这位代表，还算是说得客气了呢。他一肚子火，也尽着胆子把火气摆在了脸上；不过每个法国农民的骨子里都有一股健全的常识，他最清楚不过——什么时候向当权的势力低头，不但是上策，同时也最能保全自己的体面。

===Original===
Ma’ame Colombe﻿—or rather the citizeness﻿—made the lieutenant comfortable and that was all; but at the bottom of her heart she felt that she must do unto him as she would wish her own son to be done by presently, when he too was a soldier in that army which she detested. She fell asleep thinking of Amédé tramping the high road as these men had done, stockingless, hatless, with unwashed shirt and a dirty worsted cap on his head; and she dreamed all night of him, deprived even of his weekly bath in the big tub, over in the washhouse. That is what she objected to mostly in these men: the dirt. It was wonderful, of course, their fighting for their country, now that all the other countries in the world were attacking France, but Ma’ame Colombe argued to herself that patriotism might just as well be allied to cleanliness. Even the lieutenant, who was after all an officer, and should be setting a good example to his men, would have looked much more imposing if he had washed his face and taken the dust of the road out of his hair.

===Chinese===
科隆布大娘——如今该唤女公民了——把中尉安顿得舒舒服服，也仅止于此；可在心底里，她觉得总得按她盼望自己儿子日后受到的待遇来待这个人——早晚阿梅代也要进这支叫她厌恶透顶的军队当兵的。她想着阿梅代会像这伙人一样，不打袜子，不戴帽子，衬衫不换洗，头上扣一顶脏兮兮的绒线帽，一路跋涉大路，就这么睡着了；一整夜都梦见他，连每礼拜在洗衣房大木盆里洗的那回澡也洗不上了。这伙人身上最叫她看不惯的就是：脏。当然啦，如今世界各国都在攻打法国，他们为祖国打仗，自然是了不起的；可科隆布大娘心里暗自盘算，爱国心尽可以同干净清爽搭伙嘛。就连那位中尉——好歹是个军官，本该给部下做个好样子——若是肯洗把脸、把头发里的路尘掸掸干净，看上去也要威风得多呢。

===Original===
Great, therefore, was Ma’ame Colombe’s astonishment the next morning when she, along with several of her friends, being at the market, saw another detachment of soldiers marching into Laragne from the direction of Sisteron. Only eight of them there were, with one officer and a wagon drawn by two splendid horses; but *nom du ciel*! what a different set of men and horses these were. The men clean as new pins, magnificently dressed in blue coats with white facings and belts, white breeches﻿—all spotless﻿—and black gaiters that reached midway up their thighs. Beneath their elegant chapeau-bras, each adorned with a silk tricolour cockade, they wore their own hair, down to their shoulders, unfettered by the old, ridiculous queue, and each man had successfully cultivated a fierce and magnificent moustache. Everything about them glistened with cleanliness, their boots, their buckles, their muskets; as for the officer, never in all their lives had the good ladies of Laragne seen anyone so magnificent: tall, blond, with a moustache that he could easily have tucked behind his ears, and a little tuft of blond beard at the tip of his chin, he walked with drawn sword at the head of his squad, a superb tricolour sash further enhancing the glory of his attire.

===Chinese===
因此，第二天早晨，科隆布大娘正同几位女伴在集市上，忽见又一队士兵从锡斯特龙方向开进拉拉涅来，她那份惊讶可想而知。拢共只有八个人，一名军官，外加一辆两匹骏马拉的大车；可是，老天爷（*nom du ciel*）！这批人马可就大不一样了。士兵们干净得簇簇新，穿一身漂亮的蓝色军服，白领襟、白肩带，白裤子——一尘不染——黑色绑腿直裹到大腿半截。他们头上是雅致的折式礼帽（chapeau-bras），每顶缀一枚绸三色帽徽；帽下蓄着本人的头发，披到肩头，不受旧日那条可笑发辫的拘束，人人还养成了两撇威风凛凛的漂亮胡子。浑身上下，靴子、带扣、火枪，样样干净得锃亮；至于那位军官，拉拉涅的好太太们活了一辈子也没见过这般气派的人物：高个子，金头发，两撇胡子长得几乎能掖到耳朵后头去，下巴尖上留一小撮金色的须，他按着出鞘的剑走在队伍前头，一条华美的三色绶带更给那身装束添了威仪。

===Original===
Potatoes and eggs and butter were forgotten, while market women and customers stood gaping, open mouthed. Never had such beautiful specimens of manhood been seen in Laragne. By the time they reached the Rue Haute all the village had turned out to have a look at them, and heads appeared at every cottage window. The village urchins followed the little squad, intoning the “Marseillaise” and giving vent to their excitement by performing miracles of acrobatic evolutions. Even Ma’ame Colombe, who was at the moment selecting a piece of meat for Sunday’s dinner, could not help but say to herself that she would not mind Amédé being in the army if he was going to look like that!

===Chinese===
土豆、鸡蛋、黄油全都忘了，赶集的娘们和买主们张着嘴，看呆了。拉拉涅从来没有见过这般漂亮的人材。等队伍开到上街，全村人都出来看希罕，家家农舍的窗口都探出了脑袋。村里的顽童跟在那小小一队人马后面，高唱着《马赛曲》，翻筋斗竖蜻蜓，把满腔兴奋发泄成种种杂技般的奇观。连科隆布大娘——她此刻正在挑一块礼拜天晚饭吃的肉——也不由得暗自寻思：阿梅代若是去当兵，能当出这副模样，她倒也不介意呢！

===Original===
At that very moment one of the urchins paused in the midst of a magnificently sketched somersault in order to run down the street and back to the marketplace, shouting excitedly:

===Chinese===
正在这当口，一个顽童一个漂亮的筋斗翻到一半，忽然收住，撒腿顺街跑回集市，兴奋地大声喊道：

===Original===
“Ma’ame Colombe! Ma’ame Colombe! the soldiers are at the *épicerie*.”

===Chinese===
「科隆布大娘！科隆布大娘！大兵到杂货铺（*épicerie*）来啦。」

===Original===
And so they were! Ma’ame Colombe, hastily straightened her cap and snatching up her market basket, ran to the corner of the Rue Haute just in time to see the soldiers with their officer and wagon come to a halt outside her front door. The worthy Hector with his son Amédé, and the old man who helped in the shop, were busy taking down the shutters and displaying the sacks of various kinds of haricots and lentils in tempting array all along the shop front. Ma’ame Colombe heard the magnificent officer give a quick order: “*Halte!*” and “Attention!” and the next moment she saw him enter the shop followed by his men, the wagon remaining drawn up a little further down the street. The urchins and gaffers crowded round the doorstep open-mouthed, and Ma’ame Colombe had some difficulty in pushing her way through into her own house.

===Chinese===
可不是嘛！科隆布大娘慌忙扶正帽子，抓起菜篮，一口气跑到上街的拐角，来得正是时候，只见士兵们跟着军官和大车，在她家正门外停了下来。体面的埃克托带着儿子阿梅代，还有铺子里帮工的老头儿，正忙着卸门板，把一袋袋各色菜豆、扁豆沿铺面摆得琳琅满目、诱人眼目。科隆布大娘听见那位气派非凡的军官疾声下了两道口令：「*Halte!*」（立定！）「*Attention!*」（立正！）转眼便见他领着部下走进了铺子，大车仍停在这条街稍远一点的地方。顽童和看热闹的老汉们把门槛围得里三层外三层，个个张着嘴，科隆布大娘费了好大的劲才挤进自家家门。

===Original===
The officer began by asking Hector Colombe how many soldiers of the Republic were still sleeping under his roof.

===Chinese===
军官开口先问埃克托·科隆布，他屋檐底下还睡着几个共和国的士兵。

===Original===
“Only the lieutenant and two men, M’sieu’ l’officier,” Hector replied. Whereupon the officer broke in curtly:

===Chinese===
「只有中尉和两个弟兄，军官先生，」埃克托答道。那军官当即打断了话头，简慢地说：

===Original===
“Call me citizen captain. This is the army of the Revolution and its soldiers are not aristos meseems.”

===Chinese===
「叫我公民上尉。这是革命的军队，依我看，它的士兵可不是贵族佬。」

===Original===
Which remark boded no good to Ma’ame Colombe’s ears. Clean or dirty they all appeared to be the same type of brigands; overbearing, exacting and merciless! Ah that poor dear Amédé!

===Chinese===
这话落进科隆布大娘的耳朵里，可不是好兆头。干净也罢，腌臜也罢，瞧来都是一路的土匪：蛮横，苛勒，不讲情面！唉，她那可怜的宝贝阿梅代！

===Original===
The officer then demanded to see the lieutenant and the two soldiers. Amédé offered to call them, but was stopped by a brief command from the captain:

===Chinese===
军官接着要见中尉和那两个士兵。阿梅代自告奋勇要去叫，却被上尉一道简短的命令止住了：

===Original===
“No, not you,” he said curtly, “I want you here, the citizeness can go.”

===Chinese===
「不，不是你，」他简慢地说，「我要你留在这儿。女公民可以去。」

===Original===
Ma’ame Colombe, obedient and vaguely frightened, put down her basket and went upstairs to fetch the lieutenant and the two men, who were still in bed. But although she had only been gone a couple of minutes, her sense of fear took on a more tangible form when she came down again, for she found all the drawers of the counter open, and much of their contents scattered about the floor. Some of the soldiers were busy ferreting about, behind and under the counter. The officer stood in the middle of the shop talking with Hector, who looked both choleric and sullen; in the doorway, the crowd of gaffers were being kept back by two of the soldiers, who were using the butts of their muskets when some venturesome urchin tried to cross the threshold. But what filled poor Ma’ame Colombe’s heart with dismay was the sight of Amédé sitting in the parlour behind the shop, with two other soldiers obviously on guard over him.

===Chinese===
科隆布大娘不敢不从，心里又隐隐发慌，放下菜篮，上楼去叫中尉和那两个士兵——他们还赖在床上没起。可她统共才走开两分钟，再下楼时，那点惶恐已经变成实实在在的惊惧：柜台的抽屉全被拉开了，里头的东西撒了一地。几个士兵正忙着在柜台后头、柜台底下钻来钻去地搜。军官站在铺子当中同埃克托说话，埃克托一脸又恼又倔的神色；门口那群看热闹的老汉被两个士兵拦着，哪个胆大的顽童想跨过门槛，枪托子就敲过去。可最叫可怜的科隆布大娘心头发凉的，是瞧见阿梅代坐在铺子后头的客厅里，另有两个士兵分明是在看守他。

===Original===
Her instinct prompted her to run first of all to her husband with a quick whispered: “Hector, what does this mean?”

===Chinese===
她的本能催着她头一个奔向丈夫，急急地悄声问：「埃克托，这是怎么回事？」

===Original===
But the magnificent officer brusquely thrust himself between her and Hector and said gruffly: “It means, citizeness, that not only treason, but also theft has been traced to this house, and that it is lucky for you that news of it reached the Committee of Sisteron in time, else,” he added grimly, “it had been worse for you and your family.”

===Chinese===
可那位气派非凡的军官粗暴地把身子一横，插到她和埃克托中间，粗声粗气地说：「这就是说，女公民，这户人家不但查出了叛国，还查出了偷窃。算你们运气，消息及时传到了锡斯特龙的委员会；要不然，」他狠狠地补了一句，「你们一家的下场还要更糟。」

===Original===
“Treason and theft?” Ma’ame Colombe exclaimed in hot indignation. “You must take it from me, young man, citizen, captain, or whatever you may be, that I’ll allow no one to﻿—”

===Chinese===
「叛国？偷窃？」科隆布大娘气得火冒三丈，嚷了起来。「你给我听清楚了，年轻人，公民，上尉，随你是个什么吧，我可不许谁来——」

===Original===
“Hold your tongue, woman,” the officer broke in curtly; “you do yourself no good by these protests. Obedience is your wisest course.”

===Chinese===
「住嘴，娘们，」军官简慢地打断她，「这么吵嚷对你没半点好处。乖乖听话，才是你最聪明的路数。」

===Original===
“Good or no good,” Ma’ame Colombe persisted heatedly, “I won’t have the word theft used in connection with this house, and if﻿—”

===Chinese===
「有好处没好处且不管，」科隆布大娘兀自火辣辣地不肯让步，「偷窃这个词儿，休想安到这户人家头上来，要是——」

===Original===
“Make your wife hold her tongue, citizen,” the officer, now addressing Hector once more, broke in curtly, “or I shall have to send her to the *poste* for interfering with a soldier of the Republic in the execution of his duty.”

===Chinese===
「叫你老婆闭嘴，公民，」军官又冲着埃克托简慢地打断，「不然我就只好把她送到宪兵队去，罪名是妨碍共和国士兵执行公务。」

===Original===
Poor Hector Colombe, whose choler was shrinking in inverse ratio to that of his wife, did his best to pacify the worthy dame.

===Chinese===
可怜的埃克托·科隆布，火气正随着老婆火气的上涨成反比地消下去，只得尽力安抚这位可敬的太太。

===Original===
“It is all a mistake, Angelique,” he said gently. “M’sieu’ le Capitaine﻿—pardon! the citizen captain thinks that Amédé has some papers and valuables belonging to Madame﻿—I mean, to the Citizeness Frontenac﻿—”

===Chinese===
「这全是个误会，安热莉克（Angelique），」他温和地说。「上尉先生——啊，恕罪！公民上尉认为，阿梅代手里有几件文书和值钱物件，是夫人的——我是说，是女公民弗朗特纳克的——」

===Original===
“Are they calling my Amédé a thief, then?” Ma’ame Colombe demanded hotly.

===Chinese===
「这么说，他们是把我阿梅代当贼看了？」科隆布大娘勃然质问。

===Original===
“No! No!” Hector replied, trying to be patient and conciliatory. “Have I not told you that it is all a mistake? Everyone knows there are no thieves in this house; but it seems the authorities think that Amédé may have hidden those valuables *pour le bon motif*.”

===Chinese===
「不！不！」埃克托答道，极力耐着性子，好言抚慰。「我不是跟你说过了吗，这全是个误会？谁都知道这户人家没有贼；不过是当官的以为，阿梅代兴许是出于好心的缘故（*pour le bon motif*），才把那些值钱物件藏了起来。」

===Original===
“If he had,” the mother retorted obstinately, “he would say so. Let me just ask him﻿—”

===Chinese===
「他若是藏了，」做母亲的拧着性子回嘴，「自己会说出来的。让我去问问他——」

===Original===
Hector had hold of her hand, but she wrenched it free, and before any of the soldiers could bar the way, she had run into the back parlour, shouting:

===Chinese===
埃克托攥住了她的手，却被她猛地挣脱；不等哪个士兵来得及拦挡，她已一头冲进后客厅，扬声喊道：

===Original===
“Amédé, my little one, have you told those soldiers that you know nothing of Madame’s valuables? Why, *nom de Dieu!*” she went on, hands on hips, defiant and aggressive like the true female defending its young, “look at the innocent. Is that the face of a thief?”

===Chinese===
「阿梅代，我的小心肝，你可告诉了这些大兵，你说你对夫人的值钱物件一概不知？咦，我的老天（*nom de Dieu*）！」她双手叉腰，接着往下说，那副挑衅好斗的架势，活像一头护崽的真正的母兽，「你瞧瞧这副无辜的模样。这像是贼的面孔吗？」

===Original===
She pointed at Amédé, who, however, remained strangely silent.

===Chinese===
她指着阿梅代；阿梅代却古怪地一声也不吭。

===Original===
“*Voyons, mon petit*, tell them!” Angelique Colombe went on with perhaps a shade less assurance than she had displayed at first. The next moment, however, the Captain had seized her unceremoniously by the arm, and dragged her back into the front shop. Here he gave her arm a good shake.

===Chinese===
「*Voyons, mon petit*，我的小宝贝，告诉他们呀！」安热莉克·科隆布接着说，口气比起初已经少了几分把握。可是下一刻，上尉已经不容分说地一把攥住她的胳膊，把她拖回前头的铺子里，就势把她的胳膊狠狠抖了两下。

===Original===
“Did I not order you to hold your tongue?” he demanded roughly.

===Chinese===
「我不是命令过你闭嘴吗？」他粗声喝问。

===Original===
Cowed, in spite of herself, not so much by the officer’s tone of command as by Amédé’s silence, Ma’ame Colombe did in effect, hold her tongue. A sense of disaster as well as of shame had suddenly descended upon her. Her ample bosom heaving, she sank into a chair and threw her apron over her head. She was not crying, but she felt the need of shutting out from her vision, the picture of Amédé looking so confused and sullen, of Hector looking as perplexed as she was herself, as well as of that magnificent officer with his fine clothes and his tricolour sash. But chiefly she wanted for the moment to lose sight of that crowd of gaffers and urchins and neighbours, all staring at her, with that unexplainable feeling, not exactly of contentment for her misfortune, but which can only be expressed by that untranslatable word Schadenfreude. Thus shut out from the rest of her little world, the poor woman slowly rocked herself backwards and forwards, murmuring inaudible words under cover of her apron, until she heard the captain’s voice saying abruptly:

===Chinese===
科隆布大娘不由自主地怯了下去——倒不是被军官的命令口吻吓住的，多半还是被阿梅代的沉默压垮的——当真闭上了嘴。大祸临头加上奇耻大辱的感觉，突然一齐落到她身上。她那丰满的胸脯起伏着，颓然坐进一把椅子，把围裙往头上一蒙。她没有哭，只是觉得非把眼前的景象遮出去不可：阿梅代那副又慌乱又倔硬的样子，埃克托那副同她自己一样六神无主的样子，还有那位衣冠漂亮、系三色绶带的气派军官。而最要紧的，是这一刻再不想看见门外那群老汉、顽童和邻居——人人都盯着她瞧，眼里带着一种说不清道不明的神情，说是对她遭殃称心快意吧，又不止于此，只有那个没法翻译的词儿——幸灾乐祸（Schadenfreude）——才表达得出来。就这样同她那个小天地里的其余一切隔绝开来，可怜的女人身子一前一后地慢慢摇着，躲在围裙底下无声地念念有词，直到听见上尉的嗓音蓦地响起：

===Original===
“Were you the officer in charge of detachment number ninety-seven?”

===Chinese===
「你是第九十七分队的带队军官吗？」

===Original===
Curiosity got the better of sorrow, and Ma’ame Colombe peeped round the edge of her apron. The picture which she saw made her drop her apron altogether. The lieutenant who, the night before, had been so overbearing and so hilarious, stood before his superior officer now, a humble, dejected figure, dreading reprimand, like a schoolboy fearing the cane.

===Chinese===
好奇心压倒了伤心，科隆布大娘从围裙边儿上偷偷探出头来。她瞧见的那幅光景，叫她索性把围裙整个撂下了。头天夜里还那么飞扬跋扈、兴高采烈的中尉，此刻站在自己长官面前，一副低声下气、垂头丧气的模样，提心吊胆地等着挨训，活像个怕吃藤条的小学生。

===Original===
“I am in charge of the detachment ninety-seven﻿—yes, citizen captain,” he replied haltingly.

===Chinese===
「我……是第九十七分队的带队——是的，公民上尉，」他吞吞吐吐地答道。

===Original===
What a contrast these two! Ma’ame Colombe, in spite of her anxiety, her indignation and whatnot, could not help but compare. Womanlike, she had an eye for the handsome male, and what more gorgeous than this Captain of the Republican, or revolutionary army, as he apparently liked to style his men, with his braided jacket and superb tricolour sash, with his blonde hair and fierce moustachios? He poked his tufted chin out at the bedraggled-looking lieutenant before him, looked down with obvious contempt at the latter’s ragged coat and mud-stained breeches. But he made no remark on the want of cleanliness and decency, as Ma’ame Colombe expected him to do.

===Chinese===
这两人一比，真是天渊之别！科隆布大娘尽管满心焦急、愤懑，还有这样那样的一大堆，也禁不住要拿来比一比。女人家天生生着一双识得俊男人的眼睛，而眼前这位共和国军队——或者照他显然爱说的说法，革命军队——的上尉，还有比他更华丽的人物吗？镶绦的军服，华美的三色绶带，金色的头发，威凛的胡子！他把那缀着一撮须的下巴朝面前这副邋遢相的中尉一探，居高临下，明明白白地露出轻蔑，看不起对方那件破外套和溅满泥的裤子。可是，他并没有像科隆布大娘指望的那样，就这副不干不净、不成体统的模样数落上半个字。

===Original===
“Where do you come from?” he demanded.

===Chinese===
「你从哪里来？」他喝问。

===Original===
“From Orange, citizen captain.”

===Chinese===
「从奥朗日来，公民上尉。」

===Original===
“What is your objective?”

===Chinese===
「你们的行军目标是哪里？」

===Original===
“After this, Serres, citizen captain, and then Valence.”

===Chinese===
「过了这一站是塞尔，公民上尉，然后再去瓦朗斯（Valence）。」

===Original===
“And your orders are to arrest on the way every person suspected of treason against the Republic?”

===Chinese===
「你们的命令，是沿途逮捕每一个涉嫌叛国、危害共和国的人，是不是？」

===Original===
“Yes, citizen.”

===Chinese===
「是的，公民。」

===Original===
“And how have you obeyed these orders, citizen lieutenant?” the captain demanded sternly.

===Chinese===
「那么这些命令你执行得怎么样，公民中尉？」上尉声色俱厉地问。

===Original===
“I have done my best, citizen captain,” the other replied with an attempt at bluster; “at Vaison﻿—”

===Chinese===
「我尽了力了，公民上尉，」那一位答话还想撑出几分虚声势，「在韦松（Vaison）——」

===Original===
“I am not talking of Vaison, which you know quite well, citizen lieutenant. I wish to know how you obeyed the orders given to you to arrest the ci-devant Frontenac, his wife and daughter?”

===Chinese===
「我不是在说韦松，这一点你自己心里很清楚，公民中尉。我要知道的是，给你下的逮捕旧贵族弗朗特纳克及其妻女的命令，你执行得怎么样？」

===Original===
“Citizen﻿—”

===Chinese===
「公民——」

===Original===
“Have you done it, citizen lieutenant?” the officer thundered, and all of the bluster went out of the subaltern as he stammered meekly:

===Chinese===
「你到底办了没有，公民中尉？」军官雷吼一声。那下级军官登时虚气全泄，服服帖帖地嗫嚅道：

===Original===
“When we reached the house of the ci-devant Frontenacs, the two women had gone.”

===Chinese===
「我们赶到旧贵族弗朗特纳克家的时候，那两个女人已经走了。」

===Original===
“Gone?” and the Captain’s voice boomed through the low-raftered room like distant roll of cannon. “Gone? Whither?”

===Chinese===
「走了？」上尉的嗓音隆隆滚过低梁的屋子，像远方滚过的炮声。「走了？上哪儿去了？」

===Original===
“Gone, citizen captain,” the lieutenant murmured under his breath: “spirited away. The devil alone knows how.”

===Chinese===
「走了，公民上尉，」中尉压着嗓子咕哝，「凭空没了踪影。只有魔鬼知道是怎么走的。」

===Original===
“Which means that there is a traitor among you.”

===Chinese===
「这就是说，你们中间出了内奸。」

===Original===
“Citizen captain﻿—” the other protested.

===Chinese===
「公民上尉——」那一位还想分辩。

===Original===
“A traitor I say. You had secret orders, and yet the women were warned!” And once more the officer’s glance flashed down with scorn on his unfortunate subordinate. His blonde hair seemed to bristle with wrath; his moustachios stood out like spikes: he looked a veritable god of vengeance and of wrath.

===Chinese===
「我说的就是内奸。你们领的是密令，可那两个女人还是得了风声！」军官的目光又一次挟着轻蔑，直扫向他那倒霉的部下。他一头金发仿佛根根倒竖，怒气冲冲；两撇胡子像铁刺一样张开：他看上去真像一尊复仇之神、震怒之神。

===Original===
“Where,” he thundered, “is the ci-devant Frontenac?”

===Chinese===
「旧贵族弗朗特纳克，」他雷鸣般喝道，「人在哪里？」

===Original===
“At the commissariat, citizen captain, guarded by our men,” the lieutenant replied.

===Chinese===
「押在宪兵队里，公民上尉，由我们的人看守着，」中尉答道。

===Original===
“And the rest of your detachment?”

===Chinese===
「你分队其余的人呢？」

===Original===
“In billets in the village.”

===Chinese===
「在村子里宿营。」

===Original===
“And did you search this house when you entered it?”

===Chinese===
「你进这户人家的时候，搜查过没有？」

===Original===
“No﻿—that is﻿—no﻿—I did not﻿—that is﻿—” stammered the wretched man.

===Chinese===
「没——就是——没——我没有——就是——」那可怜虫结结巴巴地说。

===Original===
“Or the other houses where you billeted your men?”

===Chinese===
「你部下宿营的其他人家呢？」

===Original===
But this time the lieutenant only shook his head in dejected silence.

===Chinese===
这一回，中尉只垂头丧气、一言不发地摇了摇头。

===Original===
“Which means that you allowed soldiers of the Republic to sleep under strange roofs without ascertaining whether they were safe. Why, citizen Lieutenant, this place might have been swarming with traitors.”

===Chinese===
「这就是说，共和国的士兵睡在人家的屋檐底下，你也不先查一查那人家稳妥不稳妥。哼，公民中尉，这地方说不定早就挤满了奸细呢。」

===Original===
“The people here, citizen, are﻿—”

===Chinese===
「这地方的人，公民，都是——」

===Original===
“Enough. You are relieved of your command, and you will proceed now with us to Sisteron where you will render an account of your conduct before the Committee of Public Safety.”

===Chinese===
「够了。你被解除指挥权了。你这就随我们一道开赴锡斯特龙，到公安委员会面前交代你的所作所为。」

===Original===
Ma’ame Colombe, who had watched the two men closely during this exciting colloquy, saw an ashen hue spread over the Lieutenant’s face, beneath the thick coating of grime. Though they did not know much in this tucked-away corner of Dauphiné, of what went on in the great cities, they had vaguely heard how great officers of the army had been deprived of their rank and sent to the guillotine for not doing their duty by the army of the Republic. The crowd at the door had also listened in silence; many a cheek turned pale at sound of that thundering voice which held in its arrogant tone a menace of death.

===Chinese===
这场舌战来得紧张，科隆布大娘一直瞪大眼睛盯着这两人；这时她看见一层死灰的颜色，从中尉那厚厚一层积垢的脸皮底下泛了上来。虽说多菲内这个偏僻的角落，对大城里的风云知道得不多，可他们也影影绰绰地听说过，军队里多大的军官，只因对不起共和国的军队、有亏职守，就被革去军衔、送上断头台。门口的人群也屏着气听着；那雷鸣般的嗓音里挟着杀气，多少人的脸颊都听白了。

===Original===
And now the captain turned to the other two down-at-heel soldiers who stood skulking behind their lieutenant.

===Chinese===
这时，上尉又转向另外那两个破衣烂鞋的士兵，他们正缩头缩脑地躲在长官背后。

===Original===
“Go,” he commanded, “round the billets where your comrades are. Bring them hither. And one of you to the commissariat, and bring the ci-devant here too. And no delay, remember. No gossip on the way as you value your lives. I give you five minutes to have all the men and the prisoner here.”

===Chinese===
「去，」他命令道，「到你们弟兄宿营的各户走一圈，把他们全带到这儿来。你们俩再抽一个去宪兵队，把那旧贵族也一并押来。不许耽搁，记住了。路上不许嚼舌根，除非你们不要命了。我限你们五分钟，把所有人连同俘虏一齐带到。」

===Original===
The men went immediately to execute the peremptory order, while the lieutenant remained in the shop looking the picture of humility and dejection. Ma’ame Colombe who had a kindly heart inside her ample bosom, felt almost sorry for the man, so miserable did he look. Indeed it seemed as if this squad of elegantly clad soldiers sowed anguish and terror in their path.

===Chinese===
那两人立刻去执行这道说一不二的命令，中尉却还留在铺子里，一副低眉顺眼、灰头土脸的光景。科隆布大娘那丰满的胸脯里装着一副好心肠，见这人这般狼狈，几乎要替他难受起来了。说真的，这一队衣冠楚楚的士兵，所过之处撒下的仿佛尽是伤心和恐怖。

===Original===
But the worst was yet to come. Ma’ame Colombe thought that she had probed the last depths of humiliation when she heard that gorgeous officer call her Amédé a thief. To such a pass had this so-called revolution brought the respectable children of France, that they saw themselves bullied and insulted, and held up to shame before their neighbours. What was all that in comparison with the shame of seeing Amédé confronted with the proof that in very truth he was in possession of papers and valuables which were the property of Madame de Frontenac?

===Chinese===
可是，最糟的还在后头呢。科隆布大娘原以为，听见那位衣光焕彩的军官骂她的阿梅代是贼，羞辱已经受到底了。这场所谓的革命，把法兰西体面人家的儿女折腾到了这步田地：眼睁睁看着自己受人欺凌、受人辱骂，在乡邻面前出尽了大丑。可这同后一种羞辱比起来，又算得了什么——眼看着阿梅代被人当面拿住了真凭实据，证明他手里实实在在存着德·弗朗特纳克夫人的文书和值钱物件！

===Original===
It all happened so quickly. Poor Ma’ame Colombe could scarce believe her eyes. All that she saw was two soldiers guided by their sumptuous captain go straight through the back parlour and out by the back door into the yard. What happened out there she did not know, but a minute or two later the three men were standing once more in the parlour, and the captain had in his hand a small box, a thick leather wallet and a bag which obviously contained money.

===Chinese===
这一切来得那么快，可怜的科隆布大娘简直不敢相信自己的眼睛。她只看见两名士兵由他们那位衣饰华贵的上尉带领着，径直穿过后客厅，出了后门进了院子。院子里出了什么事，她不知道；可不一两分钟后，三个人又回到了客厅，上尉手里拿着一只小匣子、一只厚皮钱夹，还有一只一看便知装着银钱的钱袋。

===Original===
At sight of these Amédé﻿—her Amédé﻿—had jumped to his feet as if he had been stung; all the blood rushed to his face, and made it crimson with choler, and it looked for the moment as if he would hurl himself on the officer of the Republican army﻿—which would have meant instant death for him, as the soldiers had already shouldered their muskets. Ma’ame Colombe gave a terrified shriek, whereat Amédé suddenly seemed to realize his position, the flush died out of his poor face, and with eyes downcast he resumed his former silent, constrained attitude.

===Chinese===
一见了这些东西，阿梅代——她的阿梅代——像被什么东西蜇了似的，霍地跳了起来；满腔热血直冲到脸上，激得脸通红，那一刻看样子竟要朝那位共和国军队的军官扑过去——那可是要当场送命的，因为士兵们早已端平了火枪。科隆布大娘发出一声吓破了胆的尖叫，阿梅代这才猛然醒悟自己的处境；那可怜的脸上的红潮褪了下去，他垂下眼睛，恢复了先前那副沉默拘谨的姿态。

===Original===
The Captain shrugged his shoulders and with a note of dry sarcasm in his voice he said:

===Chinese===
上尉耸了耸肩，话音里带着一丝干巴巴的讥诮，说道：

===Original===
“I see you make no attempt at denial. You are wise, citizen. Try and induce your mother not to shriek and you’ll find that everything will turn out for the best.”

===Chinese===
「我看你并不打算抵赖。你是个明白人，公民。劝住你母亲，叫她别尖叫，你会发现一切都会有个最好的了局。」

===Original===
He did not say this unkindly, and poor Ma’ame Colombe even thought that she detected an indulgent tone in his voice. She rose to her feet and put her podgy hands together, and when the Captain reentered the shop, she looked up at him with tearful, entreating eyes.

===Chinese===
这话说得不带恶意，可怜的科隆布大娘甚至还觉得，自己从他嗓音里听出了一点宽容的调子。她站起身来，把一双胖乎乎的手合在胸口；等上尉重新走进铺子，她抬起头，用一双泪汪汪的、哀恳的眼睛望着他。

===Original===
“He did it with a good motive, M’sieu’ le﻿—I mean citizen captain. Look at the innocent. He is no thief. I swear he is no thief. I’d like,” she went on, turning fiercely round and darting defiant glances on the crowd of gaffers on the doorstep, “I’d like to see the man who dared to say that my Amédé is a thief.”

===Chinese===
「他是一片好心才这么做的，上尉先——我是说，公民上尉。您瞧瞧这副无辜的模样。他不是贼。我赌咒他不是贼。我倒要看看，」她猛地转过身去，朝门槛上那群看热闹的老汉甩过一道挑衅的目光，接着说，「我倒要看看，哪个人敢说我阿梅代是贼。」

===Original===
The officer had handed the *pièces de conviction* to one of his men, with orders to put them in the wagon. Then he commanded Amédé to stand up before him.

===Chinese===
军官已把那些罪证物件（*pièces de conviction*）交给一名部下，吩咐放到大车上去。接着，他命令阿梅代站到他跟前来。

===Original===
“Thief or no thief,” he said dryly, “you are guilty of having acted contrary to the interests of the Republic. You know what that means?”

===Chinese===
「是贼不是贼且不论，」他干巴巴地说，「你行事违背了共和国的利益，这就构成了罪。你可知道这是什么意思？」

===Original===
Amédé made no reply, only hung his head, and twiddled his hot fingers together.

===Chinese===
阿梅代没有答话，只垂着脑袋，两只滚烫的手指头绞来绞去。

===Original===
“It means,” the officer continued, “that but for one thing, your life would have had to answer for this act of treason.”

===Chinese===
「这就是说，」军官接着道，「若不是有一样缘故，你这条命，就得拿这桩叛国的勾当来抵偿。」

===Original===
A groan went round the crowd on whose ears those words had fallen like the toll of a passing bell. But Ma’ame Colombe did not utter a sound. She clung to her Hector and the two old people stood there hand in hand, striving by this loving contact to conquer the icy fear that had gripped their hearts.

===Chinese===
人群里滚过一声呻吟，那两句话落在众人耳中，就像送葬的钟声敲过。可科隆布大娘一声也没有吭。她紧紧偎着她的埃克托，两位老人手搀着手站在那里，想凭这一点恩爱的偎依，压住攫住他们心头的冰冷的恐惧。

===Original===
“The one thing that will probably save you,” the officer resumed after a dramatic pause, “is that the Republic has need of you in her revolutionary army. The enemy is at the gates of France, you are young, healthy, vigorous; it is for you to show your mettle by defending your country. Thus you will redeem the past. For the moment it is my duty to take you before the Committee of Public Safety, whose final word will dispose of your fate.”

===Chinese===
「那一样多半能救你的缘故，」军官煞有介事地顿了一顿，又接着说，「就是共和国的革命军队正用得着你。敌人打到了法兰西的大门口，你年轻，结实，筋骨强壮；正该拿出你的胆气来，保卫你的祖国。这样，你就能赎清前愆。眼下，我的职分是把你解到公安委员会去，你的命运，全凭他们最后的一句话发落。」

===Original===
He spoke loudly so that all the listeners might hear. Gaffers and urchins and market-women hardly dared to breathe. They felt awed, and could only gaze at one another, as if trying to read each other’s thoughts. And while awed whispers still went the round, the down-at-heel soldiers, who had spent the night in the village, came skulking back in groups of two or threes. They pushed their way through the crowd into the shop. One of the last to arrive was M. de Frontenac, closely guarded by two of the men.

===Chinese===
他说得很响，好教人人听得见。老汉、顽童和赶集的娘们，大气也不敢出。他们心里只觉敬畏，面面相觑，仿佛想从彼此脸上读出各自的心思。敬畏的窃窃私语还在人群里转着圈儿，在村里过了一夜的那些破衣烂鞋的士兵，已经三三两两、缩头缩脑地回来了。他们从人群中挤进铺子。到得最迟的几个人里头，就有德·弗朗特纳克先生，由两名士兵紧紧押着。

===Original===
And there they all stood now in the shop, a dozen or so of them, beside the sacks of haricots and button-onions and split peas; all of them with the exception of the prisoner, looking dirty and bedraggled, with their worsted caps covered in dust, bits of hay and straw clinging to their coats and to their hair, bare-legged and grimy-faced, the steel of their bayonets dull with sludge, their breeches mud-stained. Such a contrast to their superb officer and his splendidly attired squad. And they could hear the women drawing humiliating comparisons, tittering and pointing fingers of scorn at them, whilst even the drabs, with whom they had drunk and jested the night before, turned contemptuous shoulders upon them now.

===Chinese===
此刻他们全都站在铺子里，十来个人，挨着一袋袋菜豆、珍珠洋葱和干豌豆瓣；除了那名俘虏，个个都显得又脏又邋遢：绒线帽子上落满尘土，干草麦秸的碎屑粘在外套上、头发上，光着两条腿，脸上乌一块黑一块，刺刀的钢刃叫泥浆糊得暗淡无光，裤子上溅满泥点。同他们那位气概非凡的军官和他那支服饰华美的队伍一比，真是天壤之别。他们还听得见女人们作者种种不堪的比对，嗤嗤笑着，伸出手指戳点着奚落他们；连头天夜里同他们喝酒调笑过的那些荡妇，此刻也把轻蔑的脊背朝着他们了。

===Original===
And thus they were mustered before the magnificent captain; all soldiers together, shoulder to shoulder, the down-at-heel and the grandees﻿—aristos one would have called them, only that they were of the Revolutionary Army, which set out to exterminate the very last of the aristocracy, the hated tyrants and dissolute brood. And while they stood there, under the eye of the officer, the crowd outside watched them, and instinctively something of the spirit that animated the rest of France, swept like a poisonous sirocco over these worthy villagers of Laragne; the same spirit that in the great cities sent old women knitting and gossiping at the foot of the guillotine and that prompted young girls to dip their kerchiefs in the blood of its victims. A poisonous wind like the breath of demons! Some of the men and women had been to Sisteron and heard the hymn of hate, the “Carmagnole”! “*Ça ira! Ça ira! Les aristos à la lanterne!*” One or two of them began to hum it, stamping their feet to its rhythm.

===Chinese===
他们就这样在那位气派非凡的上尉面前列队听点；大家全是当兵的，肩并着肩——破衣烂鞋的和衣冠楚楚的站在一处；若不是他们正属革命军队——那支立志要把贵族阶级、可恨的暴君和淫侈的孽种斩尽杀绝的军队——人家简直要管他们叫贵族佬了。他们站在军官的眼皮底下，门外的人群望着他们；不知不觉间，那股激励着法兰西各地的风气，已经像一股有毒的西罗科风（sirocco），扫过了拉拉涅这些善良村民的心头；正是这股风气，在大城市里教老太婆们坐在断头台脚下打毛线、说长道短，教年轻姑娘们把手绢浸到受刑人的血里。一股毒风，就像群魔呵出的气息！这些人里，有几个男人女人到过锡斯特龙，听过那首仇恨的赞歌《卡马尼奥拉》（Carmagnole）：「*Ça ira! Ça ira! Les aristos à la lanterne!*」——就要好啦！就要好啦！把贵族佬吊上路灯去！——有一两个人已经哼起这调子，用脚和着节拍踏起地来。

===Original===
Gradually the song swelled, one after another they took up the tune, these village men and maids who, unbeknown even to themselves had absorbed some of the insidious poison of hatred and black envy.

===Chinese===
歌声渐渐涨起来，一个接一个跟上了调子——这些村里的男人和姑娘，连他们自己也没有觉察，早已中了仇恨和黑心嫉妒那潜移默化的毒。

===Original===
“Right! Turn!” the Captain commanded, and marking time with their feet, the little squad now over twenty strong, started on its way. Ma’ame Colombe, now loudly moaning, still clung to her boy. He was very brave and tried to reassure and to comfort her. Anyway he would have had to go today, he argued, his orders were to report himself at Serres, to be drafted into the army. From the officer’s attitude it certainly seemed as if nothing more terrifying was to happen to him. The boy was brave enough too not to let his mother know how doubly his heart ached, because he was saying goodbye to his home, and could not say goodbye to Fleurette. His heart was filled with the image of Fleurette, but he would not add to his mother’s sorrow by speaking to her of his own. He was just an unsophisticated village lad, knowing little, understanding less. His own life and comfort were nothing to him, beside the sorrow which his mother felt and which, he knew, would bring such countless tears in Fleurette’s lovely blue eyes. The father too tried to be brave; the effort to keep back his tears brought the perspiration streaming down his round, kindly face. When the crowd﻿—his friends and neighbours some of them﻿—intoned the revolutionary song, his powerful fist was clenched, but he did not shake it at the singers. His sound common sense had come again to his rescue, and whispered to him that for Amédé’s sake, quiet submission was the soundest policy.

===Chinese===
「向右——转！」上尉一声令下，小小的一队人马踏着步点开拔了，如今已凑足二十多人。科隆布大娘这会儿放声哀哭着，兀自搂住她的儿子不放。儿子很勇敢，竭力宽慰她、给她壮胆。横竖他今天也得走了，他辩解说，他的军令是到塞尔去报到，编入军队的。瞧那军官的态度，他的事多半不会再有更可怕的下场了。这孩子也够勇敢，不肯让母亲知道他自己的心是怎样双重地作痛——既是要同家门道别，又不能同弗勒蕾特道一声别。他心里满满装着弗勒蕾特的影子，却不愿再拿自己的伤心去增添母亲的心伤。他不过是个没见过世面的乡下小子，懂的少，明白的更少。他自己的性命和安逸，比起母亲的伤心来，在他眼里算不得什么；而且他知道，那伤心事还不知要在弗勒蕾特那双可爱的蓝眼睛里，催落多少数也数不清的泪水呢。父亲也竭力振作；忍住眼泪的那番用力，叫他圆滚滚的、慈和的脸上汗珠直流。人群——里头有他的朋友和乡邻——唱起那首革命歌的时候，他那只粗壮的拳头攥得紧紧的，却没有朝唱歌的人挥去。他那健全的常识又来救了他，悄悄对他说：为了阿梅代，安安静静地低头，才是最稳妥的路数。

===Original===
While mother and son clung to one another in a last farewell. Hector contrived to approach M. de Frontenac who, alone in the midst of such excitement and such conflicting emotions, had remained perfectly calm. The casual observer, not knowing him, might have thought that the fate of his wife and daughter, his separation from them, and the blow that destiny had dealt to these worthy folk here, whom he had known all his life, had left him completely indifferent. He had spent the night in a prison cell, under the eye of men﻿—the local gendarmes﻿—whose welfare and whose families had been his care for years; but seemingly he had slept peacefully. At any rate his face showed no sign of fatigue, or his eyes of sleeplessness. He had dressed with scrupulous care; his well-worn clothes, the ones he was wearing at dinner when the soldiers made irruption into the château, were clean and tidily put on; his cravat neatly tied, his hair smooth. When Hector Colombe approached him, he gripped the worthy *épicier* warmly by the hand.

===Chinese===
母子俩正搂作一团，依依诀别，埃克托却瞅空子挨近了德·弗朗特纳克先生。在这样一片激动、这样百感交攻之中，唯独他始终安之若素。不相识的旁观者，也许会以为妻女的下落、同她们的生离，以及命运落到这些他相识了一辈子的善良人身上的打击，都未能搅动他的心，他全然无动于衷。他在牢房里过了一夜，处在那些人的眼皮底下——本地的宪兵——这些人的生计和家小，多年来都是他挂心照拂的；可他显然睡得安稳。至少，他脸上不见一丝倦容，眼里也没有半点失眠的痕迹。他穿戴得一丝不苟：那身穿旧了的衣裳，正是士兵们冲进庄园那天他吃着晚饭的那一身，干干净净、齐齐整整地穿在身上；领巾打得端端正正，头发梳得服服帖帖。埃克托·科隆布走近时，他热热地握住了这位体面杂货商的手。

===Original===
And now the crowd parted to allow the soldiers to pass. Some of the girls tried to ogle the handsome ones and to leer at the others, but no one attempted to do more than stare in awe and admiration at the magnificent officer. The two prisoners were ordered to mount into the wagon; one of the soldiers took the reins and the next moment the order, “Quick March!” was given.

===Chinese===
这时人群闪开，放士兵们通过。有几个姑娘朝俊的抛媚眼，朝其余的挤鬼脸；可对那位气派非凡的军官，大家除了又敬又羡地盯着看，谁也不敢再多一分。两名俘虏被喝令爬上大车；一个士兵接过缰绳，紧接着一声令下：「开步——走！」

===Original===
The crowd broke into an excited “Hurrah!” and the little squad slowly moved off, officer *en tête*, and the wagon in the rear, in the direction of Sisteron. Then one of the villagers once more struck up the Carmagnole, and the crowd took it up. “*Ça ira! ça ira!*” they sang gaily, and the men took the girls by the waist and twirled them round in a gay rigadoon. Old men and young girls; for there were no young men in the villages of France these days, when the army claimed them all, they danced and twirled in the wake of the retreating squad, and around them barefooted urchins somersaulted along in the cloud of dust raised by the horses and wagon.

===Chinese===
人群爆出一声兴高采烈的「乌拉！」小小的一队人马缓缓开动了，军官走在队首（*en tête*），大车殿后，取道锡斯特龙方向而去。跟着，村里又有人领头唱起了《卡马尼奥拉》，人群应声接了上去。「*Ça ira! ça ira!*」——就要好啦！就要好啦！——他们欢欢喜喜地唱着，男人们搂住姑娘们的腰，旋起一支欢快的里戈东舞（rigadoon）。老头儿配着年轻的姑娘——如今法兰西的村子里再没有年轻男人了，军队把他们全征了去——他们跟在渐渐远去的小队后面跳着、旋着；在他们四周，光着脚板的顽童们在人马大车扬起的尘雾里，一路翻着筋斗。

===Original===
And that was the picture that Amédé Colombe and Charles de Frontenac, sitting side by side in the wagon, saw gradually receding before their eyes as they were driven away, prisoners from their homes.

===Chinese===
阿梅代·科隆布和夏尔·德·弗朗特纳克（Charles de Frontenac）并肩坐在大车上，被押解着离家而去；这幅光景在他们眼前渐渐退远、慢慢消隐——他们就这样，成了背井离乡的囚徒。

## XIII / 第十三章

===Original===
But in Lou Mas nothing was known of the tragic events that were occurring at Laragne. Old Louise and Fleurette were busy with housework, and if Fleurette went about the house, silent and wistful, it was because presently she would have to say the inevitable farewell to Amédé.

===Chinese===
可是拉拉涅那边正发生着惨事，庐庄里却一无所闻。老路易丝和弗勒蕾特忙着家务；弗勒蕾特若是在屋里默默转悠、一脸怅惘，那是因为眼看她就得同阿梅代道那一场免不了的别离。

===Original===
