“Citizen lieutenant,” he said at last, “you have heard the decision of the committee. It has been found expedient to withhold punishment from you, because it is in your power to serve the State in a way that no other man could do at this moment. You have seen the English spies face to face; you know something of their appearance, something of their mode of speech. Go then into the highways and byways, the men who with you were guilty of negligence shall go with you. It is for you to use the full powers which the Law of the Suspect has placed in your hands. Go scour the country. Yours is the power to seize any man, woman or child whom you suspect of treason to the State, make use of that power in order to track down to their lair the English foxes who have outwitted you. Only let me add a word of warning in your ear. Do not be led by the nose a second time. If you are, no power on earth will save you. The State may forgive incompetence once: the second time it will bear the ugly name of treason.”

===Chinese===
「公民中尉，」他终于开口道，「委员会的裁决，你已经听见了。暂且不惩办你，是因为此际你还能以一种别人谁也顶替不了的方式为国家效力。你同那伙英国奸细当面照过；他们的模样，你知道了几分；他们的腔调，你也知道了几分。那就到通衢大道、僻巷小路上去吧，同你一起疏于职守的人，跟你同去。嫌疑犯法令已然交到你手里的全权，就由你去行使。去，把这一乡一野细细梳一遍。凡是你疑心叛国的男人、女人、孩子，你都有权捉拿；就行使这个权柄，去把那班耍弄了你的英国狐狸，一路追到它们的老窝里去。只是容我在你耳边再进一言，提个醒。切莫再叫人牵着鼻子走第二遭。若再有那么一遭，普天之下没有任何权柄救得了你。无能，国家或许饶恕一回：到第二回，它就要顶上那个丑名——叛国。」

===Original===
He had risen to his feet, and just for a moment the muscles of his hand relaxed, and the scrap of paper which he had crushed into a ball rolled upon the table.

===Chinese===
他已经站起身来；恰在那一瞬，他手上的肌肉松了一松，那张叫他攥成一团的纸片，便滚落在桌面上。

===Original===
His colleague Pochart picked it up and idly opened and smoothed it out: he studied for a moment or two the close writing upon it, then looked inquiringly up at Chauvelin.

===Chinese===
同僚波夏尔把它拾起，漫不经心地展开抚平：他对上面密密的字迹端详了片刻，随即抬起眼来，朝肖维兰投去询问的一瞥。

===Original===
“Can you tell us what is written on this paper, citizen?” he asked.

===Chinese===
「你能告诉我们这纸上写的是什么吗，公民？」他问道。

===Original===
And while he spoke he tossed the paper across to his colleague Danou.

===Chinese===
说话间，他把那张纸片丢给了同僚达努。

===Original===
“Is it English?” Danou asked puzzled.

===Chinese===
「是英文么？」达努困惑地问。

===Original===
“Yes,” Chauvelin replied curtly.

===Chinese===
「是英文，」肖维兰简截地答道。

===Original===
“It looks like poetry,” Pochart remarked.

===Chinese===
「看着像诗，」波夏尔说。

===Original===
“Doggerel verses,” commented Chauvelin.

===Chinese===
「打油诗罢了，」肖维兰评了一句。

===Original===
“And you can’t read it?”

===Chinese===
「那你还读不懂它？」

===Original===
“No!”

===Chinese===
「读不懂！」

===Original===
“I thought you knew English.”

===Chinese===
「我还当您懂英文呢。」

===Original===
“Not I.”

===Chinese===
「我不懂。」

===Original===
“Strange why a bit of doggerel verse should have been slipped into the pocket of Citizen Godet’s tunic,” Pochart remarked dryly. “And there’s your name, Citizen Chauvelin,” he added, pointing to the words “A *mon ami* Chauvelin,” which preceded the four lines of poetry written in English, a language which, apparently, no one here understood.

===Chinese===
「怪了，怎么会有一首打油诗，偏偏塞进公民戈岱的上衣口袋，」波夏尔干巴巴地说。「这儿还有您的大名呢，公民肖维兰，」他补了一句，指着那四行英文诗前头的一行题字『A *mon ami* Chauvelin』（给我朋友肖维兰）——那诗是用英文写的，而看样子，这儿没有一个人懂这门语文。

===Original===
But Chauvelin was at the end of his patience. He seized the scrap of paper and tore it savagely into innumerable little pieces.

===Chinese===
可是肖维兰的耐性已经到了头。他一把抓过那张纸片，狠狠撕作数不清的碎片。

===Original===
“Enough of this futility,” he said, and brought his clenched fist down with a crash on the table. “The English spies have been facetious, that is all. We do them too much honour by attaching importance to this senseless, childish verse. Lieutenant Godet,” he went on, once more addressing the accused, “you are dismissed, under the conditions I told you of just now. When next we meet face to face, you will either be the lucky man who has helped to lay these impudent English adventurers by the heel, or you will stand before me arraigned for treason and preparing for death. Now you can go.”

===Chinese===
「这种无用的空话，够了，」他说，攥紧的拳头砰的一声捶在桌上。「那伙英国奸细不过打了个诨，仅此而已。给这首没意味的孩子气的歪诗这样郑重其事，倒是高抬他们了。戈岱中尉，」他接着说，重新朝被告说下去，「你退下吧，条件就按我方才说的。下一回你我再当面相见，你要么是那个走运的人，帮着把那班无法无天的英国冒险家一举擒获；要么就站到我面前来，顶着一纸叛国的控状，等死。现在你可以走了。」

===Original===
Without another word Godet turned on his heel and went out of the room. Past the guard at the door, he went with head erect, and with a firm step he walked the whole length of the corridor. But there was one moment when in the vestibule he found himself alone. Unwatched. At any rate he thought so. So he paused and looked over his shoulder in the direction of the room where he had just spent an uncomfortable two hours. He paused and raising his fist, he shook it at the unseen presence of the man who had so terrorized him, and whom he hated because of the terror which he inspired.

===Chinese===
戈岱再没有一句话，一转身出了屋子。走过门口的岗哨，他昂着头，步子坚实，走完了整条长廊。可是有一刻，他在门厅里发觉只剩了自己一个人。没人盯着。至少他这样以为。于是他站住，回头朝那间屋子的方向望了一眼——在那屋里，他刚熬过了不好受的两个钟头。他站定，举起拳头，朝着那个看不见的身影挥了一挥——那人把他吓成了这样，他也正因为这份吓人的威风，恨透了那人。

===Original===
“With a bit of luck,” he muttered through his teeth, “we shall be even yet, you and I, *mon ami* Chauvelin.”

===Chinese===
「只要运气不太坏，」他从牙缝里咕哝道，「咱们总还有清账的一天，你和我，我的朋友（*mon ami*）肖维兰。」

===Original===
Then once more with a firm step he walked out of the Town Hall.

===Chinese===
随后他重新迈开坚实的步子，走出市政厅去了。

## XVIII / 第十八章

===Original===
It was on the following day that the *coche* from Sisteron was due to arrive at Orange, and Lieutenant Godet, his mind set on the one purpose, to find a clue to his mysterious adventure with the English spies, hied him to the posting-inn which is situated in the Rue de la République.

===Chinese===
就在第二天，从锡斯特龙开来的乡村公共马车（*coche*）该到奥朗日了。戈岱中尉一门心思只惦着一桩事——要为那场同英国间谍的神秘遭遇寻出一点线索——便径自往共和国街（Rue de la République）上的那家客栈去了。

===Original===
At noon the *coche*, covered with dust, unloaded its wearied passengers; a farmer and his son come to negotiate a sale of stock; the wife of Citizen Henriot, the lawyer, home from her annual visit to her mother; two or three skilled artisans from the country, come to seek their fortune in town, and so on; and finally there descended from the *coche* two women, one of whom carried a small bundle, while the other﻿—well! at sight of the other Citizen Lieutenant Godet uttered such a cry of surprise and of excitement that the crowd around him thought that here was a poor soldier who had taken leave of his senses.

===Chinese===
正午时分，那辆乡村公共马车满身尘土，卸下了一车疲惫的旅客：一个农夫带着儿子，来洽谈一桩牲口买卖；律师公民昂里奥（Henriot）的妻子，一年一度回娘家探望过母亲，正在归途；两三个乡下来的手艺人，进城来寻出路，诸如此类；最后，车上又下来两个女人，一个挎着个小包袱，至于另一个嘛——嘿！一见到另一位，公民戈岱中尉就发出那么一声又惊又喜的喊叫，倒叫他周围的人群以为，这里是一个发了疯的可怜大兵。

===Original===
The woman who had caused Lieutenant Godet thus to lose his self-control, was a perfectly self-possessed young woman wrapped in a cloak and hood from beneath which peeped strands of golden curls that vied in colour with the ripe corn of the Dauphiné, and eyes bluer than the sky that spread over Orange on this exquisite midday in May. The older woman who accompanied her appeared travel-stained, weary and cross; not so this beautiful girl, who tripped lightly from the *coche* towards the parlour of the posting-inn and with a little air of triumph and encouragement called gaily to her companion:

===Chinese===
害得戈岱中尉这般失了自持的女人，是位再镇定不过的年轻姑娘。她裹着斗篷，戴着兜帽，帽沿底下探出几绺金色鬈发，那颜色赛得过多菲内成熟的麦穗；一双眼睛，比这个明媚的五月正午铺展在奥朗日上空的蓝天还要蓝。陪她同行的那位年长妇人风尘满面，又倦又恼；这位美丽的姑娘却全然不是这样：她轻快地从车上下来，走向客栈的客厅，带着一点小得意、小鼓舞的神气，快活地招呼她的同伴：

===Original===
“The end of our journey, my Louise! And now to find Bibi!”

===Chinese===
「我们的旅程到头啦，我的路易丝！这就去找到比比！」

===Original===
Even in these days of advanced democracy which in Orange had of late reached its apogee, the shattering of ancient manners and customs had not got to the stage where a beautiful woman would not command the attention and services of impressionable males, to the exclusion of others less favoured. And thus it came to pass that while the other weary and travel-stained passengers were left to look after themselves and their bits of luggage, and to wait their turn until such time as the servants of the inn were pleased to get them refreshments, the landlord himself, a florid man in shirt sleeves and baize apron, bustled obsequiously around Fleurette and Louise, offering wine, bread and advice, polishing the chairs on which they were invited to sit, and generally placing himself and his house at the disposal of this attractive customer.

===Chinese===
即使在这先进民主的年代——这民主在奥朗日近来已达于极盛——古老风习的打碎，也还没到这一步田地：一位美丽的女人，竟不能把容易动情的男子们的注意和效力统统召到自己身边，把不那么受垂青的人一概挤出去。于是就有了这样的事：其余那些疲惫风尘的旅客，都得自己照管自己和自己那一点点行李，都得挨着号等，直等到客栈的伙计们高兴了，才给他们端来饮食；而客栈老板本人——一个红光满面、光穿着衬衫、系一条台面呢围裙的汉子——却在弗勒蕾特和路易丝身边殷勤地忙前忙后，献上酒、面包，还有种种主意，把请她们落座的椅子揩了又揩，总而言之，把他本人连同他这座店，一齐听凭这位迷人顾客的差遣。

===Original===
Fleurette took all these attentions as a matter of course. She was accustomed to being the centre of attraction at Lou Mas or in the house of M’sieu’ and Ma’ame Colombe, and although her trust in the goodwill of men had received one or two somewhat rude shocks of late, she still retained that self-possession and gentle air of mingled modesty and graciousness which is the attribute of every pretty, unspoilt woman. She asked for a room where she and her companion might tidy their kerchiefs and caps, and use their precious piece of scented soap, and she felt so triumphant and so elated that when she found herself in the privacy of that room she took poor old Louise by the waist and twirled her round and round in a mad dance.

===Chinese===
弗勒蕾特把这一切殷勤看作理所当然。她在庐庄、在科隆布先生和科隆布大娘的家里，做众人瞩目的中心惯了；虽说近来她对世人善意的信任，已经挨过一两记相当粗暴的打击，她却仍旧保有那份自若沉着，和那种既腼腆又亲切的温柔神气——那是每一个美丽而不骄纵的女子天生的品性。她要了一个房间，好让她和她的同伴整理头巾和便帽，用一用她们那块宝贵的香皂；她心里那么得意，那么欢畅，一到那间屋子的清净里，便搂住可怜的老路易丝的腰，拉着她转了一圈又一圈，跳起一支疯舞。

===Original===
“We are in Orange, Louise darling!” she cried. “We are here! here! here! and in less than an hour we shall have found Bibi, and Bibi will have commanded Amédé to be set free! Just think of it, Louise,” she went on more seriously, “four whole days since he was arrested! Poor, poor Amédé, under a horrible accusation of a sin which he never committed! What he must have suffered! What he must have thought of me who knew the truth and did not at once set to work to obtain his freedom.﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「我们到奥朗日啦，亲爱的路易丝！」她嚷道。「我们到了！到了！到了！不出一个钟头，咱们就能找着比比，比比就会吩咐把阿梅代放出来！你想想吧，路易丝，」她接着往下说，口气郑重了些，「他被抓走整整四天了！可怜的、可怜的阿梅代，顶着一项可怕的罪名，一桩他从来不曾犯下的罪！他该受了多少苦啊！他心里该怎么看我这个人——我知道真相，却没有立刻着手去争他的自由……」

===Original===
Gradually her tone became more and more dull, all the excitement died out of it. She saw Amédé in prison, with irons round his wrists and ankles, or else standing before stern judges who condemned him to a terrible punishment, because he held his tongue, and would not accuse the real delinquent, who was none other than she, Fleurette. She sighed, and her eyes now were full of tears, while old Louise, stolidly, and with much grumbling, got some water and proceeded to wash her face and hands and to tidy her dress.

===Chinese===
渐渐地，她的声调越来越低沉，那股兴奋劲儿都熄了。她看见阿梅代在监狱里，手腕脚踝上箍着铁镣；要不然，就是立在铁面的法官们面前，听他们判下可怕的刑罚——因为他咬定了不开口，不肯指认真正的罪人，而那罪人不是别人，正是她弗勒蕾特。她叹了口气，眼睛里这会儿噙满了泪水；老路易丝呢，闷头闷脑，一面嘟囔个不住，一面打了水来，动手洗脸洗手，拾掇衣裳。

===Original===
“Come, child,” she said dryly after a while, “we’ll go down now and get something to eat.”

===Chinese===
「来吧，孩子，」过了半晌，她干巴巴地说，「咱们这就下去，弄点吃的。」

===Original===
She had never ceased to protest against the madness of this adventure, prophesying every kind of calamity for them both: but Fleurette with the quiet obstinacy of the habitually meek had persisted. She had begun by wheedling the money out of Louise, then obtained the passes for places in the *coche*. Once on the way, it would of course have been ridiculous to turn tail and go back, and Louise, led unconsciously by a force of will stronger than her own, had found herself meekly acquiescing in all the arrangements which Fleurette made on the way. As a matter of fact she had not ceased to marvel at the child. Here was this young thing, who had never travelled in a *coche* before, who had never in her life been further than Serres and Sisteron, calmly undertaking a three days’ journey, sleeping and eating in strange inns, and arriving at her destination unscathed. There certainly was a miracle in all this good luck, for old Louise had heard many a tale of what terrible adventures usually befell unprotected females upon the high roads. What she did not realize was that the miracle merely consisted in the fact that in these outlying corners of beautiful France, in Dauphiné and in Provence, there was still plenty of the good old kindly stock left, some of the chivalry, the warmth of heart, the bonhomie, which all the tyranny and the cruelty perpetrated in the great cities had not contrived to kill; and that there was something in Fleurette’s beauty, her simplicity as well as her determination, which brought forth that chivalry and bonhomie and helped her to win through.

===Chinese===
她自始至终没有停止过抗议这桩疯癫的冒险，预言她们两个会遭上种种大祸；可弗勒蕾特凭着素性温顺者那种不声不响的执拗，硬是坚持定了。她先是连哄带磨，从路易丝那儿磨出了盘缠，接着又弄到了乘乡村公共马车的通行证。一旦上了路，再打退堂鼓折回去，那当然就可笑啦；路易丝不知不觉被一股比她自己的意志更坚强的意志牵着走，发觉自己竟乖乖认可了弗勒蕾特路上作出的一切安排。其实呢，她一直没有不为这孩子暗暗称奇。瞧这小小的人儿，从前从没坐过乡村公共马车出门，一辈子走得最远也不过塞尔和锡斯特龙，如今竟安安稳稳担起三天的旅程，在陌生的客栈里吃住歇脚，完好无损地到了目的地。这份好运里头准定藏着个奇迹，因为老路易丝听过多多少少故事，讲的是没人护送的妇女在大路上通常会遭怎样可怕的变故。她没有参透的是：这奇迹无非在于，在美丽的法兰西这些偏远的角落，在多菲内，在普罗旺斯，古道热肠的老派好人还有的是，还剩下几分侠义、几分热心肠、几分敦厚和气——大城市里干下的种种专横暴虐，都没能把它们弄死；也在于弗勒蕾特的美貌、她的单纯同她的决断里，自有一点什么，能把那份侠义与和气引出来，助着她一路闯了过来。

===Original===
When the two women returned to the parlour where hot milk and country bread awaited them, they were met by a young soldier, who very politely and deferentially claimed acquaintance with them.

===Chinese===
两位妇人回到客厅，热牛奶和乡村面包正在那里等着她们；一位年轻的士兵迎上前来，客客气气、恭恭敬敬地同她们攀谈起来。

===Original===
“You would not remember me, citizeness,” he said more particularly addressing Fleurette, “but I and some very weary soldiers under my command, are deeply indebted to you for your kindness to us, when, like a good Samaritan, you gave us food and drink, on the bridge near your home. Do you remember?”

===Chinese===
「您怕是记不得我了，女公民，」他说，这话主要是冲着弗勒蕾特讲的，「可我，还有我手下那些疲乏不堪的士兵，都深深感念您待我们的好意——那回您像一位善心的撒玛利亚人，在您家近旁的桥上，给了我们吃的喝的。您还记得吗？」

===Original===
He looked very bedraggled and out-at-elbows, but frank and kind. Fleurette raised shy, blue eyes up to his, and gave a little gasp of recognition. She well remembered the soldier. She remembered how sorry she had been for them all, in their shabby clothes and stockingless feet, weary and thirsty, and how she had sent Adèle out to them with food and drink. She also remembered, though she would not remind him of that, that he had been very curt and uncivil with her, had made a sneering remark when she told him that she was Citizen Armand’s daughter, and also that the men under his command had been positively cruel to a poor inoffensive old man whom she afterwards befriended.

===Chinese===
他看去相当邋遢，肘头磨穿，人却坦率和善。弗勒蕾特羞怯地抬起蓝眼睛望着他的眼睛，认出他来，轻轻倒抽了一口气。她清清楚楚记得这个士兵。她记得自己当时多么替他们一伙人难过——衣衫褴褛，脚上连袜子也没有，又累又渴；也记得她是怎样打发阿黛尔给他们送吃送喝去。她也记得——虽说她不愿向他提起——他当时对她多么粗鲁无礼，她告诉他自己是公民阿尔芒的女儿，他还冷笑着挖苦了一句；还有，他手下的兵对一个可怜无害的老头儿着实狠得过分，那老头儿，她后来还照应过来着。

===Original===
However, for the moment, she was perhaps conscious of a slight feeling of relief at sight of a familiar face; she had seen nothing but strangers ever since she left Laragne four days ago. So when the soldier, still speaking quite deferentially, reiterated his: “Do you remember?” she replied simply: “Of course I do, citizen lieutenant.” Which goes to prove that Fleurette had learned a great deal in the past three days, and the word “citizen” now came quite glibly to her tongue.

===Chinese===
不过，眼下见到一张熟面孔，她心里也许倒觉出一点宽慰；打四天前离开拉拉涅，她眼里见到的尽是生人。于是，那士兵依旧恭恭敬敬地又把那句「您还记得吗」重问了一遍，她便简单地答道：「当然记得，公民中尉。」这足以证明，弗勒蕾特这三天里长了许多见识，如今「公民」这个词到了她嘴边，已经说得怪顺溜了。

===Original===
Lieutenant Godet had told her his name, told her that he was a native of Orange and was home on leave for a few days.

===Chinese===
戈岱中尉早已通名报姓，讲过自己是奥朗日本地人，这回是回家休几天假。

===Original===
“A real piece of luck,” he went on lightly, “seeing that perhaps I might be of service to you.”

===Chinese===
「真是一桩走运的事，」他轻快地接着说，「兴许我正好能替二位效劳呢。」

===Original===
The two women sat down at the table and he helped to wait on them, brought them bread and cheese and a jug of hot milk, and bustled the maid of the inn if the latter appeared negligent. He made himself very agreeable to Louise, talked of his own journey, and inquired after her adventures. Louise, despite her innate suspicion of soldiers, gradually unbent to him. The warm food further put her into a good temper, and presently the three of them were conversing in the most amicable manner.

===Chinese===
两位妇人在桌边坐定，他帮着侍候，给她们端来面包、干酪和一壶热牛奶，那客栈女仆稍有怠慢，他便催促个不停。他在路易丝跟前极力显得和气，谈了自己一路的行程，又问起她们的遭遇。路易丝尽管对大兵怀着天生的疑心，也渐渐对他缓和下来。热乎乎的饭食更叫她心里舒坦，不多时，三个人已经谈得再融洽不过了。

===Original===
When the meal had been duly paid for, the soldier once more offered his services. Could he pilot the citizenesses through the town?

===Chinese===
一顿饭按数付清了账，那士兵便再度献起殷勤来。他可不可以领两位女公民在城里逛逛？

===Original===
“Well yes, you can,” Louise said resolutely, “we want to find M’sieu’ Armand.”

===Chinese===
「嗯，行，」路易丝斩钉截铁地说，「我们要找阿尔芒先生。」

===Original===
“Citizen Armand,” Fleurette broke in, “my father. I think you know him, citizen lieutenant.”

===Chinese===
「公民阿尔芒，」弗勒蕾特插嘴道，「是我父亲。我想您认得他，公民中尉。」

===Original===
“Know him?” he exclaimed, “of course I know Armand. Who does not know Citizen Armand in Orange?”

===Chinese===
「认得？」他嚷道，「阿尔芒我当然认得。在奥朗日，谁不认得公民阿尔芒？」

===Original===
“Then he is here now?” Fleurette cried eagerly.

===Chinese===
「那么他眼下就在这儿？」弗勒蕾特急切地喊道。

===Original===
“Of a surety he is.”

===Chinese===
「千真万确，他在。」

===Original===
“You know where he lodges?”

===Chinese===
「您知道他落脚在哪儿？」

===Original===
“Everyone in Orange knows where Citizen Armand lodges.”

===Chinese===
「奥朗日人人都知道公民阿尔芒落脚在哪儿。」

===Original===
“Then you can take me to him?”

===Chinese===
「那您能带我去见他？」

===Original===
“At your service, citizeness.”

===Chinese===
「听候吩咐，女公民。」

===Original===
“Now?”

===Chinese===
「这就去？」

===Original===
“When you wish.”

===Chinese===
「但凭您什么时候。」

===Original===
With a little cry of delight Fleurette gathered up her cloak.

===Chinese===
弗勒蕾特欢喜地轻呼一声，拢起了她的斗篷。

===Original===
“Let us go,” she said simply.

===Chinese===
「走吧。」她简单地说。

===Original===
Louise sighing, but stolid, followed meekly. The thought that she would soon relinquish her wayward charge into the keeping of M’sieu’ Armand was a comforting one; Fleurette was tripping it gaily beside the soldier, but the latter’s dirty clothes and bedraggled appearance still filled old Louise with mistrust.

===Chinese===
路易丝叹着气，却闷头不响，乖乖地跟了上去。一想到她很快就能把这个任性的孩子交到阿尔芒先生手里照管，心里便是一宽；弗勒蕾特在那士兵身边走得轻盈欢快，可那人一身脏衣裳、一副邋遢相，照旧叫老路易丝满心不放心。

===Original===
They crossed the river by the old bridge and then trudged along the dusty streets to a great open place, now called Place de la République. The soldier led the way across the square to a tall stone building, flanked by a square tower, to which a flight of steps gave access. He seemed to know his way about. At the top of the steps a couple of soldiers in somewhat tidier uniforms than his own, were on guard. They stood in what Louise, who had old-fashioned notions as to the behaviour of soldiers on duty, put down as a slouchy and disrespectful attitude. When Lieutenant Godet walked past them, they did not salute. This want of respect of the soldier for his officer was another manifestation, it seems, of Equality and Fraternity. Louise, with her nose in the air, sailed past in the wake of Godet and Fleurette.

===Chinese===
她们从老桥上过了河，又拖着脚步，走过一条条尘土飞扬的街道，来到一片开阔的大广场——如今叫作共和国广场（Place de la République）。那士兵领着路穿过广场，走向一座高大的石头房子，旁边傍着一座方塔，一道台阶直通上去。这地方他似乎熟门熟路。台阶顶上，两个当岗的士兵，军服比他自己那身略齐整些。照老路易丝那套关于士兵当值仪态的老派讲究，他们那副站相，只能算作吊儿郎当、没上没下。戈岱中尉从他们跟前走过，他们也不敬礼。士兵对军官的这般不敬，看来也正是平等与博爱的又一番表现。路易丝鼻孔朝天，尾随着戈岱和弗勒蕾特，昂然走了过去。

===Original===
After crossing a wide vestibule and turning on the right into a long paved corridor, Lieutenant Godet came to a halt before a door which bore the legend: “Committee of Public Safety, Section III.” Beside the door another soldier, also in very shabby uniform, stood leaning upon his bayonet. Fleurette, overawed by the vastness and silence of the place, gazed with vague terror at this man who without uttering a word had put his bayonet athwart the door and held it there, barring the way, motionless as a statue. Lieutenant Godet then spoke to him:

===Chinese===
穿过一道宽敞的门厅，向右拐进一条长长的石铺走廊，戈岱中尉在一扇门前站住了，门上题着一行字：「公安委员会，第三区（Section III）」。门旁另有一个士兵，也是一身极破旧的军服，倚着刺刀站在那里。弗勒蕾特被这地方的宏大与寂静慑住了，怀着一种茫茫的恐惧，望着这个人——他一言不发，把刺刀往门前一横，就那么擎住，拦住去路，纹丝不动，像一尊雕像。于是戈岱中尉开口对他说：

===Original===
“The citizeness,” he said, “is the daughter of Citizen Chauvelin. She desires to speak with him!”

===Chinese===
「这位女公民，」他说，「是公民肖维兰的女儿。她有话要同他讲！」

===Original===
The daughter of Citizen Chauvelin? What did the man mean? Fleurette, puzzled and frowning, pulled him by the sleeve. She was the daughter of Citizen Armand: she’d never heard the name of Chauvelin before. Nevertheless the soldier on guard lowered his bayonet. Godet pushed open the door and the next moment Fleurette found herself facing a large desk which was covered with papers, and behind which Bibi was sitting, writing. A voice said loudly:

===Chinese===
公民肖维兰的女儿？这个人说的是什么意思？弗勒蕾特又困惑又蹙眉，扯了扯他的衣袖。她是公民阿尔芒的女儿：肖维兰这个名字，她生平从来没有听见过。话虽如此，守门的士兵还是垂下了刺刀。戈岱推开门，下一刻，弗勒蕾特发觉自己正面对着一张宽大的书桌，桌上铺满文件，比比就坐在桌子后面写字。一个声音高声说道：

===Original===
“Citizen Chauvelin, here’s your daughter come to see you.”

===Chinese===
「公民肖维兰，您的女儿来看您了。」

===Original===
Whereupon Bibi raised his head and looked at her, staring as if he had seen a ghost.

===Chinese===
比比闻声抬起头来望着她，直勾勾地瞪着，仿佛见了鬼一般。

===Original===
Forgetting everything save the joy of seeing chéri Bibi at last, Fleurette gave a glad little cry, ran round the table, and came to halt on her knees beside Bibi’s chair, with her arms round his neck.

===Chinese===
把什么都忘了个干净，只剩终于见到亲爱的比比的欢喜，弗勒蕾特快乐地轻呼一声，绕过桌子，跪倒在比比的椅子旁边停住，双臂搂住了他的脖子。

===Original===
She felt so glad, so glad, that she was ready to cry.

===Chinese===
她那么欢喜，那么欢喜，欢喜得简直要哭了。

===Original===
“Bibi,” she said softly, whispering in his ear, “chéri Bibi, are you not glad to see me?”

===Chinese===
「比比，」她柔声说，凑在他耳边低语，「亲爱的比比，见到了我，你不欢喜吗？」

## XIX / 第十九章

===Original===
At sight of Fleurette, Chauvelin had stared as if he had seen a ghost. He did not trust his eyes: they were obviously playing him a trick. It was only a second or two later that he realized it was indeed the child, come, heaven only knew why and how, but here in this awful city where treachery, hatred and cruelty were holding sway under his own command.

===Chinese===
一见到弗勒蕾特，肖维兰便瞪直了眼，仿佛见了鬼。他不信自己的眼睛：这双眼分明是在捉弄他。过了一两秒钟他才明白过来，这果真是这孩子，天知道她为什么来、又怎么来的，总之是到了这座可怕的城市——在这城里，背信、仇恨与残忍，正挟着他自己的号令横行霸道。

===Original===
Half-dazed, he yielded to the caresses of this one being in the whole wide world whom his tigerish heart had ever loved. His arms closed round her beloved form, whose sweet breath as of thyme and violets filled his soul with joy. Then, looking up he saw Louise standing there: silent, stolid, mutely accusing, and he asked roughly:

===Chinese===
他半晕半醒，听任那爱抚摆布——茫茫尘世之间，他那颗虎狼之心爱过的，唯有这一个人。他的双臂环住那具心爱的身躯，她呼吸间百里香与紫罗兰般的甜美气息，把欢喜灌满了他的灵魂。随后他抬起眼来，看见路易丝站在那里：不作声，闷头闷脑，无言地含着责备。他粗声粗气地问：

===Original===
“How the hell did you both get here?”

===Chinese===
「你们两个到底是怎么到这儿来的，见鬼！」

===Original===
Louise shrugged her shoulders.

===Chinese===
路易丝耸了耸肩膀。

===Original===
“By the *coche*,” she said, “from Sisteron.”

===Chinese===
「坐乡村公共马车（*coche*）来的，」她说，「打锡斯特龙动身。」

===Original===
“I know,” he rejoined. “But why did you come?”

===Chinese===
「这我知道，」他应道。「可你们为什么要来？」

===Original===
“Ask her,” Louise replied curtly. “She would come. I could not let her travel alone.”

===Chinese===
「问她去，」路易丝抢白道。「她非要来。我又不能叫她一个人上路。」

===Original===
Bibi’s two hands were clasped round Fleurette’s head, his fingers were buried in her hair: he pressed that dearly beloved head closer and closer to his breast; joy at sight of her had already given place to terror. What was the child doing here? How and why had she come? He had kept her so completely aloof from his real life, that it seemed to him that some awful cataclysm must have occurred over in that peaceful home in Dauphiné, else she were not here.

===Chinese===
比比的双手捧着弗勒蕾特的头，手指埋进她的发间：他把那颗心爱的头颅越搂越紧，贴向自己的胸膛；见到她的欢喜，早已让位给了恐惧。这孩子到这儿来做什么？她怎么来的，又是为什么来？他一向把她同自己的真实生活隔得那样彻底，以致在他看来，多菲内那处安宁的家里准是出了什么可怕的大变故，不然她是不会到这儿来的。

===Original===
His pale, restless eyes searched Louise’s impassive face:

===Chinese===
他那双惨白的、游移不定的眼睛，在路易丝那张不动声色的脸上探来探去：

===Original===
“Who brought you here?” he reiterated roughly.

===Chinese===
「是谁把你们带到这儿来的？」他又粗声问了一遍。

===Original===
“An officer in a draggle-tailed uniform,” Louise replied, still speaking curtly, whilst with a glance that was distinctly hostile her eyes swept round the room. “I thought,” she added, “that he followed us into the room.”

===Chinese===
「一个穿着邋遢军服的军官，」路易丝答道，说话照旧那么冲，一面用分明含着敌意的目光扫过整间屋子。「我还当，」她补了一句，「他跟在我们身后进了这间屋子呢。」

===Original===
“What was he like?”

===Chinese===
「他生得什么模样？」

===Original===
She described him as closely as she could, and then added: “I don’t remember his name.”

===Chinese===
她竭力把他的模样细细描述了一番，然后添了一句：「他的名字我不记得了。」

===Original===
She too had heard the name “Chauvelin” spoken by the soldier and for a moment had pondered. Marvelled. In her downright peasant mind, vague doubts, doubts that were eighteen years old now, turned to more definite suspicions. She knew well enough that some kind of mystery hung around the personality of Fleurette’s father; she knew for instance that he was really a wealthy and highborn gentleman; but eighteen years ago, in the days of the old regime, the fact that a highborn gentleman chose to hide a love-romance from the eyes of his equally highborn friends was not an infrequent occurrence. If at any time during the past eighteen years she had learned that M’sieu’ Armand was really a great Duke or Prince or Ambassador, she would have been neither surprised nor suspicious. But Chauvelin!!! For the past three years whenever rumours of cruelty or ruthless persecution of innocent men and women had penetrated to these distant corners of Dauphiné or Provence, the name of Armand Chauvelin had stood out as the protagonist of these terrible tragedies; people spoke of Danton the lion of the revolution, and also of Marat its tiger, of Robespierre and of Chauvelin.

===Chinese===
她也听见了那士兵口中说出的「肖维兰」这个名字，怔了片刻，又暗自惊叹。在她那直来直去的农妇心里，那些模模糊糊的疑团——如今已积了十八年的疑团——变成了较为明确的猜疑。她心里很清楚，弗勒蕾特的父亲这个人的身上笼罩着某种神秘；譬如说，她知道他其实是一位有钱的出身高贵的绅士；可是在十八年前，在旧制度的岁月里，一位出身高贵的绅士偏要把自己的一段风流情事，瞒住一班同样出身高贵的朋友，这原不是什么稀罕事。过去这十八年里，不论什么时候，倘若她得知阿尔芒先生其实是一位大公爵、亲王或是大使，她本不会吃惊，也不会起疑。可是肖维兰！！！过去三年里，每逢种种残酷与无情迫害无辜男女的传闻传进多菲内或普罗旺斯这些遥远的角落，阿尔芒·肖维兰这个名字，总是赫然现作这一出出可怕惨剧的主角；人们谈起革命的雄狮丹东，也谈起革命的猛虎马拉，谈起罗伯斯庇尔，也谈起肖维兰。

===Original===
Chauvelin!!!

===Chinese===
肖维兰！！！

===Original===
And he, meeting her glance, understood what went on in her mind. As to this he was indifferent. What Louise thought of him was less than nothing. It was the child that mattered now: the child who clung to him quivering with excitement. The terror in his heart grew in intensity: it gripped him till he felt physically sick. The mad dogs of hatred and cruelty, which he himself had helped to unchain, seemed to be snarling at him and threatening his Fleurette. With a hand that trembled visibly, he stroked the pretty golden hair.

===Chinese===
而他，迎着她的目光，也明白她心里转的是什么念头。这个他倒满不在乎。路易丝怎么看他，比什么都不如。眼下要紧的是这孩子：这孩子正偎着他，激动得浑身发颤。他心里的恐惧一阵深似一阵：它攫住他，攥得他五脏翻腾直欲作呕。那一群仇恨与残忍的疯狗——他自己就曾帮着解开锁链——此刻仿佛正朝他狺狺狂吠，威逼着他的弗勒蕾特。他伸出一只肉眼可见地发抖的手，抚着那头漂亮的金发。

===Original===
“Now, little one,” he said, steadying his voice as much as he could, “are you going to tell me why you’ve come?”

===Chinese===
「好了，小宝贝，」他说，竭力稳住自己的嗓音，「你肯告诉我，你为什么来了吗？」

===Original===
Fleurette struggled to her feet. Self-possessed she stood before her father and said firmly:

===Chinese===
弗勒蕾特撑着站起身来。她镇定自若地立在父亲面前，语气坚定地说：

===Original===
“Chéri Bibi, I came in order to right a great wrong. I believe that you are strong and powerful and that you will help me to see justice done. That is why I came to you.”

===Chinese===
「亲爱的比比，我来，是为了昭雪一桩大大的冤屈。我相信你又强大、又有力量，会帮我看到正义得到伸张。我就是为这个才来找你的。」

===Original===
He frowned, more puzzled then before, angered with himself for being so dull-witted, for not making a guess at what had brought the child along. His mind just before she came had been so completely absorbed in the latest adventure of his arch-enemy the Scarlet Pimpernel, that the presence of Fleurette, here and now, had been for him like a sudden stunning blow on the head. He felt dazed and stupid: unable to turn his thoughts into this fresh channel.

===Chinese===
他皱起眉头，比先前更加摸不着头脑，又恼恨自己这般不开窍，竟猜不出是什么把这孩子引了来。就在她进来之前，他的心思还整个儿沉浸在那位宿敌猩红繁笺花最新的一场历险里，因此弗勒蕾特此时此地的出现，对他来说就像当头挨了一记打懵人的闷棍。他只觉又昏又笨：怎么也没法把心思转到这条新的道儿上来。

===Original===
“Fleurette, my darling,” he pleaded, “try and tell me more clearly. I don’t understand. What do you mean by righting a wrong? What wrong?”

===Chinese===
「弗勒蕾特，我的宝贝，」他恳求道，「想法子说得更清楚些。我不明白。你说昭雪冤屈，是什么意思？什么冤屈？」

===Original===
“Why,” she replied simply, “the arrest of M’sieu’ Amédé for a crime which he did not commit.”

===Chinese===
「嗐，」她简单地答道，「就是阿梅代先生蒙冤被捕的事，那桩罪他压根儿没有犯。」

===Original===
“You knew M’sieu’ Amédé had been arrested?” she insisted.

===Chinese===
「您知道阿梅代先生被捕了吗？」她追问。

===Original===
Yes, he knew that. The mock arrest of young Colombe was one of the tricks played on that fool Godet by those impudent English spies. But what had Fleurette’s presence here to do with that?

===Chinese===
是的，他知道。小科隆布那场假逮捕，正是那伙胆大包天的英国间谍耍弄戈岱那个蠢货的把戏之一。可弗勒蕾特人到这儿来，同那桩事又有什么相干？

===Original===
She was trying to explain.

===Chinese===
她正试着解释呢。

===Original===
“Then you know, chéri Bibi,” she was saying in that sweet eager way of hers, “that some valuables belonging to Madame over at the château were found in the shed behind M’sieu’ Colombe’s shop?”

===Chinese===
「那么你是知道的，亲爱的比比，」她正用她那又甜又急的口吻说着，「有人把庄园那边夫人的几件值钱物件，在科隆布先生铺子后头的工具棚里搜出来了？」

===Original===
Yes, he knew that too. But what had she﻿ ﻿… ?

===Chinese===
是的，这个他也知道。可是她这是……？

===Original===
“And that the soldiers accused M’sieu’ Amédé of having stolen them?”

===Chinese===
「还知道那些士兵指控阿梅代先生偷了这些东西？」

===Original===
A sigh of relief escaped him. He was beginning to understand. Nothing to worry about apparently. Indeed he might have guessed. The child had come to plead for that young fool Amédé, and﻿—

===Chinese===
一声宽慰的叹息从他胸中逸出。他开始明白了。看样子没有什么好担心的。其实他本该猜到的。这孩子是来替阿梅代那个小傻瓜求情的，于是——

===Original===
“And what I had come to tell you, chéri Bibi,” she went on glibly, “is that it is not Amédé who stole the things belonging to Madame.”

===Chinese===
「而我赶来要告诉你的，亲爱的比比，」她顺顺溜溜地往下说，「就是：偷了夫人那些东西的，不是阿梅代。」

===Original===
She paused for a second or two. What she was about to say required courage: and how Bibi would take it she did not know. But Fleurette had come all the way from Lou Mas, had journeyed three days, so that Bibi might right a great wrong, as only he could do, and, once more sinking on her knees beside her father’s chair, she added in a clear voice, rendered somewhat shrill with excitement:

===Chinese===
她停顿了一两秒。她将要说出口的话需要勇气：比比会怎么受它，她无从知道。可弗勒蕾特已经从庐庄一路赶来，走了三天的旅程，为的就是让比比昭雪一桩大冤屈——这件事，天底下只有他办得到。她重又跪倒在父亲的椅子旁，用一副清亮的、激动得有些发尖的嗓音添了一句：

===Original===
“I stole the valuables out of Madame’s room, chéri Bibi.”

===Chinese===
「是我把夫人房里的值钱物件偷出来的，亲爱的比比。」

===Original===
With a hoarse cry he clapped his hand against her mouth. My God, if someone had heard! The guard outside, or one of these innumerable spies whom he himself had set in motion, and whose ears were trained to penetrate through the most solid walls.

===Chinese===
他嘶哑地喊了一声，一巴掌捂住她的嘴。我的天主，要是叫人听了去呢！外头那个哨兵，或是他自己撒出去的那无数密探里的哪一个——那些耳朵，个个都训练得能穿透最厚的墙壁。

===Original===
His pale eyes in which now lurked a kind of vague terror, wandered furtively round the room, whilst Louise, equally horrified and frightened, exclaimed almost involuntarily:

===Chinese===
他那双惨白的眼睛里此刻潜着一种茫然的恐怖，贼溜溜地在屋里四下逡巡；路易丝也同样又惊又怕，几乎身不由己地喊出声来：

===Original===
“The child is mad, M’sieu’, do not listen to her.”

===Chinese===
「这孩子疯了，先生，别听她的。」

===Original===
Fleurette alone remained self-possessed: she was still on her knees, but at Bibi’s rough gesture she had fallen back steadying herself with one hand against the floor. Slowly, noiselessly, Chauvelin had risen and tiptoed across the room, Louise, wide-eyed and scared, following his every movement. They were furtive like those of a cat on the prowl, and his face was the colour of ashes. He went to the door and abruptly pulled it open. Outside the soldier on guard was quietly chatting with Lieutenant Godet; at sight of Citizen Chauvelin they stood at attention and saluted.

===Chinese===
唯独弗勒蕾特依然镇定自若：她还跪在那里，只是比比那粗鲁的一捂把她推得向后一仰，一只手撑住了地板。肖维兰慢悠悠、无声无息地站起身，踮着脚尖穿过屋子，路易丝睁大眼睛、心惊胆战地盯着他的一举一动。那些动作鬼鬼祟祟，活像一只潜行觅食的猫；他的脸则成了死灰的颜色。他走到门边，猛地把门拉开。门外，当值的士兵正同戈岱中尉轻声闲聊；一见到公民肖维兰，两人倏地立正敬礼。

===Original===
“Go and tell Captain Moisson over at the barracks,” Chauvelin said curtly, addressing Godet, “that I shall want to see him here at two o’clock.”

===Chinese===
「你去告诉营房那边的穆瓦松上尉（Captain Moisson），」肖维兰冲着戈岱简短地说，「我要今天两点钟在这儿见他。」

===Original===
“Very good, citizen.”

===Chinese===
「是，公民。」

===Original===
Godet saluted again and turned on his heel. Chauvelin looked at him closely, but his face was expressionless. He watched him for a moment or two as he, Godet, strode along the corridor. Then he closed the door and went back to his seat behind the table.

===Chinese===
戈岱又敬了个礼，转身就走。肖维兰细细地打量他，他却面无表情。他目送着戈岱沿走廊大步走去，望了片刻。然后他关上门，回到桌子后面自己的座位上。

===Original===
He had made an almost superhuman effort to regain his composure. He wanted to hear more, and did not want to scare the child. The sight of Godet standing outside the door talking to the man on guard, had made him physically sick, raised that same terror in his heart which his presence and his glance were wont to raise in others. The expression of his face must at one moment have been absolutely terrifying, for Fleurette could hardly bear to look at him; but when he sat down again his face was just like a mask, waxen and grey. He turned to her, and rested his elbow on the table, shading his eyes with his long, thin hand. And Fleurette felt how dreadful it must be for him to think that his daughter was a thief.

===Chinese===
他费了近乎超人的努力，才重新敛住神。他还想多听一些，又不想吓着孩子。方才望见戈岱站在门外同守门的说话，他险些儿呕出来，心里升起的那股恐怖，正是他的在场、他的目光素来惯于在别人心里激起的那一股。有一瞬间，他脸上的表情一定可怕到了极点，因为弗勒蕾特几乎不敢朝他看；可等他重新坐下，他的脸已像一副面具，蜡也似地灰白。他转向她，把肘支在桌上，用那只又长又瘦的手遮着眼睛。弗勒蕾特感觉得到，对他来说，一想到自己的女儿是个贼，该是多么可怕的一件事。

===Original===
So before he had time to ask her any questions she embarked on glib explanations.

===Chinese===
于是不等他来得及发问，她便滔滔不绝地解释起来。

===Original===
“You must not think, chéri Bibi,” she said, “that I stole those things for a bad motive. I did it because﻿—”

===Chinese===
「你可不要以为，亲爱的比比，」她说，「我是存着坏心眼才偷那些东西的。我那么做，是因为——」

===Original===
She checked herself, and went on after a second or two:

===Chinese===
她把话收住，过了一两秒又接着说：

===Original===
“You remember, chéri Bibi, that evening at the château when we met, you and I, by the stable door?”

===Chinese===
「你记得吗，亲爱的比比，那天傍晚在庄园里，你和我，咱俩在马厩门边碰上的那一回？」

===Original===
Yes, he remembered. “But speak softly, child! these walls have ears!”

===Chinese===
是的，他记得。「可是说话轻声些，孩子！这些墙壁长着耳朵呢！」

===Original===
“I had taken the things out of Madame’s room then,” Fleurette continued speaking in an agitated whisper, “and hidden them under my shawl.” She gave a nervous little laugh: “Oh! I was terrified I can tell you,” she said, “that you would notice.”

===Chinese===
「那时候，我已经把东西从夫人房里拿出来了，」弗勒蕾特接着说，声音激动得成了耳语，「掖在了我的披巾底下。」她紧张地轻笑了一声：「哦！我跟你说吧，我怕得要命，」她说，「就怕你瞧出来。」

===Original===
He had his nerves under control by now. His mind keen, active, was concentrated on her story, his indomitable will was slowly mastering his terror. What had he to fear? Godet was out of the way, and the child’s whispers could not be heard outside these four walls. If only that fool Louise did not look so scared: the sight of her face, open-mouthed and with big, round eyes, got on his nerves. He tried not to look her way. While his glance was fixed on Fleurette he felt that he could think of her, scheme for her and above all protect her﻿—he, so important in the councils of State. So powerful. He could shield her even against the consequences of her own folly.

===Chinese===
到这时，他的神经已经稳住了。他的头脑又敏锐、又活跃，全神贯注在她的讲述上；他那百折不回的意志，正慢慢降服他的恐怖。他有什么可怕的呢？戈岱已经支开了，孩子的耳语出了这四面墙壁便听不见。只恨路易丝那蠢货，偏要摆出那么一副吓破了胆的脸：她那副张着嘴、瞪着圆圆大眼的模样，搅得他心烦意乱。他竭力不去朝她那边看。只要目光定在弗勒蕾特身上，他便觉得自己能为她思虑，为她筹划，尤其能保护她——他，这位在国务枢机里何等举足轻重的人物，何等有权有势。哪怕是她自己一时糊涂闯下的祸，他也能替她遮挡过去。

===Original===
Of course, he must make light of the whole affair. Oh! above all make light of it. The child was silly, wilful and ignorant, but he would know how to protect her, and how to make her hold her tongue. Louise was a fool, but she was safe and these walls were solid, there was really no cause for this insane terror which had turned him giddy and faint, and at first paralysed his brain.

===Chinese===
当然，他必须把整件事轻轻放过去。哦！尤其要轻描淡写。这孩子又傻气、又任性、又不懂事，可他会懂得怎样保护她，怎样叫她守口如瓶。路易丝是个蠢货，但她靠得住；这些墙壁也是实打实的，实在没有什么缘由，要这样丧魂落魄地恐惧一场——那恐怖方才弄得他头晕目眩、脚下发虚，起先还麻透了他的脑子。

===Original===
So he forced his quaking voice into tones of gentle banter, forced himself to smile, to tweak her cheek and to look gaily, almost incredulously into her eyes.

===Chinese===
于是他强压住发颤的嗓音，换成温柔打趣的声口，强逼着自己微笑起来，拧了拧她的脸颊，快快活活、几乎带着难以置信的神气，望进她的眼睛里。

===Original===
“*Allons, allons*,” he said lightly, “what story is this? My little Fleurette taking things that belong to others? I won’t believe it.”

===Chinese===
「得啦，得啦（*Allons, allons*），」他轻快地说，「这是说的什么故事？我的小弗勒蕾特，拿别人家的东西？我可不信。」

===Original===
“Only *pour le bon motif*, chéri Bibi,” she insisted; “because you see the soldiers were at the château, and they were ruining and stealing everything they could lay their hands on.﻿ ﻿… And also because﻿—”

===Chinese===
「无非是出自好心的缘故（*pour le bon motif*），亲爱的比比，」她坚持道；「因为你瞧，那些士兵当时就在庄园里，凡是他们能沾手的，都在糟蹋、都在偷……还因为——」

===Original===
Once more she checked herself, loath to give away that one cherished little secret: The mysterious voice at which perhaps Bibi would scoff. But she did tell Bibi how with the precious burden under her shawl she had hurried homewards until, fearing that she would be overtaken by the soldiers on the road, she had sought refuge in the widow Tronchet’s cottage. She told him how she had watched him riding past, heading towards Lou Mas, and how she had become scared lest, if he spent the night at home, he would find out what it was that she was keeping so carefully hidden underneath her shawl.

===Chinese===
她又把话收住了，舍不得泄露那一点珍藏着的小秘密：那个神秘的声音——比比说不定要笑话它。不过她到底还是告诉了比比：她怎样把那件宝贵的负担掩在披巾底下，一路急急往家赶，直到生怕在路上被士兵们赶上，才躲进特龙谢寡妇的小屋避难。她告诉他，她怎样望着他骑马经过，朝庐庄去；又怎样害怕起来，生怕他若在家里过夜，就会发现她那么当心掩在披巾底下的，究竟是些什么。

===Original===
And then she told him how she had thought of M’sieu’ Amédé and had asked Adèle to tell him to meet her outside the widow’s cottage, and how she had entrusted him with the precious treasure and he had undertaken to hide it in the shed outside his father’s shop. But how it came to pass that those other soldiers, who were as magnificently dressed as anything Fleurette had seen in all her life, how they had come to suspect M’sieu’ Amédé of the theft, she could not conjecture. All she knew was that M’sieu’ Amédé was innocent and that he must be proclaimed innocent at once. At once.

===Chinese===
接着她又告诉他，她怎样想到了阿梅代先生，怎样托阿黛尔传话，叫他到寡妇的小屋外头同她碰面；又怎样把那件珍贵的宝藏托付给他，他也应承下来，把它藏到他父亲铺子后头的工具棚里。可是另外那一队士兵——穿得那么堂皇体面，弗勒蕾特一辈子没见过那么气派的——究竟怎么会疑心到阿梅代先生头上，说是他偷的，她可就猜不透了。她只知道一件事：阿梅代先生是清白的，必须立刻宣布他清白。立刻。

===Original===
“I stole the things, Bibi,” she concluded, “not for a bad motive, I swear, but I did steal them and gave them to M’sieu’ Amédé to keep. If anyone is to be punished, then it must be I, not he.”

===Chinese===
「东西是我偷的，比比，」她归结道，「我起誓，绝不是存着坏心眼；可我确确实实偷了，还把它们交给阿梅代先生保管。要论谁该受罚，那也该是我，不是他。」

===Original===
She was sitting on her heels, and looking up boldly, and with a little wilful air at her father. Her dear little hands were resting on her knees. She looked adorable. Chauvelin mutely put out his arms and she snuggled into them, pressing her cheek against his breast with a nervous little gesture, twiddling one of the buttons of his coat.

===Chinese===
她跪坐在自己脚跟上，大胆地仰起脸望着父亲，带着一点任性的神气。她那双亲爱的小手搁在膝上。她那模样真叫人疼爱。肖维兰一言不发地伸出双臂，她便偎了进去，带着一点紧张的小动作把脸颊贴上他的胸膛，捻弄着他外衣上的一粒纽扣。

===Original===
Old Louise sitting at the far end of the room had listened, open-mouthed, wide-eyed, to the tale. Her furrowed face was a mirror of all the different expressions with which Chauvelin regarded her from time to time. Terror and slow reassurance. “If that is all, then I can deal with it!” he seemed to be telling her now, when it was all over, and he knew the worst. He held the child very close to him, and there was a certain nervous terror still lurking in his eyes as he buried his face in the soft waves of her hair.

===Chinese===
老路易丝坐在屋子顶里头，张着嘴、瞪着眼，把这番话从头听到了尾。她那张沟壑纵横的脸，成了一面镜子，映出肖维兰不时投向她的种种表情：恐怖，以及慢慢归于宽慰。如今一切都过去了，最坏的他都知道了，他仿佛正在对她说：「要是不过如此，那我就能对付！」他把那孩子紧紧搂在身边，把脸埋进她鬈发柔软的波纹里，眼睛里还潜藏着一丝紧张的恐怖。

===Original===
“Bibi chéri,” Fleurette insisted, “I must find those who are going to sit in judgment on M’sieu’ Amédé. And you will help me find them, won’t you? I must tell them the truth. Mustn’t I?”

===Chinese===
「亲爱的比比，」弗勒蕾特坚持道，「我一定得找到那些将要审判阿梅代先生的人。你会帮我找到他们的，好不好？我必须把真相告诉他们。难道不是吗？」

===Original===
“You shall, child, you shall,” he babbled incoherently. He was trying to steady his voice, so as not to let her know how scared he had been.

===Chinese===
「你会的，孩子，你会的，」他语无伦次地喃喃道。他是在竭力稳住嗓音，好不让她知道他方才多么害怕。

===Original===
“When Adèle told us the next morning about the soldiers having found Madame’s valuables and arrested M’sieu’ Amédé, I knew at once that you would help me to put everything right. So Louise and I just started then and there, as I thought we would find you in Sisteron.”

===Chinese===
「第二天早上，阿黛尔告诉我们，士兵们搜出了夫人的值钱物件，还抓走了阿梅代先生，我当时就知道，你一定会帮我把一切扳正过来。于是我和路易丝当场就动了身，我还以为会在锡斯特龙找到你呢。」

===Original===
“The child told me nothing,” Louise protested in answer to a mute challenge from Chauvelin. “I only thought she wanted to see you in order to plead for young Colombe.”

===Chinese===
「这孩子什么也没跟我说，」路易丝抗声道，回应肖维兰一声无言的诘问。「我只当她是想见你，好替小科隆布求情。」

===Original===
“There is no need,” he said steadily, “for me or anyone else to plead for him. Amédé Colombe is a free man at this hour.”

===Chinese===
「用不着，」他声气平稳地说，「用不着我，也用不着任何旁人替他求情。阿梅代·科隆布此时此刻已经是个自由人了。」

===Original===
Fleurette’s little cry of rapture gave him a short, sharp pang of jealousy.

===Chinese===
弗勒蕾特那一声欢天喜地的小小惊呼，叫他心头挨了一记又短又尖的嫉妒的刺痛。

===Original===
“Do you love him so much as all that, little one?” he asked almost involuntarily.

===Chinese===
「你就这么样地爱他吗，小宝贝？」他几乎不由自主地问。

===Original===
She blushed, and without replying, hung her head. For a second or two he debated within himself whether he would tell her the whole truth, then came to the conclusion that on the whole it would be best that she should know. Doubtless she would hear the story, anyhow, from others, and so he told it her just as he had had it the day before from Lieutenant Godet. The magnificent soldiers who had come that morning into Laragne were not real soldiers of the revolutionary army, they were a band of English spies, whose chief was known throughout France as the Scarlet Pimpernel: a cynical, impudent adventurer whose business it was to incite French men and women to desert their country in the hour of her greatest need, and who doubtless would incite Amédé Colombe to treachery and desertion. It was that chief, no doubt, who had spied on Fleurette and seen her that night hand over Madame’s valuables to Amédé Colombe. He had taken this means of obtaining possession of the valuables, as well as of the persons of the ci-devant Frontenac and Amédé. Both men and money he would use against France, for the English were great enemies of this glorious revolution, the friends of all the aristos and tyrants whom the people were determined to wipe off the face of the earth.

===Chinese===
她红了脸，没有作答，垂下头去。有那么一两秒，他心里掂量着要不要把全部真相告诉她，随后打定主意：总的说来，还是让她知道的好。反正这个故事，她横竖会从旁人嘴里听去。于是他把它讲给她听，就跟前一天从戈岱中尉嘴里听来的一字不差。那天早晨开进拉拉涅的那些军服煌煌的士兵，并不是革命军队的真士兵，而是一伙英国间谍，他们的头目法兰西上下无人不晓，人称猩红繁笺花：一个玩世不恭、胆大妄为的冒险家，干的营生，就是煽动法国的男男女女，在国家最危难的关头背弃自己的祖国；不用说，他还会煽动阿梅代·科隆布去通敌叛逃。准是那个头目盯上了弗勒蕾特的梢，那晚瞧见她把夫人的值钱物件交到阿梅代·科隆布手里。他便用了这个手段，既把值钱物件弄到了手，又把旧贵族（ci-devant）弗朗特纳克和阿梅代两个人抓到了手。人也要，钱也要，他都要拿去同法兰西作对；因为英国人是这场光荣革命的大敌，是一切贵族佬和暴君的朋友——而人民已经拿定了主意，要把这一伙从地面上抹掉。

===Original===
Wide-eyed and dumb, Fleurette listened to him. After the first moment of intense joy, when she heard that Amédé was safe, there had come a sense of exultation that the mysterious voice which had urged her to find Madame’s valuables had spoken with a purpose and that that purpose was now accomplished. Monsieur, Madame and Mademoiselle had all been saved by what she believed was a supernatural agency﻿—whatever Bibi might say. No man who was a mere spy and an enemy of France could have accomplished all that this mysterious being had done, from the moment when disguised as a faggot-carrier he had commanded her to look after Madame’s valuables, until the hour when clad in a magnificent uniform, daring and fearless, he had found such glorious means of saving Amédé and M’sieu’ de Frontenac too, from prison and perhaps death.

===Chinese===
弗勒蕾特睁大了眼睛，哑然无言地听他讲。听到阿梅代安然无恙的最初一刻，她满心是强烈的欢喜；随后便涌起一阵欢腾得意之感：那个催她去找夫人值钱物件的神秘声音，原是怀着目的说话的，而那目的如今已经达成了。先生、夫人和小姐，全都被一种她认定的超自然力量搭救了——不管比比怎么说。一个区区间谍、法兰西的仇敌，断然成全不了这神秘人物所成全的这一切：从那天他假扮背柴老人、吩咐她照看好夫人的值钱物件起，到这一刻他一身堂堂的军服、义胆包天、无所畏惧，寻出这样辉煌的手段，把阿梅代和德·弗朗特纳克先生也都从监狱里、说不定还从死亡里救了出来。

===Original===
And after the joy and the exultation there had crept into Fleurette’s heart a feeling of awe and dread for the father who apparently she had never really known until this hour. She had only known him as kind, indulgent, loving﻿—loving in a kind of fierce way at times, snarling like a wild cat if she thwarted him﻿—but always indulgent and always secretive. Now he seemed to lay his soul bare before her. His love of France, of that revolution which apparently he had helped to make. His hatred of those whom he called traitors and enemies of France, the aristocrats, the men who owned land and property, who had ancestors and family pride, and then the English who were the real enemies, who worked against the people, against democracy, and against liberty, who had harboured every traitor that plotted against France. Bibi hated them all and Fleurette felt awed and chilled thus to hear him speak. He, who was so gentle with her always, now spoke as if he approved of all the cruelty perpetrated against those who did not think as he did, and whom he hated with such passionate intensity.

===Chinese===
欢喜与欢腾过后，弗勒蕾特心里又悄悄爬进一种对父亲的敬畏与惧怕——这个父亲，看来她直到此刻才算真正认识了。她从前只知道他和蔼、纵容、疼她——那疼爱有时带着几分凶狠，她若违拗了他，他会像野猫似的龇牙低吼——可他总是纵容，也总是讳莫如深。如今，他却仿佛把灵魂摊开在她面前了。他对法兰西的爱，对那场看来他曾参与缔造的革命的爱。他对那些被他唤作叛徒、法兰西之敌的人的恨：贵族们，那些有田有地有产业、有祖先有门第之骄的人，还有英国人——那才是真正的敌人，同人民作对、同民主作对、同自由作对，窝藏过每一个密谋反对法兰西的叛徒。比比恨他们所有这些人，弗勒蕾特听他这样讲，只觉又敬畏、又心寒。他，一向待她那么温柔的人，此刻讲起话来，却仿佛对施加在那些同他想法不一、又遭他这般切齿痛恨的人身上的种种暴虐，全然赞同似的。

===Original===
Instinctively, and she hoped imperceptibly, she recoiled from him when he once more tried to clasp her in his arms. This man with the pale eyes and the cruel sneer was not the Bibi she loved. He was just a man whom she feared. All she wanted now was to get away, to get back to Lou Mas. Since Amédé was safe, why should she stay any more in this awful place where even Bibi seemed like a stranger?

===Chinese===
他再度张开双臂要搂她时，她出于本能地——她但愿也是不着痕迹地——朝后躲闪开了。这个眼睛惨白、冷笑残忍的男人，不是她所爱的那个比比。他不过是一个叫她害怕的男人。如今她一心只想走，只想回庐庄去。既然阿梅代已经平安，她何必再在这可怕的地方待下去呢——在这儿，连比比看上去都像个陌生人了。

===Original===
Louise now was standing near her, and Bibi was giving Louise some peremptory orders:

===Chinese===
这时路易丝已站到她身旁，比比正对路易丝发着一道道不容分说的命令：

===Original===
“You will go back now to the Chat Noir,” he said, “the inn where you were this morning. There you will wait quietly until I come to fetch you. We will get on the way as early as we can, so as to get to Vaison before dark.”

===Chinese===
「你这会儿就回黑猫客栈（Chat Noir）去，」他说，「就是你们今天上午待的那家客栈。在那儿安安静静等我出来接你们。咱们要尽早动身，赶在天黑以前到韦松（Vaison）。」

===Original===
“Vaison?” Louise asked perplexed. “But the *coche*﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「韦松？」路易丝困惑地问。「可是乡村公共马车……」

===Original===
“We are not travelling by the public *coche*,” Bibi broke in impatiently. “My private calèche will take us as far as Lou Mas, and I’ll not leave you till I’ve seen you safely home.”

===Chinese===
「咱们不坐公共马车走，」比比不耐烦地打断她。「我的私人篷车（calèche）送咱们一直到庐庄，不亲眼看着你们平平安安到了家，我不会撇下你们。」

===Original===
“A calèche!” Louise exclaimed. “Holy Virgin!”

===Chinese===
「篷车！」路易丝惊呼起来。「我的圣母！」

===Original===
“Silence, woman,” Bibi cried with an oath. “There is no Holy Virgin now.”

===Chinese===
「住口，婆娘，」比比咒骂着喊道。「如今可再没有什么圣母了。」

===Original===
Well! of course, Bibi had said that sort of thing before now, but never in such a rough, almost savage tone. Slowly Fleurette struggled to her feet. All of a sudden she was feeling very, very tired. For four whole days excitement and anxiety had kept her up; but now excitement had died down and dull reaction had set in. A sense of unreality came over her: the voice of Bibi giving all sorts of instructions to Louise came to her muffled as if through a thick veil. All that she knew﻿—and this was comforting﻿—was that soon they would all be starting for home: not in a crowded, jostling old *coche*, but in a calèche. What a wonderful man Bibi was: so grand and powerful and rich, that he had a calèche of his own and could come and go as he pleased. She remembered how deferential the soldiers had been to him that night at the château, and even now her eyes fastened on the beautiful tricolour sash which he wore, the visible sign of his influence and power.

===Chinese===
嗐！当然喽，这类话，比比从前也不是没有说过，可从没用过这样粗野、简直蛮横的口气。弗勒蕾特慢慢撑起身子站了起来。她忽然觉得非常、非常疲倦了。整整四天，兴奋和焦虑一直撑着她；可如今兴奋劲儿退了下去，沉沉的倦怠反涌了上来。一阵恍惚之感攫住了她：比比对路易丝发号施令的嗓音，传到她耳中都发了闷，像隔着一层厚厚的面纱。她所知道的——这一点倒叫人宽慰——只是他们很快就要动身回家了：不坐那辆又挤又撞的旧乡村公共马车，坐篷车。比比是个多么了不起的人啊：这么气派、这么有权、这么有钱，自己有一辆篷车，想上哪儿就上哪儿。她想起那天夜里在庄园里，士兵们对他是多么恭恭敬敬；就是此刻，她的眼睛还盯在他身上那条漂亮的三色绶带上——那是他的势力与权柄的醒目标记。

===Original===
When Bibi finally took her in his arms and kissed her as affectionately, as tenderly as was his wont, she swayed a little when he released her and the things in the room started to go round and round before her eyes. Louise put her strong arm round her and Fleurette heard her say: “Leave her to me, she’ll be all right!” She felt herself being led out of the room, past the sentry at the door, and then along a corridor.

===Chinese===
末了，比比把她搂进怀里，像素来那样亲热、那样温存地吻了她；他松开手时，她晃了一晃，屋里的东西开始在她眼前团团打转。路易丝伸出结实的胳膊搂住她，弗勒蕾特听见她说：「交给我吧，她不要紧的！」她觉出自己被领出了屋子，打门口的哨兵身边经过，又走过一条走廊。

===Original===
When she felt the soft, spring air strike her in the face, she felt revived, and walked steadily beside Louise as far as the inn.

===Chinese===
等她觉出春天柔和的空气拂在脸上，精神便一振，稳稳当当地傍着路易丝，一路走回了那家客栈。

## XX / 第二十章

===Original===
Bibi’s orders to Louise had been to go back to the inn and there to wait until he came in his calèche to take them home to Lou Mas. And the two women ready for the journey home, so tired that only excitement kept them from breaking down, waited for him patiently in the parlour downstairs.

===Chinese===
比比吩咐路易丝先回客栈，在那儿等他驾着篷车来接她们回庐庄。两位妇人已经打点好回家的行装，累得全凭一股兴奋劲儿撑着才没有垮下来，正在楼下客厅里耐心等他。

===Original===
The travellers who had arrived in the early morning by the old *coche*, had all disappeared by now, some had found accommodation at the Chat Noir, others had gone to their homes or to friends in the city: the hour for dinner was not yet, and the personnel of the inn was busy in the kitchen.

===Chinese===
清晨搭那辆老乡村公共马车（*coche*）来的旅客，到这会儿都已散尽：有的在黑猫客栈（Chat Noir）投了宿，有的回自己家或城里亲友家去了：饭时还没到，客栈的伙计们都在厨房里忙着。

===Original===
The place was deserted and silent; the room itself hot and stuffy. The air was heavy with the mingled odour of dust, stale grease and boiled food. Up on the wall a large white-faced clock ticked with noisy monotony, and against the small windowpane a lazy fly kept up an intermittent buzz. Now and again from a remote part of the house came the sound of a human voice or the barking of a dog, or the rattling of pots and pans.

===Chinese===
这地方空空落落、悄无声息；屋子里又热又闷。空气中沉甸甸地混着尘土、陈油和煮食的气味。墙上，一座白盘大钟滴答滴答，单调地响着闹人；一只懒苍蝇贴着小小的窗玻璃，一阵一阵地嗡嗡。时不时的，从房子深处远远传来人语声、狗吠声，或是锅盆的磕碰声。

===Original===
Louise, sitting in a large, old-fashioned armchair by the side of the great hearth, had closed her eyes. The monotonous ticking of the clock, the buzzing of the fly, the heat and the silence lulled her to sleep. Fleurette, on a straight-backed chair, sat wide awake, unable to keep her eyes closed even for a few minutes, although they ached terribly and she was very, very tired. But there was so much to keep her brain busy. In the past four days more exciting events had been crowded into her life than in all the eighteen years that lay behind her. And round and round they went﻿—these events﻿—beginning with the first sight of the squad of soldiers marching down the high road and coming to a halt on the bridge, until the happy moment when Bibi had assured her that M’sieu’ Amédé was safe and free, under the protection of that mysterious personage whom Bibi called an impudent spy and enemy of France, but whom she, Fleurette, believed to be an agent of the *bon Dieu* Himself.

===Chinese===
路易丝坐在大壁炉旁一把老式大扶手椅里，闭上了眼睛。钟声的单调滴答，苍蝇的嗡嗡，暑热与寂静，把她催入了梦乡。弗勒蕾特坐在一把直背椅上，人却毫无睡意，尽管眼睛疼得厉害，她累极了、累极了，可就连闭上一会儿也做不到。她脑子里要忙的事太多了。过去这四天，涌进她生活的种种惊险变故，比她身后整整十八年加起来还要多。这些事转啊、转啊——从她第一眼望见那队士兵沿着大路开过来、在大桥上停住那一刻起，直到那个幸福的时刻：比比向她担保，阿梅代先生安然无恙、自由自在，正在那位神秘人物的护佑之下——比比骂那人是无礼的间谍、法兰西的敌人，可她弗勒蕾特相信，他是好天主（*bon Dieu*）本人差遣的使者。

===Original===
It seemed a part of her confused thoughts, presently, when she saw the door of the parlour slowly open and the kind soldier who had conducted her to Bibi standing in the doorway. He cast a quick glance all over the room, and as Fleurette was obviously on the point of uttering a cry of surprise, he put up a warning finger to his lips and then beckoned to her to come. She rose, eager as well as mystified, and once more he made a gesture of warning, pointing to Louise and then raising a finger to his lips. A warning it was to make no noise, and not to waken Louise. Fleurette tiptoed across the room to him.

===Chinese===
不多时，客厅的门缓缓开了，领她去见比比的那位和善士兵立在门口——她恍惚觉得，这仿佛也是她纷乱思绪的一部分。他朝屋里飞快扫了一眼；弗勒蕾特眼见就要惊呼出声，他便竖起一根手指抵在唇上，随即招手叫她过去。她站起身，又急切又纳闷；他又做了个警告的手势，指了指路易丝，再把一根手指竖到唇边。那是警告她别出声、别惊醒路易丝。弗勒蕾特踮着脚，穿过屋子走到他跟前。

===Original===
“Your father sent me round,” he said in a whisper.

===Chinese===
「您父亲差我过来的。」他悄声说。

===Original===
He beckoned to her to come outside. She cast a last look at Louise who was obviously peacefully asleep, and then slipped out past him into the street.

===Chinese===
他招手叫她到外头去。她回头望了路易丝最后一眼——她分明睡得正香——便侧身从他身边溜出去，到了街上。

===Original===
“There is something your father forgot to say to you,” the soldier said as soon as he had closed the door behind Fleurette. “But he told me not to bring the old woman along, and so as she was asleep﻿—”

===Chinese===
「您父亲有一句话忘了跟您说。」士兵随手关上身后那扇门，便对弗勒蕾特说。「不过他吩咐我别把老太太带在身边，既然她睡着了——」

===Original===
“But if she wakes and finds me gone﻿—?” Fleurette rejoined, and turned to go back to the inn. “I must just tell her﻿—”

===Chinese===
「可她要是醒了，发现我不在了——？」弗勒蕾特应道，转身要回客栈去。「我总得跟她说一声——」

===Original===
Immediately he seized hold of her hand.

===Chinese===
他立刻一把攥住了她的手。

===Original===
“Your father,” he said, “told me to bring you along as quickly as I could. You know how impatient he is. It is but a step to the Hôtel de Ville. We’ll be there and back before the old woman wakes.”

===Chinese===
「您父亲，」他说，「吩咐我尽快把您带去。您是知道他那急性子的。到市政厅（Hôtel de Ville）不过几步路。老太太醒过来之前，咱们准能打个来回。」

===Original===
No one knew better than Fleurette how impatient Bibi could be. If he said anything, it had to be done at once. At once. So, without further protest, she followed the kind soldier down the narrow street. A few minutes later she was back in the Hôtel de Ville, outside the door which bore the legend: “Committee of Public Safety, Section III.” The same soldier in the shabby uniform was lounging, bayonet in hand, outside the door, but at sight of Lieutenant Godet he stood up at attention and made no attempt this time to bar the way. Godet pushed the door open and at a sign from him Fleurette stepped into the room. Of course she had expected to see Bibi sitting as before behind the table, alone, busy writing.

===Chinese===
比比的急性子，没有谁比弗勒蕾特更清楚。他只要发下话来，那就得立刻办。立刻。于是她不再推拒，跟着那和善的士兵走下窄街。几分钟之后，她又回到了市政厅，站在那扇门外，门上题着一行字：「公安委员会，第三区（Section III）」。还是那个穿破旧军服的士兵，提着刺刀在门外懒懒地倚着，可一看见戈岱中尉，他便挺身立正，这一回没有再拦路。戈岱推开门，朝她一示意，弗勒蕾特便跨进了房间。她自然以为会看见比比还像先前那样，独自坐在桌子后面，忙着写字。

===Original===
Bibi certainly was there, she saw that at a glance, also that at sight of her he jumped to his feet with an expression on his face, far, far more terrible than when she had told him that it was she who had stolen Madame’s valuables. But Bibi was not alone. To right and left of him two men were sitting dressed very much like he was himself and wearing the same kind of tricolour sash round their waist. There was a moment of tense silence while Fleurette, a little scared, but not really frightened, stepped further into the room. She could not take her eyes off Bibi, whose dear face had become the colour of lead. He raised his hand and passed it across his forehead. He seemed as if he wanted to speak, yet could not articulate a sound. After a second or two he looked down first at the man on his right, and then at the one on his left, then back again at her, and over her head at Lieutenant Godet.

===Chinese===
比比确实在那里，这个她一眼就看见了；也看见了他一见她，便霍地跳起身来，脸上的神情，比她招认偷了夫人的值钱物件那会儿可怕得多、可怕得多。可比比并非独自一人。他左右两侧各坐着一个人，穿戴同他自己几乎一个样，腰间也系着同样的三色绶带。一时紧张的沉默。弗勒蕾特有点心怯，却并不真怕，又向屋里走了几步。她的眼睛没法从比比身上挪开：他那张亲爱的脸，已经变成了铅灰色。他抬起一只手，在额头上抹了一把。他像是要说话，却发不出一点声音。过了一两秒，他先低头看看右边的人，又看看左边的人，再回头看看她，然后越过她的头顶，望向戈岱中尉。

===Original===
It was Fleurette who first broke the silence.

===Chinese===
先打破沉默的是弗勒蕾特。

===Original===
“What is it, father?” she said. “You sent for me?”

===Chinese===
「怎么了，父亲？」她说。「您打发人来叫我？」

===Original===
She did not call him Bibi just then; he seemed so very, very unlike Bibi.

===Chinese===
这会儿她没有唤他比比；他看上去那么、那么不像比比。

===Original===
But all he said was:

===Chinese===
可他只说了一句：

===Original===
“What﻿—is the meaning of﻿—of this?” and the words seemed to come through his lips with a terrible effort.

===Chinese===
「这——到底是什么——什么意思？」这几个字仿佛是费了天大的力气，才从他唇间挤出来的。

===Original===
“It means, Citizen Chauvelin, that I am trying to do my duty, and redeeming my faults of negligence and incompetence, for which you passed such severe strictures on me yesterday.”

===Chinese===
「意思是，公民肖维兰，我在尽力恪尽职守，弥补我疏忽与无能的过失——昨天您还为这个狠狠地训斥了我。」

===Original===
The voice was that of Lieutenant Godet. Fleurette could not see him because he stood immediately behind her, but she recognized the voice, even though it was no longer amiable and almost servile as it had been earlier in the day. It had, in fact, the same tone in it which Fleurette had so deeply resented that day upon the bridge when first she had told him that she was Citizen Armand’s daughter.

===Chinese===
说话的是戈岱中尉。弗勒蕾特看不见他，因为他正好站在她身后；可她听出了这个声音，虽说它已不再像白天早些时候那样和气，甚至近乎卑躬。它此刻的声调，事实上正是那天在大桥上，她头一回告诉他自己是公民阿尔芒的女儿时，曾叫弗勒蕾特深深着恼的那一种。

===Original===
“You ordered me,” Godet went on deliberately, “to go into the highways and the byways, and you gave me full power to arrest any man, woman or child whom I suspected of connivance with the enemies of France. This I have done. I have cause to suspect this woman of such connivance, and in accordance with your instructions I have brought her before you on a charge of treason.”

===Chinese===
「您命令我，」戈岱不慌不忙地接着说，「到大路上小路上去查访，并授权我逮捕任何涉嫌私通法兰西敌人的男人、女人或孩童。我照办了。我有理由怀疑这个女人有此等私通之嫌，因此遵照您的指示，我以叛国罪把她带到您面前。」

===Original===
Whereupon the man sitting on the right of Bibi nodded approvingly and said:

===Chinese===
于是坐在比比右手边的那个人赞许地点了点头，说道：

===Original===
“If indeed you have cause to suspect this woman, citizen lieutenant, you did well to arrest her.”

===Chinese===
「您若果真有理由怀疑这个女人，公民中尉，您逮捕她就是办得对。」

===Original===
And the man on Bibi’s left asked: “Who is this woman, citizen lieutenant?”

===Chinese===
坐在比比左手边的人问道：「这个女人是谁，公民中尉？」

===Original===
Then only did Bibi appear to find his voice, and it came through his lips just as if someone held him by the throat and were trying to choke him before he had time to speak.

===Chinese===
直到这时，比比才算找回了声音，而那声音从他唇间出来，就像有人扼住他的喉咙，要赶在他开口之前掐死他似的。

===Original===
“My daughter,” was all he said.

===Chinese===
「我的女儿。」他只说了这么一句。

===Original===
As a matter of fact Fleurette did not understand. That something terrible had occurred, she could see well enough, but for the moment the fact that she was in any way involved had not reached her inner consciousness. She did not realize that when Lieutenant Godet spoke of having arrested a woman, he was referring to her. Thinking that she was probably in the way amongst these serious and busy men, she asked timidly:

===Chinese===
其实弗勒蕾特并没有听懂。出了什么可怕的事，她看得再清楚不过，可她自己也被牵连在内这个事实，一时还没有透进她的心底。她没有意识到，戈岱中尉说什么逮捕了一个女人，指的正是她。她还只道是自己夹在这些正经忙碌的大人物中间碍事，怯生生地问：

===Original===
“Shall I go, father?” whereat the man on the left gave a short, dry laugh.

===Chinese===
「我走吧，父亲？」左边那人听了，短促地干笑了一声。

===Original===
“Not just yet, citizeness,” he said, “we shall have to ask you one or two questions before we let you go.”

===Chinese===
「还不忙，女公民。」他说，「放您走之前，我们还得问您一两个问题。」

===Original===
“Citizen Pochart,” Bibi now rejoined somewhat more steadily, “there is obviously some grave error here on the part of the citizen lieutenant and﻿—”

===Chinese===
「公民波夏尔（Pochart），」比比这时答话，口气稍稍稳了些，「这里显然是公民中尉出了严重差错，而且——」

===Original===
“Grave error,” Pochart broke in with a sneer. “We have heard nothing in the way of witnesses or details of the accusation so far, so why should you think there is an error, Citizen Chauvelin?”

===Chinese===
「严重差错。」波夏尔冷笑着打断他。「到目前为止，证人也好，控告的详情也好，我们一样还没有听着呢，您凭什么认定出了差错，公民肖维兰？」

===Original===
Fleurette could see the struggle on Bibi’s face; she could see the great drops of moisture on his forehead, the swollen veins upon his temples; she saw his hands clenched one against the other, and how he passed his tongue once or twice over his lips.

===Chinese===
弗勒蕾特看得出比比脸上的挣扎；她看得见他额头上大颗大颗的汗珠，太阳穴上暴起的青筋；她看见他两手紧紧地攥在一处，还看见他舌头在唇上舔了一两次。

===Original===
“The citizen lieutenant,” he said with a marvellous assumption of calm, “has shown too much zeal. My daughter is as good a patriot as I am myself﻿—”

===Chinese===
「这位公民中尉，」他说，装出一副了不得的镇定，「热心得过了头。我的女儿同我自己一样，是个好样的爱国者——」

===Original===
“How do you know that, Citizen Chauvelin?” the other man asked, the one on the left of Bibi.

===Chinese===
「这个您怎么知道，公民肖维兰？」另一个人问，就是坐在比比左手边的那个。

===Original===
“Because she has led a modest and a sheltered life, Citizen Danou,” Bibi replied firmly. “Knowing nothing of town life, nothing of intrigues or plots against the State.”

===Chinese===
「因为她过的一向是安分守己、深居简出的日子，公民达努（Danou）。」比比坚定地答道。「城里的世面她一无所知，什么勾心斗角、危害国家的密谋，她一概不知。」

===Original===
“It is impossible,” Pochart put in sententiously, “for any man to know what goes on in a woman’s head. The soundest patriot may have a traitor for a wife﻿—or else a daughter.”

===Chinese===
「谁也别想，」波夏尔一副说教腔地插言，「知道一个女人脑袋里转的是什么念头。最靠得住的爱国者，也可能摊上一个当叛徒的老婆——要不然，一个女儿。」

===Original===
Bibi was obviously making a superhuman effort to control himself. No one knew better than Fleurette how violent could be his temper when he was thwarted, and here were those two men, not to mention Lieutenant Godet, taunting and contradicting him, and she could see the veins swelling upon his temples and his hands clenched until the knuckles shone like polished ivory under the skin.

===Chinese===
比比分明在作超人的努力克制自己。他一旦受挫，脾气能有多暴烈，没有谁比弗勒蕾特更清楚的，而眼前这两个人，还不算戈岱中尉，正在讥刺他、顶撞他；她看得见他太阳穴上暴起的青筋，看得见他的双手攥得指节在皮下亮得像磨光的象牙。

===Original===
“My daughter is not a traitor, Citizen Pochart,” he said loudly and firmly.

===Chinese===
「我的女儿不是叛徒，公民波夏尔。」他响亮而坚定地说。

===Original===
“Lieutenant Godet says she is,” Pochart retorted dryly.

===Chinese===
「戈岱中尉说她就是。」波夏尔干巴巴地回敬。

===Original===
“I challenge him to prove it.”

===Chinese===
「我倒要他拿出证据来。」

===Original===
“You forget, Citizen Chauvelin,” Danou put in suavely, “that it is not for the citizen lieutenant to prove this woman guilty; rather it is for her to prove her innocence.”

===Chinese===
「您忘了，公民肖维兰，」达努温文尔雅地插话，「证明这个女人有罪，这担子并不在公民中尉肩上；倒该由她自己来证明清白。」

===Original===
“The Law of the Suspect,” Pochart added, “has been framed expressly to meet such cases as these.”

===Chinese===
「嫌疑犯法令，」波夏尔补上一句，「正是专为对付这类情形而制定的。」

===Original===
The Law of the Suspect! Ye gods! He himself, Chauvelin, had in the National Assembly voted for its adoption.

===Chinese===
嫌疑犯法令！天上的众神哪！他肖维兰本人，就在国民议会上投过赞成票，通过的就是它。

===Original===
“Are we not ordered instantly to arrest all persons who by their actions, their speaking, their writing or their connections have become suspect?” This from Danou who spoke slowly, unctuously, without a trace of spite or anger in his voice. And Pochart, more rough of tone, but equally conciliatory added:

===Chinese===
「我们奉到的命令，难道不是立即逮捕一切因行为、言论、文字或交往而形迹可疑的人吗？」这话出自达努之口，他说得慢条斯理、油润甜腻，声气里不带一丝怨毒或恼怒。波夏尔呢，口气粗鲁些，却同样客客气气地接着说：

===Original===
“The Law tells us that if suspect of nothing else, a man, or a woman, or even a child, may be ‘suspect of being suspect.’ Is that not so, Citizen Chauvelin? Methinks you yourself had something to do with the framing of that law.”

===Chinese===
「法律告诉我们，一个男人，一个女人，乃至一个孩子，哪怕别的什么都不可疑，也还可能是『可疑之可疑』。是不是这样，公民肖维兰？我想，这条法律的起草，您本人恐怕也出过一份力吧。」

===Original===
“It was aimed at traitors﻿—”

===Chinese===
「它是冲着叛徒去的——」

===Original===
“No! No! at the suspect﻿—”

===Chinese===
「不！不！是冲着嫌疑犯去的——」

===Original===
“My daughter﻿—”

===Chinese===
「我的女儿——」

===Original===
“Ah *ça*, Citizen Chauvelin,” here interposed Pochart with an expressive oath, “are you by any chance on the side of traitors? What has the State to do with the fact that this woman is your daughter? A patriot has no relatives these days. He is a son of the State, a child of France, what? Her enemies are his enemies, his hatred of traitors should override every other sentiment.”

===Chinese===
「我说呀，公民肖维兰，」波夏尔这时插了进来，还带着一声意味十足的咒骂，「您该不会是站在叛徒那一边的吧？这个女人是您的女儿，这与国家有什么相干？如今爱国者是没有亲属的。他是国家的儿子，法兰西的孩子，是不是？她的敌人就是他的敌人，他对叛徒的仇恨，应当压倒其余一切感情。」

===Original===
“A patriot has no sentiment,” Danou echoed suavely.

===Chinese===
「爱国者是不讲感情的。」达努温文尔雅地随声附和。

===Original===
Chauvelin now looked like an animal at bay. Caught in a net turning round and round, wildly, impotently; seeking an egress and only succeeding in getting more and more firmly enmeshed. But he kept himself under control nevertheless. He felt the eyes of those three men probing his soul. Exulting over his misery. Hatred all around him. Cruelty. Godet openly hostile, vengeful, with a grievance for his own humiliation; ready to hit back, to demand humiliation for humiliation, and terror for terror. Revenge! My God! who but a fiend could dream of such revenge? And the other two: that fool Danou and that brute Pochart! No actual hostility about them. Only envy: a mad desire to save their own skins, to purchase notoriety, advancement at any price﻿—even at the price of innocent blood.

===Chinese===
这时肖维兰活像一头被逼入绝境的困兽。落进了网里，疯狂地、徒劳地转着圈；寻着出路，结果只是越缠越紧、越缚越牢。但他到底还是克制住了自己。他感觉得到那三个人的目光在刺探他的灵魂，在对着他的狼狈幸灾乐祸。仇恨从四面八方围着他。残忍。戈岱公然怀着敌意，怀着报复心，记恨着他自己所受的羞辱，随时准备还手，要讨还羞辱对羞辱、恐怖对恐怖。报复！我的天哪！除了恶魔，谁能想出这样的报复？至于另外那两个：达努那个蠢货，波夏尔那个畜生！对他们倒谈不上真有多大的敌意。只有嫉妒：一股保住自家脑袋的疯狂欲求，要捞取名声，不惜任何代价往上爬——哪怕代价是无辜者的血。

===Original===
And as a wild beast twirling and turning in the trap will pause from time to time and glare out into the open, which means all that its life has stood for until now, so did Chauvelin, with soul enmeshed in vengeance and envy, pause a moment in his mad struggle for freedom. He paused and with wildly dilated eyes gazed upon a swift, accusing vision of all the innocent blood he himself had helped to shed. Those clenched hands of his, on which his gaze for one instant rested, fascinated, how many times had they signed the decree which had deprived a father of his son, a wife of her husband, a lover of his mistress. And through the meshes that tightened round him now, Chauvelin gazing into space saw before his eyes the awful word “Retribution” written in letters of fire and blood.

===Chinese===
正如陷入陷阱的野兽在翻腾打转的当口，会不时停下来，瞪眼望着外面那片旷野——那意味着它到此为止的全部生活——肖维兰也是这样，灵魂缠在复仇与嫉妒之网里，在争取自由的疯狂挣扎中停顿了片刻。他停下来，瞪得大大的眼睛，怔怔望见一幅飞掠而过、含着控诉的幻象：那些他自己曾帮着流淌的无辜之血。他那双紧攥的手，他的目光在上面怔怔地逗留了一瞬——多少回，就是这双手，签署了一道道法令，夺走过一位父亲的儿子、一位妻子的丈夫、一位恋人的心上人。而此刻，透过那四面收紧的网眼，肖维兰凝望虚空，看见眼前赫然显出一个可怕的字眼——「报应」，用火与血写成。

===Original===
And seeing the writing on the wall, he felt an immense rage against these men who dared to taunt him, who dared to hit at him, through the one vulnerable spot in his armour of callousness and of cruelty. How dared they stand up to him, these miserable creatures whose existence was of less account than that of a buzzing fly? And throwing back his head he gazed upon them all, one after the other, meeting their sneering glance with a bold challenge. How dared they defy him? Him, Chauvelin? The trusted friend of Robespierre, one of the makers of this glorious revolution; one of its most firm props? Now a representative of the National Convention on special mission? There stood the child, his daughter, his little Fleurette, silent, wide-eyed, obviously not fully aware of the terrible position in which she stood: and they dared to hit at her, to accuse her, without rhyme or reason, just in order to hit at him through her. It was Godet, of course, that vile, incompetent brute: savage and cruel like the fool he was: vengeful for the bad half-hour he had been made to spend in this very room. He must have heard something of what the child had said. At one moment her sweet voice had risen to shrill tones. Oh! what a senseless, mad confession! and he had seized upon it so that he might hit back: have his revenge. But he could prove nothing. It would be one man’s word against another, and he, Chauvelin, representative on special mission, with the ear of all the great men up in Paris, would see to it, that his word carried all the weight. He would deny everything, swear that Godet lied. His was the power, he was more influential, more unscrupulous than most.

===Chinese===
眼见这写在墙上的字，他对这几个人涌起一股狂怒——他们竟敢讥嘲他，竟敢顺着他那副冷酷与残暴的铠甲上唯一的一处软肋下手。他们怎么敢同他分庭抗礼？这些可怜虫，一条命还不如一只嗡嗡叫的苍蝇值钱！他猛地仰起头，把他们挨个儿瞪了一遍，用大胆的挑战迎住他们讥诮的目光。他们怎么敢违逆他？他，肖维兰？罗伯斯庇尔信任的朋友，这场光荣革命的缔造者之一，它最坚固的支柱之一？如今更身居国民公会特派代表之任？那孩子立在那里，他的女儿，他的小弗勒蕾特，默默无言，睁大眼睛，显然还不全然明白自己处境有多可怕：而他们竟敢冲她下手，控告她，无缘无故、没凭没据，只为借她来打击他。这自然是戈岱干的好事，那个卑劣无能的畜生：蠢货一个，又野又狠：为着在这同一间屋里挨过的那半个时辰的晦气，来寻报复了。那孩子说的话，他必定听去了几句。有一阵子，她那甜润的嗓子曾拔高成了尖声。啊！多么糊涂、多么疯狂的招认！他便一把揪住这个话柄，好还手，好泄愤。可他什么也证明不了。到头来不过是一个人的话对另一个人的话，而他肖维兰，特派代表，巴黎上头那些大人物个个都听得进他的话，自有办法叫自己的话一言九鼎。他要全盘否认，发誓说戈岱撒谎。权柄在他手里，他比多数人更有势力，也更无所不为。

===Original===
If only the child held her tongue! She would if she was assured that her Amédé was in no danger. How thankful he, Chauvelin, was that he had told her the truth this morning. He couldn’t bear to look at her just at this moment, she looking so innocent, so unconscious of danger, but nevertheless he tried to convey to her with eyes and lips the warning to hold her tongue.

===Chinese===
但愿这孩子能咬紧牙关不开口！只要让她确信她的阿梅代没有危险，她是会闭紧嘴的。他肖维兰多么庆幸，今早把实情告诉了她。这会儿他简直不忍看她——她那副天真模样，浑然不觉大难临头——可他仍旧竭力用眼睛和嘴唇，向她传去那道警告：千万别开口。

===Original===
Chauvelin had been silent for quite a little while; the others thought that they had cowed him. In their hearts Pochart and Danou were not a little afraid of him. A representative on special mission had unlimited power and this Chauvelin was always a crouching beast, ready to snarl and to spring, and they knew well enough how influential he was. But here was a double chance to show their zeal, and to get even with the man whom they had always feared. As for Godet, he had obviously staked everything on this throw. His life was anyhow forfeit; Chauvelin’s threats yesterday had left him no loophole for hope. But here was revenge to his hand, and at worst a powerful lever wherewith to force his enemy’s hand.

===Chinese===
肖维兰已经沉默了好一阵子；旁人都当他们把他镇住了。波夏尔和达努心里，对他着实不是不害怕。特派代表握有无上的权力，而这一个肖维兰，又是一头永远蹲伏着的野兽，随时会龇牙、会扑上来；他的势力有多大，他们也再清楚不过。可眼下是个双料的好机会，既可显示他们的热忱，又可同这个他们一直惧怕的人算算旧账。至于戈岱，他分明把身家性命全押上了这一掷。横竖他这条命已是在劫难逃：肖维兰昨天的威胁，没给他留下一线指望的缝隙。可如今复仇就在手边，退一步说，也是一根强有力的杠杆，足以逼他的仇敌就范。

===Original===
Chauvelin’s mind had been so busily at work that for a while he lost consciousness of these men. After his rage against them he forgot their very presence. Nothing mattered﻿—no one﻿—except the child, and his own power to save her. Through that semiconsciousness he only heard vague words. Snatches of phrases that passed rapidly between those two men and Godet. “Proofs﻿—” “Witnesses﻿—” And then Danou’s voice soft and unctuous as usual:

===Chinese===
肖维兰的脑子忙作一团，有一阵子竟忘却了这几个人的存在。对他们的狂怒过去之后，他连他们在场这事都忘了。什么都无关紧要——什么人都无关紧要——除了那孩子，除了他自己救她的力量。在那半明半昧的意识里，他只听见些模模糊糊的话音，那两个人和戈岱之间飞快来去的只言片语。「证据——」「证人——」接着是达努的声音，照例又软又腻：

===Original===
“Of course the more solid your proofs﻿—”

===Chinese===
「当然啦，您的证据越扎实——」

===Original===
And Pochart’s rough and determined:

===Chinese===
还有波夏尔那粗声粗气、斩钉截铁的：

===Original===
“Why should we not hear that witness now?”

===Chinese===
「咱们何不现在就听听那个证人？」

===Original===
Godet replied lightly: “I have her here. Perhaps it would be best.”

===Chinese===
戈岱轻快地答道：「我把她带来了。也许这样最好。」

===Original===
It seemed as if they were determined to ignore him, Chauvelin; to shut him out of their counsels. He was so silent, so self-absorbed; they thought that he was cowed, and dared not raise his voice in defence of his daughter. They were all alike these men﻿—these masters of France as they liked to be called﻿—overbearing, arrogant, always menacing, until you hit back, when all the starch would go out of them, and they would cringe, or else become surly and defiant like any aristo.

===Chinese===
他们像是打定了主意不理会他，不理会肖维兰；要把他排斥在他们的计议之外。他那么沉默，那么出神；他们只当他被镇住了，不敢扬起嗓子为女儿辩护。这些人全是一路货——这些喜欢自称为法兰西主人的人——盛气凌人，倨傲不逊，动辄威吓，可只要你一还手，他们满身的浆硬气便登时塌了架，要么卑躬屈膝，要么就悻悻抗拒，同任何一个贵族佬没两样。

===Original===
“Go and fetch your witness, citizen lieutenant,” Pochart said in the end.

===Chinese===
「去把您的证人带来，公民中尉。」波夏尔终于说道。

===Original===
Then Chauvelin woke, like a tiger out of his sleep.

===Chinese===
这时肖维兰才惊醒过来，像一头从酣睡中惊起的老虎。

===Original===
“What?” he queried abruptly, “what is this?”

===Chinese===
「什么？」他猝然问道，「这是怎么回事？」

===Original===
“A witness, citizen representative,” came in unctuous tones from Danou. “It will be more satisfactory in this case﻿—the Law does not demand witnesses﻿—suspicion is enough﻿—but﻿—”

===Chinese===
「一个证人，公民代表。」达努用油滑甜腻的声调应道。「这样办更叫人满意——法律并不要求证人——嫌疑就够了——不过——」

===Original===
“Out of deference to your position, citizen,” Pochart broke in with a short laugh. “Go and fetch your witness, Citizen Godet,” he added dryly.

===Chinese===
「不过是顾念您的地位，公民。」波夏尔短笑一声，打断了他。「去把您的证人带来，公民戈岱。」他又干巴巴地补了一句。

===Original===
Chauvelin brought his clenched fist down with a crash on the table.

===Chinese===
肖维兰攥紧拳头，砰的一声砸在桌上。

===Original===
“I’ll not allow you﻿—” he began in thundering accents, and met Danou’s sneering, inquiring gaze.

===Chinese===
「我不许你们——」他声如雷震地开了腔，却正迎上达努那讥诮而探问的目光。

===Original===
“Allow what, citizen representative?” Pochart asked roughly.

===Chinese===
「不许什么，公民代表？」波夏尔粗声问。

===Original===
“Refuse to hear witnesses? On what grounds?” Danou put in in smooth, velvety accents.

===Chinese===
「不许听取证人吗？凭什么？」达努用圆滑柔腻的声调插了进来。

===Original===
Godet said nothing. It was not for him to speak; but he met Chauvelin’s glance just then, and almost drained his cup of revenge to its dreg.

===Chinese===
戈岱一言不发。这还轮不到他说话；可就在这时，他迎住了肖维兰的目光，把自己那杯复仇之酒，几乎饮到了最后一滴残滓。

===Original===
“No one,” now put in Pochart significantly, “has more respect for family ties than I have. But I am first of all a patriot, and then only a family man. I happen to be a single man, but if I were married and discovered my wife to be a traitor to the State, and an enemy of the people, I would with my own hand adjust the guillotine which would end such a worthless and miserable life.”

===Chinese===
「没有人，」波夏尔这时意味深长地插言，「比我更尊重骨肉亲情。可我首先是个爱国者，其次才是一家之人。我恰好是个单身汉，可我若娶了妻，又发觉她是国家的叛徒、人民的公敌，我会亲手调校断头台，了结那样一条卑贱可悲的性命。」

===Original===
“Now you, Citizen Chauvelin,” Danou said, taking up his colleague’s point, “are doing your daughter no good by trying to shield her from punishment if she be guilty.”

===Chinese===
「至于您呢，公民肖维兰，」达努接过同僚的话头，说道，「她若果真有罪，您想护她不受惩罚，那是帮不了您的女儿的。」

===Original===
“You would not dare﻿—”

===Chinese===
「你们不敢——」

===Original===
“Dare what, Citizen Chauvelin? Act up to the principles which you yourself have helped to promulgate in France? Indeed we dare! We dare strike at the enemies of the State whoever they may be. That woman,” he added, indicating Fleurette, “is suspect; the Law of the Suspect gives our Committee power to arrest her. If she be proved innocent she shall go free. If she be guilty, you, by defending her, cannot save her and do but condemn yourself.”

===Chinese===
「不敢什么，公民肖维兰？不敢奉行您本人帮着在法兰西颁行的那套原则吗？我们真就敢！凡国家的敌人，不管是谁，我们都敢打击。那个女人，」他指了指弗勒蕾特，又道，「形迹可疑；嫌疑犯法令授权我们的委员会逮捕她。她若证明清白，自会放她自由。她若有罪，您袒护她也救不了她，只会连您自己一并定罪。」

===Original===
And that was true! No one knew it better than Chauvelin, who but a few weeks ago in Paris had helped Merlin and Douai to frame that abominable Law. The heavy hand of Retribution was indeed upon him. The voice of the innocent had cried out for Vengeance before the Lord, and Nemesis, hourglass in hand, had stalked him now at last. All that was left him at this moment, out of all that arrogance which had imposed his personality upon the masters of France, made them forget his failures and fear him even in the hour of humiliation, was just a shred of pride, which enabled him to hide his misery and his despair behind a mask of impassiveness. He even succeeded in hiding his hatred and contempt of these three curs who were yapping at his heels. And when Pochart for the third time reiterated his order to Godet to go and fetch his witness, Chauvelin made no further protest. He rose from the table and went round to where Fleurette was standing, silent, bewildered, with great tears, like those of a frightened child, running down her cheeks.

===Chinese===
而这话是真的！没有谁比肖维兰更明白：就在几个星期前的巴黎，还是他帮着梅兰（Merlin）和杜埃（Douai）起草了那部可憎的法律。报应的重手确确实实压到了他身上。无辜者的声音，早已在天主面前呼求过复仇；而今，复仇女神（Nemesis）手持沙漏，终于寻踪而至，把他盯上了。当年那股傲气——正是靠它，他把自己的威权强加给法兰西的主人们，叫他们忘掉他的种种败绩，甚至在他蒙羞的时刻也惧他三分——此刻剩下的，只有一星残存的骄傲，靠着它，他才能把凄苦与绝望藏到一副漠然的面具后头。他甚至藏住了对那三条跟在他脚后狂吠的癞皮狗的仇恨与轻蔑。于是，当波夏尔第三次重申命令、叫戈岱去带证人时，肖维兰没有再提出异议。他从桌边起身，绕到弗勒蕾特站着的地方——她一言不发，满脸惶惑，大颗大颗的泪珠，像受惊的孩子似的，顺着双颊往下淌。

===Original===
He held his hands tightly clenched behind his back, to prevent himself from seizing her in his arms and raining kisses upon her golden hair, letting those sneering men see how terribly he had been hit and how he suffered. Godet had gone out of the room to fetch the witness﻿—what witness? and the other two were sitting at the table, whispering together. Chauvelin through compressed lips murmured in Fleurette’s ears:

===Chinese===
他把双手紧紧攥在背后，为的是拦住自己，不把她一把搂进怀里，不把雨点般的吻落在她的金发上——那就等于让那些讥诮的家伙看个明白，他挨的这一击有多重，他有多么痛苦。戈岱已经出屋去带证人了——什么证人？另外那两个还坐在桌边，交头接耳。肖维兰抿紧了嘴唇，凑在弗勒蕾特耳边低语：

===Original===
“Try not to be frightened, little one! Don’t let them see you are frightened. They dare not do anything to you.”

===Chinese===
「尽量别害怕，小家伙！别让他们看出你害怕。他们不敢把你怎么样。」

===Original===
“I am not frightened, chéri Bibi,” she replied, smiling at him through her tears.

===Chinese===
「我不害怕，亲爱的比比。」她答道，噙着泪对他微笑。

===Original===
“And you will hold your tongue, Fleurette,” he urged under his breath, “about what you told me this morning? Swear to me that you will.”

===Chinese===
「还有，今早你跟我说的那些事，你可要守口如瓶，弗勒蕾特，」他压着嗓子催促道，「向我起誓，你一定不说。」

===Original===
“If M’sieu’ Amédé is safe﻿—”

===Chinese===
「只要阿梅代先生平安——」

===Original===
“I swear to you on my soul, that we do not even know where he is.”

===Chinese===
「我拿灵魂向你起誓，我们连他在哪儿都不知道。」

===Original===
“In that case, Bibi chéri﻿—”

===Chinese===
「那样的话，亲爱的比比——」

===Original===
Quick footsteps outside the door. A challenge from the man on guard. The opening of the door. Then Godet’s voice saying loudly:

===Chinese===
门外响起急促的脚步声。守卫喝问了一声口令。门开了。接着是戈岱的高声通报：

===Original===
“The witness, citizens.”

===Chinese===
「证人到了，公民们。」

===Original===
Chauvelin looked up and saw beside Godet a woman with a shawl wrapped round her head; she came forward boldly, then threw back her shawl. Chauvelin uttered a savage oath, whilst Fleurette gasped in amazement:

===Chinese===
肖维兰抬眼望去，看见戈岱身边站着一个头上裹着披巾的女人；她大大方方走上前来，随即把披巾往后一甩。肖维兰发出一声狂怒的咒骂，弗勒蕾特却惊得倒抽了一口气：

===Original===
“Adèle!” she cried.

===Chinese===
「阿黛尔！」她喊道。

## XXI / 第二十一章

===Original===
